|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| API Routes
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------

| All API routes are versioned under v1.
|
*/

    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/auth.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/members.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/deposits.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/investments.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/profits.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/notices.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/projects.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/sales.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/expenses.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/transactions.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/reports.php';
    require __DIR__ . '/api/v1/settings.php';
});

--- console.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schedule;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Inspiring;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;

Artisan::command('inspire', function () {
    $this->comment(Inspiring::quote());
})->purpose('Display an inspiring quote');



/*
| Add this to routes/console.php if your application does not already
| schedule the reminder command elsewhere.
*/

Schedule::command('reminders:send-due')
    ->dailyAt('09:00')
    ->timezone('Asia/Dhaka')
    ->withoutOverlapping();

<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\LoginController;

|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| PUBLIC ROUTES
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

    return view('welcome');
})->name('home');


/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| AUTH ROUTES
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

Route::middleware('guest')->group(function () {

    Route::get(
        '/login',
        [LoginController::class, 'showLoginForm']
    )->name('login');

    Route::post(
        '/login',
        [LoginController::class, 'login']
    )->name('login.submit');

});

Route::post(
    '/logout',
    [LoginController::class, 'logout']
)->name('logout');

|--------------------------------------------------------------------------

--- accountsecurity.php ---
<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AccountSecurityController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Admin Account Security Routes
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| All routes in this file are protected by:
|   - admin authentication
|   - /admin URL prefix
|   - admin. route-name prefix
|
| Final route examples:
|   GET  /admin/account-security
|   GET  /admin/account-security/{id}
|   POST /admin/account-security/{id}/unlock
|
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

Route::middleware(['auth:admin'])
    ->prefix('admin')
    ->name('admin.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Account Security
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::prefix('account-security')
            ->name('account-security.')
            ->controller(AccountSecurityController::class)
            ->group(function () {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Security Accounts List
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */
                Route::get('/', 'index')
                    ->name('index');

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Security Account Details
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Example:
                | /admin/account-security/2
                |
                */
                Route::get('/{accountSecurity}', 'show')
                    ->whereNumber('accountSecurity')
                    ->name('show');

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Unlock Account
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */
                Route::post('/{accountSecurity}/unlock', 'unlock')
                    ->whereNumber('accountSecurity')
                    ->name('unlock');

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Reset Failed Login Attempts
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */
                Route::post('/{accountSecurity}/reset-attempts', 'resetAttempts')
                    ->whereNumber('accountSecurity')
                    ->name('reset-attempts');

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Force Password Change
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */
                Route::post('/{accountSecurity}/force-password-change', 'forcePasswordChange')
                    ->whereNumber('accountSecurity')
                    ->name('force-password-change');

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Clear Forced Password Change
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */
                Route::post('/{accountSecurity}/clear-password-change', 'clearPasswordChange')
                    ->whereNumber('accountSecurity')
                    ->name('clear-password-change');
            });
    });

--- admin.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AdminController;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| SYSTEM ADMIN — ADMIN ACCOUNT MANAGEMENT
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Super Admin is the System Admin.
| Only the System Admin can manage admin accounts.
*/

Route::prefix('admin')
    ->name('admin.')
    ->middleware(['admin.session', 'super.admin'])
    ->group(function () {

        Route::resource('admins', AdminController::class)
            ->except(['show']);

        Route::post(
            'admins/reset-password',
            [AdminController::class, 'resetPassword']
        )->name('admins.resetPassword');

        Route::post(
            'admins/toggle-status',
            [AdminController::class, 'toggleStatus']
        )->name('admins.toggleStatus');
    });

--- ams.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

use App\Http\Controllers\Member\AmsController as MemberAmsController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AmsManagementController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\AmsWorkflowSettingsController;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| AMS - Member Portal
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Member request submission, request history, finance verification,
| trustee approval, return, resubmission and attachment download.
|
*/

Route::prefix('member')
    ->middleware([
        'auth:member',
        'member.setup',
    ])
    ->name('member.')
    ->group(function () {

        Route::prefix('ams')
            ->name('ams.')
            ->group(function () {

                // Member AMS dashboard
                Route::get(
                    '/',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'index']
                )->name('index');

                // Create request form
                Route::get(
                    '/requests/create',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'create']
                )->name('create');

                // Submit new request
                Route::post(
                    '/requests',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'store']
                )->name('store');

                // Request details
                Route::get(
                    '/requests/{amsRequest}',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'show']
                )->name('show');

                // Request attachment download
                Route::get(
                    '/requests/{amsRequest}/attachment',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'attachment']
                )->name('attachment');

                // Finance verification
                Route::post(
                    '/requests/{amsRequest}/finance-verify',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'financeVerify']
                )->name('finance-verify');

                // Trustee approval
                Route::post(
                    '/requests/{amsRequest}/approve',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'approve']
                )->name('approve');

                // Return request for correction
                Route::post(
                    '/requests/{amsRequest}/return',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'returnRequest']
                )->name('return');

                // Resubmit corrected request
                Route::put(
                    '/requests/{amsRequest}/resubmit',
                    [MemberAmsController::class, 'resubmit']
                )->name('resubmit');
            });
    });


/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| AMS - System Admin Portal
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| System Admin can manage AMS requests and configure workflow settings.
| Request approval actions must still enforce their own authorization.
|
*/

Route::prefix('admin')
    ->middleware([
        'auth:admin',
        'admin',
        'super.admin',
    ])
    ->name('admin.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | AMS Management
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        // All AMS requests
        Route::get(
            '/ams',
            [AmsManagementController::class, 'index']
        )->name('ams.index');

        // Admin request creation form
        Route::get(
            '/ams/requests/create',
            [AmsManagementController::class, 'create']
        )->name('ams.create');

        // Request details and audit history
        Route::get(
            '/ams/requests/{amsRequest}',
            [AmsManagementController::class, 'show']
        )->name('ams.show');


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | AMS Workflow Settings
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        // Configure Trustee Board and Finance Approvers
        Route::get(
            '/ams/workflow-settings',
            [AmsWorkflowSettingsController::class, 'index']
        )->name('ams.workflow-settings');

        // Save workflow configuration
        Route::post(
            '/ams/workflow-settings',
            [AmsWorkflowSettingsController::class, 'update']
        )->name('ams.workflow-settings.update');




        
Route::post(
    '/ams/requests',
    [AmsManagementController::class, 'store']
)->name('ams.store');

Route::get(
    '/ams/requests/{amsRequest}/attachment',
    [AmsManagementController::class, 'attachment']
)->name('ams.attachment');

    });

<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\ProfitDistributionController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Admin\ProfitWorkflowController;

Route::prefix('admin')
    ->middleware(['auth:admin', 'admin'])
    ->name('admin.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Profit Distribution
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

            '/profit-distribution',
            [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'index']
        )->name('profit-distribution.index');

        Route::post(
            '/profit/distribute',
            [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'distribute']
        )->name('profit.distribute');

        Route::get(
            '/profit/check-duplicate',
            [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'checkDuplicate']
        )->name('profit.check-duplicate');


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Distribution Management
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::prefix('distributions')
            ->name('distributions.')
            ->group(function () {

                Route::post(
                    '/store',
                    [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'store']
                )->name('store');

                Route::put(
                    '/update/{id}',
                    [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'update']
                )->name('update');

                Route::put(
                    '/update-status/{id}',
                    [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'updatePaymentStatus']
                )->name('update-status');

                Route::delete(
                    '/destroy/{id}',
                    [ProfitDistributionController::class, 'destroy']
                )->name('destroy');
            });


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Profit Workflow
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::prefix('profit-workflow')
            ->name('profit-workflow.')
            ->group(function () {

                // Approve distribution and post its accounting voucher
                Route::post(
                    '/distributions/{id}/approve',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'approveDistribution']
                )->name('distributions.approve');


                // Individual member profit payment
                Route::post(
                    '/profits/{id}/pay',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'payMemberProfit']
                )->name('profits.pay');


                // Approve withdrawal
                Route::post(
                    '/withdrawals/{id}/approve',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'approveWithdrawal']
                )->name('withdrawals.approve');


                // Record withdrawal payment
                Route::post(
                    '/withdrawals/{id}/pay',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'payWithdrawal']
                )->name('withdrawals.pay');


                // Reject withdrawal
                Route::post(
                    '/withdrawals/{id}/reject',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'rejectWithdrawal']
                )->name('withdrawals.reject');


                // Approve project profit
                Route::post(
                    '/projects/{id}/approve',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'approveProjectProfit']
                )->name('projects.approve');


                // Reject project profit
                Route::post(
                    '/projects/{id}/reject',
                    [ProfitWorkflowController::class, 'rejectProjectProfit']
                )->name('projects.reject');
            });
    });

                    ->name('template');

                    ->name('upload');

                    ->name('progress');

                Route::post('/{batch}/process', 'process')
                    ->name('process');

                    ->name('show');
            });

        | IMPORTANT

        */
    });

--- v1.php ---

<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\AuthController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\DepositController as ApiDepositController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\InvestmentController as ApiInvestmentController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\MemberController as ApiMemberController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\ProfitController as ApiProfitController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\NoticeController as ApiNoticeController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\SettingController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

Route::prefix('v1')->group(function () {

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Public API Authentication
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    // Keep the existing login endpoint for Admin login.
    Route::post('login', [AuthController::class, 'adminLogin']);

    // Separate Member login endpoint.
    Route::post('member-login', [AuthController::class, 'memberLogin']);

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Authenticated API Routes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {

        Route::post('logout', [AuthController::class, 'logout']);

        // Existing resource routes retained.
        Route::apiResource('members', ApiMemberController::class);
        Route::apiResource('deposits', ApiDepositController::class);
        Route::apiResource('investments', ApiInvestmentController::class);
        Route::apiResource('profits', ApiProfitController::class);
        Route::apiResource('notices', ApiNoticeController::class);

        // System Settings API.
        Route::get('settings', [SettingController::class, 'index'])
            ->name('api.settings.index');

        Route::put('settings', [SettingController::class, 'update'])
            ->name('api.settings.update');
    });
});

--- dashboard.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\Member\MemberDashboardController;

Route::prefix('member')
    ->middleware(['portal.access'])
    ->name('member.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
         * ============================================================
         * NORMAL MEMBER DASHBOARD
         * ============================================================
         *
         * Normal Member login করলে:
         *
         * /member/dashboard
         *
         * এখানে Member guard থেকে logged-in member-এর
         * নিজের dashboard দেখাবে।
         */
        Route::get('/dashboard', [MemberDashboardController::class, 'index'])
            ->middleware('member.setup')
            ->name('dashboard');


        /*
         * ============================================================
         * SUPER ADMIN → SELECTED MEMBER DASHBOARD
         * ============================================================
         *
         * Super Admin নির্দিষ্ট Member select করলে:
         *
         * /member/dashboard/{member}
         *
         * এখানে selected Member-এর dashboard দেখাবে।
         */
        Route::get('/dashboard/{member}', [MemberDashboardController::class, 'index'])
            ->middleware('member.setup')
            ->name('dashboard.selected');

    });

--- deposits.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\Member\MemberDepositController;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| MEMBER ROUTES
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Member Deposit Flow:
|
| Confirm Deposit
|      ↓
| Deposit Store
|      ↓
| Pending Deposit
|      ↓
| Deposit Details
|      ↓
| Submit Slip Popup
|
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

Route::prefix('member')
    ->middleware([
        'auth:member',
        'member.setup',
    ])
    ->name('member.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | DEPOSITS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::prefix('deposits')
            ->name('deposits.')
            ->group(function () {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | DEPOSIT HISTORY
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/',
                    [MemberDepositController::class, 'index']
                )->name('index');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | CREATE DEPOSIT
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/create',
                    [MemberDepositController::class, 'create']
                )->name('create');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | STORE / CONFIRM DEPOSIT
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Member clicks "Confirm Deposit"
                | Controller stores the deposit as PENDING.
                | Then redirects to Deposit Details with:
                |
                | show_submit_slip_popup = true
                |
                */

                Route::post(
                    '/',
                    [MemberDepositController::class, 'store']
                )->name('store');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | GET MEMBER DUE
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/get-due',
                    [MemberDepositController::class, 'getDueInfo']
                )->name('get-due');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | RECEIPT / MR
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Keep this BEFORE /{id}
                | so "receipt" is not treated as an ID.
                |
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/{id}/receipt',
                    [MemberDepositController::class, 'receipt']
                )
                    ->whereNumber('id')
                    ->name('receipt');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | DEPOSIT DETAILS
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | After successful Confirm Deposit:
                |
                | /member/deposits/{id}
                |
                | The Deposit Details Blade automatically opens
                | the Submit Slip popup when session flag exists.
                |
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/{id}',
                    [MemberDepositController::class, 'show']
                )
                    ->whereNumber('id')
                    ->name('show');

            });

    });

--- notifications.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

use App\Http\Controllers\Member\MemberNotificationController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Member\MemberNoticeController;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| MEMBER NOTIFICATIONS & NOTICES
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

Route::prefix('member')
    ->middleware([
        'auth:member',
        'member.setup',
    ])
    ->name('member.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | MEMBER NOTIFICATIONS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::prefix('notifications')
            ->name('notifications.')
            ->group(function () {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Notification List
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/',
                    [MemberNotificationController::class, 'index']
                )->name('index');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Mark All Read
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Canonical method:
                | markAllRead()
                |
                */

                Route::post(
                    '/read-all',
                    [MemberNotificationController::class, 'markAllRead']
                )->name('read-all');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Mark Single Read
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Canonical method:
                | markRead()
                |
                */

                Route::post(
                    '/{notification}/read',
                    [MemberNotificationController::class, 'markRead']
                )->name('read');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | AJAX Mark Single Read
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Kept separately for frontend compatibility.
                |
                */

                Route::post(
                    '/{notification}/mark-read',
                    [MemberNotificationController::class, 'markRead']
                )->name('mark-read');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | AJAX Mark All Read
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | Same canonical controller method.
                |
                */

                Route::post(
                    '/mark-all-read',
                    [MemberNotificationController::class, 'markAllRead']
                )->name('mark-all-read');

            });


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | MEMBER NOTICES
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::prefix('notices')
            ->name('notices.')
            ->group(function () {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Notice List
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/',
                    [MemberNoticeController::class, 'index']
                )->name('index');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Notice Details
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get(
                    '/{id}',
                    [MemberNoticeController::class, 'show']
                )->name('show');

            });

    });

--- profile.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
use App\Http\Controllers\Member\MemberProfileController;
use App\Http\Controllers\Auth\LoginController;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| MEMBER ROUTES
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Authentication:
|   auth:member
|
| First Login:
|   MUST remain outside member.setup
|
| Normal Profile:
|   Protected by member.setup
|
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

Route::prefix('member')
    ->middleware(['auth:member'])
    ->name('member.')
    ->group(function () {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | FIRST LOGIN
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | These routes intentionally remain outside member.setup.
        |
        */

        Route::prefix('profile')
            ->name('profile.')
            ->group(function () {

                Route::get('/first-login', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'firstLogin'
                ])->name('first-login');

                Route::post('/first-login', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'completeFirstLogin'
                ])->name('first-login.complete');
            });


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | OPTIONAL LEGACY FIRST LOGIN ROUTE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::match(['get', 'post'], '/first-login', [
            MemberProfileController::class,
            'firstLogin'
        ])->name('first-login');


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | NORMAL MEMBER PROFILE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | member.setup prevents members who have not completed
        | first-login setup from accessing normal profile pages.
        |
        */

        Route::middleware(['member.setup'])
            ->prefix('profile')
            ->name('profile.')
            ->group(function () {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | PROFILE HOME
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get('/', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'index'
                ])->name('index');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | BIO
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get('/bio', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'bio'
                ])->name('bio');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | MEMBERSHIP CARD
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get('/membership-card', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'membershipCard'
                ])->name('membership-card');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | EDIT PROFILE
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get('/edit', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'edit'
                ])->name('edit');

                Route::put('/', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'update'
                ])->name('update');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | CHANGE PASSWORD
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get('/password', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'password'
                ])->name('password');

                Route::put('/password', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'updatePassword'
                ])->name('password.update');


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | NOMINEES
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                Route::get('/nominees', [
                    MemberProfileController::class,
                    'nominees'
                ])->name('nominees');
            });


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | LOGOUT
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Route::post('/logout', [
            LoginController::class,
            'logout'
        ])->name('logout');
    });

<?php

            });


            
Route::prefix('profit-withdrawals')
    ->name('profits.withdrawals.')
    ->group(function () {
        Route::get(
            '/',
            [\App\Http\Controllers\Member\ProfitWithdrawalController::class, 'index']
        )->name('index');

        Route::post(
            '/',

--- 2026_09_19_000010_create_import_batches_table.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 1. TABLE MUST EXIST
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        if (!Schema::hasTable('import_batches')) {
            Schema::create('import_batches', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->id();

                $table->uuid('record_uuid')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->unique();

                $table->string('batch_no', 100)->unique();

                $table->string('module', 100)->default('members');

                $table->string('import_type', 50)->default('excel');

                $table->string('file_name', 255)->nullable();

                $table->string('file_path', 500)->nullable();

                $table->unsignedInteger('total_rows')->default(0);

                $table->unsignedInteger('processed_rows')->default(0);

                $table->unsignedInteger('successful_rows')->default(0);

                $table->unsignedInteger('failed_rows')->default(0);

                $table->unsignedInteger('skipped_rows')->default(0);

                $table->string('status', 30)->default('pending');

                $table->longText('error_data')->nullable();

                $table->text('remarks')->nullable();

                $table->timestamp('started_at')->nullable();

                $table->timestamp('completed_at')->nullable();

                $table->unsignedBigInteger('created_by')->nullable();

                $table->timestamps();

                $table->index(
                    ['module', 'status'],
                    'import_batches_module_status_index'
                );

                $table->index(
                    ['created_by'],
                    'import_batches_created_by_index'
                );

                $table->index(
                    ['created_at'],
                    'import_batches_created_at_index'
                );
            });

            return;
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 2. ADD MODERN COLUMNS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Schema::table('import_batches', function (Blueprint $table) {

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'record_uuid')) {
                $table->uuid('record_uuid')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('id');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'batch_no')) {
                $table->string('batch_no', 100)
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('record_uuid');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'module')) {
                $table->string('module', 100)
                    ->default('members')
                    ->after('batch_no');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'import_type')) {
                $table->string('import_type', 50)
                    ->default('excel')
                    ->after('module');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'file_name')) {
                $table->string('file_name', 255)
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('import_type');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'file_path')) {
                $table->string('file_path', 500)
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('file_name');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'total_rows')) {
                $table->unsignedInteger('total_rows')
                    ->default(0)
                    ->after('file_path');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'processed_rows')) {
                $table->unsignedInteger('processed_rows')
                    ->default(0)
                    ->after('total_rows');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'successful_rows')) {
                $table->unsignedInteger('successful_rows')
                    ->default(0)
                    ->after('processed_rows');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'failed_rows')) {
                $table->unsignedInteger('failed_rows')
                    ->default(0)
                    ->after('successful_rows');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'skipped_rows')) {
                $table->unsignedInteger('skipped_rows')
                    ->default(0)
                    ->after('failed_rows');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'status')) {
                $table->string('status', 30)
                    ->default('pending')
                    ->after('skipped_rows');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'error_data')) {
                $table->longText('error_data')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('status');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'remarks')) {
                $table->text('remarks')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('error_data');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'started_at')) {
                $table->timestamp('started_at')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('remarks');
            }

            if (!Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'completed_at')) {
                $table->timestamp('completed_at')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('started_at');
            }
        });

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 3. COPY OLD DATA -> NEW DATA
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        if (
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'batch_id') &&
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'batch_no')
        ) {
            DB::statement("
                UPDATE import_batches
                SET batch_no = batch_id
                WHERE
                    (batch_no IS NULL OR batch_no = '')
                    AND batch_id IS NOT NULL
            ");
        }

        if (
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'filename') &&
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'file_name')
        ) {
            DB::statement("
                UPDATE import_batches
                SET file_name = filename
                WHERE
                    (file_name IS NULL OR file_name = '')
                    AND filename IS NOT NULL
            ");
        }

        if (
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'success_rows') &&
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'successful_rows')
        ) {
            DB::statement("
                UPDATE import_batches
                SET successful_rows = success_rows
            ");
        }

        if (
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'errors') &&
            Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', 'error_data')
        ) {
            DB::statement("
                UPDATE import_batches
                SET error_data = errors
                WHERE
                    error_data IS NULL
                    AND errors IS NOT NULL
            ");
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 4. GENERATE MISSING BATCH NUMBERS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        DB::table('import_batches')
            ->whereNull('batch_no')
            ->orWhere('batch_no', '')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->get(['id'])
            ->each(function ($row) {
                DB::table('import_batches')
                    ->where('id', $row->id)
                    ->update([
                        'batch_no' =>
                            'IMP-MIGRATED-' .
                            str_pad(
                                (string) $row->id,
                                6,
                                '0',
                                STR_PAD_LEFT
                            ),
                    ]);
            });

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 5. GENERATE RECORD UUID
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        DB::table('import_batches')
            ->whereNull('record_uuid')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->get(['id'])
            ->each(function ($row) {
                DB::table('import_batches')
                    ->where('id', $row->id)
                    ->update([
                        'record_uuid' => (string) Str::uuid(),
                    ]);
            });

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 6. DEFAULT VALUES
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        DB::statement("
            UPDATE import_batches
            SET module = 'members'
            WHERE module IS NULL OR module = ''
        ");

        DB::statement("
            UPDATE import_batches
            SET import_type = 'excel'
            WHERE import_type IS NULL OR import_type = ''
        ");

        DB::statement("
            UPDATE import_batches
            SET skipped_rows = 0
            WHERE skipped_rows IS NULL
        ");

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 7. ADD INDEXES / UNIQUE CONSTRAINTS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Schema::table('import_batches', function (Blueprint $table) {

            $table->unique(
                'record_uuid',
                'import_batches_record_uuid_unique'
            );

            $table->unique(
                'batch_no',
                'import_batches_batch_no_unique'
            );

            $table->index(
                ['module', 'status'],
                'import_batches_module_status_index'
            );

            $table->index(
                ['created_at'],
                'import_batches_created_at_index'
            );
        });

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 8. REMOVE OLD COLUMNS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $oldColumns = [];

        foreach ([
            'batch_id',
            'filename',
            'success_rows',
            'progress',
            'errors',
        ] as $column) {

            if (Schema::hasColumn('import_batches', $column)) {
                $oldColumns[] = $column;
            }
        }

        if (!empty($oldColumns)) {
            Schema::table('import_batches', function (Blueprint $table) use ($oldColumns) {
                $table->dropColumn($oldColumns);
            });
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | 9. ENSURE CREATED_BY INDEX
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        try {
            Schema::table('import_batches', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->index(
                    'created_by',
                    'import_batches_created_by_index'
                );
            });
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            // Index may already exist. Safe to ignore.
        }
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | IMPORTANT
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | This rollback intentionally does not attempt to reconstruct
        | the old schema because the migration may have transformed
        | existing production/import data.
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Schema::table('import_batches', function (Blueprint $table) {

            foreach ([
                'import_batches_record_uuid_unique',
                'import_batches_batch_no_unique',
                'import_batches_module_status_index',
                'import_batches_created_at_index',
                'import_batches_created_by_index',
            ] as $index) {
                try {
                    $table->dropIndex($index);
                } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                    // Ignore missing index.
                }
            }
        });
    }

            DB::table('monthly_payment_reminder_logs')
                ->whereNull('batch_key')
                ->orderBy('id')
                ->chunkById(500, function ($rows) {

                            '%s|%s|%s',

                            $row->reminder_date,
                            $row->source ?: 'automatic'

                });
            } catch (Throwable $e) {
                // Ignore if the target index already exists.
            }

            if (Schema::hasColumn('monthly_payment_reminder_logs', 'batch_key')) {
                try {
                    Schema::table('monthly_payment_reminder_logs', function (Blueprint $table) {
                        $table->string('batch_key', 150)->nullable(false)->change();

                }

{
    /**
     * =========================================================================
     * CREATE NOTIFICATIONS TABLE
     * =========================================================================
     *
     * Taqwa Properties BD ERP
     *
     * One unified notification table for:
     *
     * - Admin
     * - Member
     * - Deposit
     * - Payment
     * - Sale
     * - Profile
     * - Password
     * - Reminder
     * - Notice
     * - System notifications
     *
     * Architecture:
     *
     * OWNER
     *   owner_type
     *   owner_id
     *   owner_uuid
     *
     * SOURCE
     *   source_type
     *   source_id
     *   source_uuid
     *
     * ACTION
     *   action_url
     *
     * META
     *   data
     *   priority
     *   channel
     *   expires_at
     *
     * IDENTIFICATION
     *   id
     *   record_uuid
     *
     * STATUS
     *   is_read
     *   read_at
     *
     * No legacy duplicate fields:
     *
     * - user_type   ❌
     * - user_id     ❌
     * - member_id   ❌
     * - deposit_id  ❌
     * - icon        ❌
     * - url         ❌
     *
     * Those values are represented through the new architecture.
     */
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::create('notifications', function (Blueprint $table) {

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 01. PRIMARY KEY
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->id();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 02. PUBLIC RECORD UUID
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Numeric ID remains the database primary key.
            |
            | record_uuid is used as the public/application-level identifier.
            |
            */

            $table->uuid('record_uuid')
                ->unique();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 03. OWNER / RECEIVER
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | owner_type:
            |     admin
            |     member
            |
            | owner_id:
            |     Admin.id / Member.id
            |
            | owner_uuid:
            |     Admin.record_uuid / Member.record_uuid
            |
            */

            $table->string('owner_type', 30);

            $table->unsignedBigInteger('owner_id');

            $table->uuid('owner_uuid')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 04. NOTIFICATION TYPE
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Examples:
            |
            | general
            | system
            | notice
            | reminder
            | deposit_submitted
            | deposit_approved
            | deposit_rejected
            | member_registered
            | member_approved
            | member_rejected
            |
            */

            $table->string('type', 80)
                ->default('general');


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 05. NOTIFICATION CONTENT
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->string('title', 255);

            $table->text('message')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 06. SOURCE / REFERENCE
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Examples:
            |
            | source_type = deposit
            | source_id   = 123
            | source_uuid = deposit.record_uuid
            |
            | Other possible source types:
            |
            | sale
            | payment
            | profile
            | password
            | reminder
            | notice
            | other
            |
            */

            $table->string('source_type', 50)
                ->nullable();

            $table->unsignedBigInteger('source_id')
                ->nullable();

            $table->uuid('source_uuid')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 07. ACTION URL
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Examples:
            |
            | /member/deposits/123
            | /member/deposits/123/receipt
            | /admin/deposits/123/view-mr
            |
            */

            $table->text('action_url')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 08. READ / UNREAD STATUS
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->boolean('is_read')
                ->default(false);

            $table->timestamp('read_at')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 09. PRIORITY
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Supported application values:
            |
            | low
            | normal
            | high
            | urgent
            |
            */

            $table->string('priority', 20)
                ->default('normal');


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 10. CHANNEL
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Examples:
            |
            | app
            | email
            | whatsapp
            | sms
            | system
            |
            */

            $table->string('channel', 30)
                ->default('app');


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 11. EXTRA JSON DATA
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Stores additional notification-specific information.
            |
            | Example:
            |
            | {
            |     "icon": "fas fa-check-circle",
            |     "member_id": 55,
            |     "deposit_id": 123,
            |     "amount": 20000,
            |     "notification_key": "..."
            | }
            |
            */

            $table->json('data')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 12. EXPIRATION
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | NULL = notification never expires.
            |
            */

            $table->timestamp('expires_at')
                ->nullable();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 13. SOFT DELETE
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->softDeletes();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 14. CREATED / UPDATED
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->timestamps();


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 15. OWNER INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Main owner lookup:
            |
            | WHERE owner_type = ?
            | AND owner_id = ?
            |
            */

            $table->index(
                ['owner_type', 'owner_id'],
                'notifications_owner_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 16. OWNER + READ STATUS INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Used for unread notification count.
            |
            */

            $table->index(
                ['owner_type', 'owner_id', 'is_read'],
                'notifications_owner_read_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 17. OWNER + CREATED INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Used for notification list / latest notifications.
            |
            */

            $table->index(
                ['owner_type', 'owner_id', 'created_at'],
                'notifications_owner_created_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 18. SOURCE INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Example:
            |
            | WHERE source_type = 'deposit'
            | AND source_id = 123
            |
            */

            $table->index(
                ['source_type', 'source_id'],
                'notifications_source_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 19. TYPE INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->index(
                'type',
                'notifications_type_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 20. READ_AT INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->index(
                'read_at',
                'notifications_read_at_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 21. EXPIRATION INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->index(
                'expires_at',
                'notifications_expires_index'
            );


            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | 22. CHANNEL INDEX
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $table->index(
                'channel',
                'notifications_channel_index'
            );
        });
    }


    /**
     * =========================================================================
     * DOWN
     * =========================================================================
     */
    public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::dropIfExists('notifications');
    }
};

            ['investments', 'updated_by'],
            ['investments', 'responsible_person'],
            ['investments', 'project_id'],
            ['investments', 'member_id'],
            ['investments', 'created_by'],
            ['investments', 'approved_by'],
            ['import_batches', 'created_by'],
            ['expenses', 'project_id'],
            ['expenses', 'created_by'],
            ['dues', 'member_id'],
            ['dues', 'created_by'],
            ['deposits', 'updated_by'],
            ['deposits', 'rejected_by'],
            ['deposits', 'member_id'],
            ['deposits', 'created_by'],
            ['deposits', 'approved_by'],
            ['backups', 'created_by']
        ];

        foreach ($foreignKeys as [$table, $column]) {
            $this->dropForeignKeysForColumn($table, $column);
        }
    }

    private function addForeignKeyIfMissing(
        string $table,
        string $column,
        string $foreignTable,
        string $foreignColumn,
        string $onDelete,
        string $constraintName
    ): void {
        if (
            !Schema::hasTable($table) ||
            !Schema::hasColumn($table, $column) ||
            !Schema::hasTable($foreignTable) ||
            !Schema::hasColumn($foreignTable, $foreignColumn)
        ) {
            return;
        }

        $existing = $this->foreignKeysForColumn($table, $column);

        foreach ($existing as $foreignKey) {
            $sameTarget =
                strtolower((string) $foreignKey->REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME) === strtolower($foreignTable) &&
                strtolower((string) $foreignKey->REFERENCED_COLUMN_NAME) === strtolower($foreignColumn);

            if ($sameTarget) {
                return;
            }
        }

        foreach ($existing as $foreignKey) {
            $this->dropForeignKey($table, (string) $foreignKey->CONSTRAINT_NAME);
        }

        $onDelete = strtolower(trim($onDelete));

        /*
         * The original schema has one SET NULL relationship whose child
         * column was NOT NULL: profits.member_id. Make that known legacy
         * column nullable using its actual MariaDB COLUMN_TYPE.
         */
        if ($onDelete === 'set null' && !$this->isNullable($table, $column)) {
            if ($table === 'profits' && $column === 'member_id') {
                $this->makeColumnNullable($table, $column);
            } else {
                return;
            }
        }

        if ($onDelete === 'set null' && !$this->isNullable($table, $column)) {
            return;
        }

        if ($this->constraintExists($constraintName)) {
            $constraintName = $this->uniqueConstraintName($table, $column);
        }

        Schema::table($table, function (Blueprint $blueprint) use (
            $column,
            $foreignTable,
            $foreignColumn,
            $onDelete,
            $constraintName
        ) {
            $blueprint
                ->foreign($column, $constraintName)
                ->references($foreignColumn)
                ->on($foreignTable)
                ->onDelete($onDelete);
        });
    }

    private function foreignKeysForColumn(string $table, string $column): array
    {
        if (!Schema::hasTable($table) || !Schema::hasColumn($table, $column)) {
            return [];
        }

        return DB::select(
            'SELECT CONSTRAINT_NAME, REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME, REFERENCED_COLUMN_NAME
             FROM information_schema.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE
             WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
               AND TABLE_NAME = ?
               AND COLUMN_NAME = ?
               AND REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME IS NOT NULL',
            [$table, $column]
        );
    }

    private function dropForeignKeysForColumn(string $table, string $column): void
    {
        foreach ($this->foreignKeysForColumn($table, $column) as $foreignKey) {
            $this->dropForeignKey($table, (string) $foreignKey->CONSTRAINT_NAME);
        }
    }

    private function dropForeignKey(string $table, string $constraintName): void
    {
        try {
            Schema::table($table, function (Blueprint $blueprint) use ($constraintName) {
                $blueprint->dropForeign($constraintName);
            });
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            DB::statement(
                'ALTER TABLE ' . $this->quoteIdentifier($table) .
                ' DROP FOREIGN KEY ' . $this->quoteIdentifier($constraintName)
            );
        }
    }

    private function isNullable(string $table, string $column): bool
    {
        $rows = DB::select(
            'SELECT IS_NULLABLE
             FROM information_schema.COLUMNS
             WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
               AND TABLE_NAME = ?
               AND COLUMN_NAME = ?
             LIMIT 1',
            [$table, $column]
        );

        return isset($rows[0]) &&
            strtoupper((string) $rows[0]->IS_NULLABLE) === 'YES';
    }

    private function makeColumnNullable(string $table, string $column): void
    {
        $rows = DB::select(
            'SELECT COLUMN_TYPE, COLUMN_DEFAULT
             FROM information_schema.COLUMNS
             WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
               AND TABLE_NAME = ?
               AND COLUMN_NAME = ?
             LIMIT 1',
            [$table, $column]
        );

        if (empty($rows)) {
            return;
        }

        $type = (string) $rows[0]->COLUMN_TYPE;
        $default = $rows[0]->COLUMN_DEFAULT;

        $defaultSql = '';
        if ($default !== null) {
            $upper = strtoupper((string) $default);

            if (
                $upper === 'CURRENT_TIMESTAMP' ||
                str_starts_with($upper, 'CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(')
            ) {
                $defaultSql = ' DEFAULT ' . $default;
            } else {
                $quoted = DB::getPdo()->quote((string) $default);
                $defaultSql = ' DEFAULT ' . $quoted;
            }
        }

        DB::statement(
            'ALTER TABLE ' . $this->quoteIdentifier($table) .
            ' MODIFY COLUMN ' . $this->quoteIdentifier($column) .
            ' ' . $type . ' NULL' . $defaultSql
        );
    }

    private function constraintExists(string $constraintName): bool
    {
        $rows = DB::select(
            'SELECT CONSTRAINT_NAME
             FROM information_schema.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS
             WHERE CONSTRAINT_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
               AND CONSTRAINT_NAME = ?
             LIMIT 1',
            [$constraintName]
        );

        return !empty($rows);
    }

    private function uniqueConstraintName(string $table, string $column): string
    {
        $base = 'tps_' . $table . '_' . $column . '_fk';
        $name = substr($base, 0, 60);
        $counter = 1;

        while ($this->constraintExists($name)) {
            $suffix = '_' . $counter++;
            $name = substr($base, 0, 64 - strlen($suffix)) . $suffix;
        }

        return $name;
    }

    private function quoteIdentifier(string $value): string
    {
        return '`' . str_replace('`', '``', $value) . '`';
    }
};

--- 2026_09_19_999998_add_tps_compatibility_security_columns.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| TPS ERP - Compatibility & Security Columns
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Version: 2.0 SAFE
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Purpose:
| - Add compatibility/security columns only when missing.
| - Safe to run against an existing TPS database.
| - Prevent duplicate-column errors.
| - Preserve all existing data and columns.
|
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

return new class extends Migration
{
    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Add Column If Missing
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    private function addColumnIfMissing(
        string $table,
        string $column,
        callable $definition
    ): void {
        if (!Schema::hasTable($table)) {
            return;
        }

        if (Schema::hasColumn($table, $column)) {
            return;
        }

        Schema::table($table, function (Blueprint $blueprint) use (
            $column,
            $definition
        ) {
            $definition($blueprint);
        });
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | UP
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function up(): void
    {
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | USERS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $this->addColumnIfMissing(
            'users',
            'email_verified_at',
            function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->timestamp('email_verified_at')
                    ->nullable()
                    ->after('email');
            }

    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DOWN
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Only remove columns that this migration is responsible for.
    | Existing columns that were already present before this migration
    | should NOT be deleted during rollback.
    |
    */

    public function down(): void
    {
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | USERS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $this->dropColumnIfExists('users', [
            'two_factor_secret',
            'two_factor_enabled',
            'password_changed_at',
            'locked_until',
            'failed_login_attempts',
            'last_login_ip',
            'last_login_at',
            'email_verified_at',
        ]);


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | ADMINS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

            'two_factor_secret',
            'two_factor_enabled',
            'password_changed_at',
            'locked_until',
            'failed_login_attempts',
            'last_login_ip',
            'last_login_at',
            'email_verified_at',
        ]);

        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | MEMBERS
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

            'two_factor_secret',
            'two_factor_enabled',
            'password_changed_at',
            'locked_until',
            'failed_login_attempts',
            'last_login_ip',
            'last_login_at',
            'email_verified_at',
        ]);
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Drop Columns If They Exist
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    private function dropColumnIfExists(
        string $table,
        array $columns
    ): void {
        if (!Schema::hasTable($table)) {
            return;
        }

        $existingColumns = [];

        foreach ($columns as $column) {
            if (Schema::hasColumn($table, $column)) {
                $existingColumns[] = $column;
            }
        }

        if (empty($existingColumns)) {
            return;
        }

        Schema::table(
            $table,
            function (Blueprint $blueprint) use ($existingColumns) {
                foreach ($existingColumns as $column) {
                    $blueprint->dropColumn($column);
                }
            }
        );
    }
};

         * Intentionally non-destructive.

    private function addColumns(string $table, array $definitions): void
    {
        if (!Schema::hasTable($table)) {
            return;
        }

        foreach ($definitions as $column => $definition) {
            if (Schema::hasColumn($table, $column)) {
                continue;
            }

            Schema::table($table, function (Blueprint $blueprint) use ($definition) {
                $definition($blueprint);
            });
        }
    }

    private function createFundsTables(): void
    {
        if (!Schema::hasTable('funds')) {
            Schema::create('funds', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->id();
                $table->uuid('record_uuid')->unique();
                $table->string('fund_code', 50)->unique();
                $table->string('name', 191);
                $table->string('fund_type', 50)->default('general');
                $table->decimal('opening_balance', 18, 2)->default(0);
                $table->decimal('current_balance', 18, 2)->default(0);
                $table->string('status', 30)->default('active');
                $table->text('description')->nullable();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('created_by')->nullable();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('updated_by')->nullable();
                $table->softDeletes();
                $table->timestamps();

                $table->index(['status', 'fund_type'], 'funds_status_fund_type_index');
            });
        }

        if (!Schema::hasTable('fund_transactions')) {
            Schema::create('fund_transactions', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->id();
                $table->uuid('record_uuid')->unique();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('fund_id');
                $table->string('transaction_no', 50)->unique();
                $table->string('type', 30);
                $table->string('category', 100)->nullable();
                $table->decimal('amount', 18, 2)->default(0);
                $table->decimal('balance_after', 18, 2)->default(0);
                $table->string('reference_type', 100)->nullable();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('reference_id')->nullable();
                $table->dateTime('transaction_date');
                $table->text('description')->nullable();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('created_by')->nullable();
                $table->timestamps();

                $table->index(['fund_id', 'transaction_date'], 'fund_transactions_fund_date_index');
                $table->index(['reference_type', 'reference_id'], 'fund_transactions_reference_index');
            });
        }

        $this->addForeignKeyIfPossible('funds', 'created_by', 'admins', 'id', 'set null', 'funds_created_by_foreign');
        $this->addForeignKeyIfPossible('funds', 'updated_by', 'admins', 'id', 'set null', 'funds_updated_by_foreign');
        $this->addForeignKeyIfPossible('fund_transactions', 'fund_id', 'funds', 'id', 'cascade', 'fund_transactions_fund_id_foreign');
        $this->addForeignKeyIfPossible('fund_transactions', 'created_by', 'admins', 'id', 'set null', 'fund_transactions_created_by_foreign');
    }

    private function addForeignKeyIfPossible(
        string $table,
        string $column,
        string $foreignTable,
        string $foreignColumn,
        string $onDelete,
        string $name
    ): void {
        if (
            !Schema::hasTable($table) ||
            !Schema::hasColumn($table, $column) ||
            !Schema::hasTable($foreignTable)
        ) {
            return;
        }

        $exists = DB::select(
            'SELECT CONSTRAINT_NAME
             FROM information_schema.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE
             WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE()
               AND TABLE_NAME = ?
               AND COLUMN_NAME = ?
               AND REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME = ?
               AND REFERENCED_COLUMN_NAME = ?
             LIMIT 1',
            [$table, $column, $foreignTable, $foreignColumn]
        );

        if (!empty($exists)) {
            return;
        }

        Schema::table($table, function (Blueprint $blueprint) use (
            $column,
            $foreignTable,
            $foreignColumn,
            $onDelete,
            $name
        ) {
            $blueprint
                ->foreign($column, $name)
                ->references($foreignColumn)
                ->on($foreignTable)
                ->onDelete($onDelete);
        });
    }
};
