--- 2026_10_03_120000_upgrade_monthly_payment_reminder_logs_for_batches.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;


return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        if (!Schema::hasTable('monthly_payment_reminder_logs')) {
            return;
        }

        /*
         * 1. Add batch_key if it does not exist.
         */
        if (!Schema::hasColumn(
            'monthly_payment_reminder_logs',
            'batch_key'
        )) {
            Schema::table(
                'monthly_payment_reminder_logs',
                function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->string('batch_key', 150)
                        ->nullable()
                        ->after('source');
                }
            );
        }

        /*
         * 2. Backfill old records.
         *
         * Legacy records receive a deterministic batch key.
         */
        DB::table('monthly_payment_reminder_logs')
            ->whereNull('batch_key')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->chunkById(500, function ($rows) {
                foreach ($rows as $row) {
                    $batchKey = sprintf(
                        '%s|%s|%s',
                        $row->month_key,
                        $row->reminder_date,
                        $row->source ?: 'automatic'
                    );

                    DB::table('monthly_payment_reminder_logs')
                        ->where('id', $row->id)
                        ->update([
                            'batch_key' => $batchKey,
                        ]);
                }
            });

        /*
         * 3. Remove the old duplicate protection.
         *
         * Old:
         * member_id + reminder_date + month_key + source
         *
         * This prevents a new reminder batch after reset.
         */
        try {
            Schema::table(
                'monthly_payment_reminder_logs',
                function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->dropUnique('monthly_reminder_unique');
                }
            );
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            // Already removed or not present.
        }

        /*
         * 4. Add batch-based unique protection.
         *
         * Same member can receive reminders in different batches.
         * Same member cannot be inserted twice into the same batch.
         */
        try {
            Schema::table(
                'monthly_payment_reminder_logs',
                function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->unique(
                        ['member_id', 'batch_key'],
                        'monthly_reminder_batch_unique'
                    );
                }
            );
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            // Index already exists.
        }

        /*
         * 5. Add batch_key index.
         */
        try {
            Schema::table(
                'monthly_payment_reminder_logs',
                function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->index(
                        'batch_key',
                        'monthly_payment_reminder_logs_batch_key_index'
                    );
                }
            );
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            // Index already exists.
        }

        /*
         * Do NOT force nullable(false) here.
         *
         * Keeping it nullable avoids requiring doctrine/dbal
         * for existing installations.
         *
         * The application will always write batch_key
         * for new reminder records.
         */
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        /*
         * Intentionally non-destructive.
         *
         * Existing reminder history must not be deleted
         * when this migration is rolled back.
         */
    }
};

            );

        DB::statement("
            ALTER TABLE sale_payment_adjustments
            MODIFY adjustment_type
            ENUM(
                'installment',
                'advance',
                'late_fee',
                'other'
            )
            NOT NULL DEFAULT 'installment'
        ");

        DB::statement("

<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Add UUID columns without deleting existing records
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        foreach (['profits', 'profit_distributions'] as $tableName) {
            if (! Schema::hasColumn($tableName, 'record_uuid')) {
                Schema::table($tableName, function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->uuid('record_uuid')->nullable();
                });
            }
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Backfill UUIDs for existing records
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        foreach (['profits', 'profit_distributions'] as $tableName) {
            DB::table($tableName)
                ->whereNull('record_uuid')
                ->orderBy('id')
                ->chunkById(500, function ($rows) use ($tableName) {
                    foreach ($rows as $row) {
                        DB::table($tableName)
                            ->where('id', $row->id)
                            ->whereNull('record_uuid')
                            ->update([
                                'record_uuid' => (string) Str::uuid(),
                            ]);
                    }
                });
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Add unique indexes after backfilling
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        foreach (['profits', 'profit_distributions'] as $tableName) {
            $indexName = $tableName . '_record_uuid_unique';

            $hasIndex = collect(
                DB::select("SHOW INDEX FROM `{$tableName}`")
            )->contains(function ($index) use ($indexName) {
                return $index->Key_name === $indexName;
            });

            if (! $hasIndex) {
                Schema::table($tableName, function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->unique('record_uuid');
                });
            }
        }
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        foreach (['profits', 'profit_distributions'] as $tableName) {
            if (! Schema::hasColumn($tableName, 'record_uuid')) {
                continue;
            }

            $indexName = $tableName . '_record_uuid_unique';

            $hasIndex = collect(
                DB::select("SHOW INDEX FROM `{$tableName}`")
            )->contains(function ($index) use ($indexName) {
                return $index->Key_name === $indexName;
            });

            if ($hasIndex) {
                Schema::table($tableName, function (Blueprint $table) {
                    $table->dropUnique(
                        $table->getTable() . '_record_uuid_unique'
                    );
                });
            }

            Schema::table($tableName, function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->dropColumn('record_uuid');
            });
        }
    }

<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        if (!Schema::hasTable('project_costs')) {
            throw new RuntimeException(
                'project_costs table must exist before copying legacy data.'
            );
        }

        $sources = [
            'investments' => 'investment',
            'project_expenses' => 'expense',
        ];

        foreach ($sources as $table => $type) {
            if (!Schema::hasTable($table)) {
                continue;
            }

            DB::table($table)
                ->orderBy('id')
                ->chunk(200, function ($rows) use ($table, $type) {
                    foreach ($rows as $row) {
                        $r = (array) $row;

                        $sourceId = $r['id'] ?? null;

                        if (!$sourceId) {
                            continue;
                        }

                        $exists = DB::table('project_costs')
                            ->where('legacy_source_table', $table)
                            ->where('legacy_source_id', $sourceId)
                            ->exists();

                        if ($exists) {
                            continue;
                        }

                        $date = $type === 'investment'
                            ? ($r['investment_date'] ?? null)
                            : ($r['expense_date'] ?? null);

                        $payload = [
                            'record_uuid' => $r['record_uuid']
                                ?? (string) Str::uuid(),

                            'entry_type' => $type,
                            'legacy_source_table' => $table,
                            'legacy_source_id' => $sourceId,

                            'project_id' => $r['project_id'] ?? null,
                            'member_id' => $r['member_id'] ?? null,
                            'responsible_person' =>
                                $r['responsible_person'] ?? null,

                            'investment_no' =>
                                $r['investment_no'] ?? null,
                            'expense_no' => $r['expense_no'] ?? null,

                            'investment_category' =>
                                $r['investment_category'] ?? null,
                            'expense_category' =>
                                $r['expense_category'] ?? null,

                            'description' => $r['description'] ?? null,
                            'amount' => $r['amount'] ?? 0,
                            'entry_date' => $date,

                            'reference_no' => $r['reference_no'] ?? null,
                            'paid_to' => $r['paid_to'] ?? null,
                            'payment_method' =>
                                $r['payment_method'] ?? 'cash',
                            'to_bank_id' => $r['to_bank_id'] ?? null,

                            'is_approved' =>
                                $type === 'investment'
                                    ? (bool) ($r['is_approved'] ?? false)
                                    : false,

                            'approved_at' => $r['approved_at'] ?? null,
                            'approved_by' => $r['approved_by'] ?? null,

                            'remarks' => $r['remarks'] ?? null,
                            'document_file' =>
                                $r['document_file'] ?? null,

                            'created_by' => $r['created_by'] ?? null,
                            'updated_by' => $r['updated_by'] ?? null,

                            'created_at' =>
                                $r['created_at'] ?? now(),
                            'updated_at' =>
                                $r['updated_at'] ?? now(),
                            'deleted_at' => $r['deleted_at'] ?? null,
                        ];

                        // Insert only columns present in the destination.
                        $payload = array_filter(
                            $payload,
                            fn ($value, $key) =>
                                Schema::hasColumn('project_costs', $key),
                            ARRAY_FILTER_USE_BOTH
                        );

                        DB::table('project_costs')->insert($payload);
                    }
                });
        }
    }

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration

    public function up(): void
    {

                ->nullable()
                ->index();

            $table->unsignedBigInteger('finance_verified_by_member_id')
                ->nullable()
                ->index();

            $table->unsignedBigInteger('final_approved_by_member_id')
                ->nullable()

            $table->unsignedBigInteger('returned_by_member_id')
                ->nullable()
                ->index();

            // Preserve legacy Admin data.
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('created_by')
                ->nullable()
                ->change();
        });

        Schema::table('ams_approval_steps', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('approver_member_id')
                ->nullable()
                ->index();

            // Allows new steps to use Member IDs without inventing
            // a legacy Admin ID.
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('approver_admin_id')
                ->nullable()
                ->change();
        });

        Schema::table('ams_audit_logs', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->unsignedBigInteger('actor_member_id')
                ->nullable()
                ->index();

            // Keep the existing actor_admin_id and all historic logs.
        });

            $table->dropIndex(['actor_member_id']);
            $table->dropColumn('actor_member_id');
        });

        Schema::table('ams_approval_steps', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropIndex(['approver_member_id']);
            $table->dropColumn('approver_member_id');
        });

        Schema::table('ams_requests', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropIndex(['created_by_member_id']);
            $table->dropIndex(['finance_verified_by_member_id']);
            $table->dropIndex(['final_approved_by_member_id']);
            $table->dropIndex(['returned_by_member_id']);

            $table->dropColumn([
                'created_by_member_id',
                'finance_verified_by_member_id',
                'final_approved_by_member_id',

--- 2026_10_14_160755_add_revision_history_and_audit_metadata_to_ams.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        /*
         * Each approval step belongs to a specific revision.
         * Existing rows are preserved as revision 0.
         */
        if (!Schema::hasColumn('ams_approval_steps', 'revision_no')) {
            Schema::table('ams_approval_steps', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->unsignedInteger('revision_no')
                    ->default(0)
                    ->after('ams_request_id');
            });
        }

        /*
         * Replace the old unique key with a revision-aware key.
         * Existing approval-step rows are not deleted.
         */
        Schema::table('ams_approval_steps', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropUnique('ams_request_step_unique');

            $table->unique(
                ['ams_request_id', 'revision_no', 'step_no'],
                'ams_request_revision_step_unique'
            );
        });

        /*
         * Append-only request revision snapshots.
         */
        if (!Schema::hasTable('ams_request_revisions')) {
            Schema::create('ams_request_revisions', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->id();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('ams_request_id')->index();
                $table->unsignedInteger('revision_no');
                $table->json('request_data');
                $table->string('attachment_path')->nullable();
                $table->string('attachment_name')->nullable();
                $table->unsignedBigInteger('submitted_by_member_id')->nullable();
                $table->timestamp('submitted_at')->nullable();
                $table->json('metadata')->nullable();
                $table->timestamps();

                $table->unique(
                    ['ams_request_id', 'revision_no'],
                    'ams_request_revision_unique'
                );
            });
        }

        /*
         * Audit actor snapshots and optional device/location metadata.
         */
        $auditColumns = [
            'revision_no' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->unsignedInteger('revision_no')->default(0),

            'actor_type' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->string('actor_type', 30)->nullable(),

            'actor_name_snapshot' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->string('actor_name_snapshot', 255)->nullable(),

            'actor_member_code_snapshot' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->string('actor_member_code_snapshot', 100)->nullable(),

            'actor_designation_snapshot' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->string('actor_designation_snapshot', 100)->nullable(),

            'user_agent' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->text('user_agent')->nullable(),

            'device_summary' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->string('device_summary', 255)->nullable(),

            'location_latitude' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->decimal('location_latitude', 10, 7)->nullable(),

            'location_longitude' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->decimal('location_longitude', 10, 7)->nullable(),

            'location_accuracy_meters' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->decimal('location_accuracy_meters', 10, 2)->nullable(),

            'location_source' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->string('location_source', 50)->nullable(),

            'location_captured_at' => fn (Blueprint $table) =>
                $table->timestamp('location_captured_at')->nullable(),
        ];

        foreach ($auditColumns as $column => $definition) {
            if (!Schema::hasColumn('ams_audit_logs', $column)) {
                Schema::table('ams_audit_logs', function (Blueprint $table) use ($definition) {
                    $definition($table);
                });
            }
        }
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        /*
         * Intentionally do not automatically restore the old unique key:
         * later revisions may contain duplicate step_no values for the
         * same request. An automatic rollback could fail or lose history.
         *
         * Back up the database before any manual rollback.
         */
        Schema::table('ams_approval_steps', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropUnique('ams_request_revision_step_unique');
        });

        Schema::table('ams_approval_steps', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->unique(
                ['ams_request_id', 'revision_no', 'step_no'],
                'ams_request_revision_step_unique'
            );
        });

        // Preserve revision and audit data during rollback.
        // This migration's history tables/columns are intentionally retained.
    }

--- 2026_10_14_212540_add_record_uuid_to_projects_table.php ---
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        if (!Schema::hasColumn('projects', 'record_uuid')) {
            Schema::table('projects', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->uuid('record_uuid')->nullable();
            });
        }

        // Populate UUIDs for existing project records.
        DB::table('projects')
            ->whereNull('record_uuid')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->select('id')
            ->chunkById(500, function ($projects) {
                foreach ($projects as $project) {
                    DB::table('projects')
                        ->where('id', $project->id)
                        ->whereNull('record_uuid')
                        ->update([
                            'record_uuid' => (string) Str::uuid(),
                        ]);
                }
            });

        // Add a unique index only if it does not already exist.
        $indexes = collect(DB::select(
            "SHOW INDEX FROM projects"
        ))->pluck('Key_name')->unique()->values();

        if (!$indexes->contains('projects_record_uuid_unique')) {
            Schema::table('projects', function (Blueprint $table) {
                $table->unique(
                    'record_uuid',
                    'projects_record_uuid_unique'
                );
            });
        }
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        // Keep record_uuid and existing identifiers intact on rollback.
        // This migration intentionally does not remove project data.
    }
};

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;

class AccountingAccount extends Model
{
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'accounting_accounts';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'parent_id',
        'code',
        'name',
        'account_type',
        'account_subtype',
        'is_group',
        'is_system',
        'is_bank',
        'is_cash',
        'is_active',
        'opening_balance',
        'opening_balance_date',
        'description',
        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'is_group' => 'boolean',
        'is_system' => 'boolean',
        'is_bank' => 'boolean',
        'is_cash' => 'boolean',
        'is_active' => 'boolean',
        'opening_balance' => 'decimal:2',
        'opening_balance_date' => 'date',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Parent / Child Accounts
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function parent()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            self::class,
            'parent_id'
        );
    }

    public function children()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            self::class,
            'parent_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Bank Account
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function bankAccount()
    {
        return $this->hasOne(
            BankAccount::class,
            'accounting_account_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Transaction Lines
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function transactionLines()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            AccountingTransactionLine::class,
            'accounting_account_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Current Balance
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Balance is calculated dynamically from approved accounting ledger.
    |
    | Asset / Expense:
    | Opening Balance + Debit - Credit
    |
    | Liability / Equity / Income:
    | Opening Balance + Credit - Debit
    |
    */

    protected function currentBalance(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {

                $openingBalance = (float) (
                    $this->opening_balance ?? 0
                );

                $movement = AccountingTransactionLine::query()
                    ->where(
                        'accounting_account_id',
                        $this->id
                    )
->whereHas('transaction', function ($query) {
    $query->whereIn('status', [
        'approved',
        'reversed',
    ]);
})
                    ->selectRaw(
                        'COALESCE(SUM(debit), 0) as total_debit,
                         COALESCE(SUM(credit), 0) as total_credit'
                    )
                    ->first();

                $totalDebit = (float) (
                    $movement->total_debit ?? 0
                );

                $totalCredit = (float) (
                    $movement->total_credit ?? 0
                );

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | Normal Balance
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    in_array(
                        $this->account_type,
                        [
                            'liability',
                            'equity',
                            'income',
                            'revenue',
                        ],
                        true
                    )
                ) {
                    return $openingBalance
                        + $totalCredit
                        - $totalDebit;
                }

                return $openingBalance
                    + $totalDebit
                    - $totalCredit;
            }
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'is_active',
            true
        );
    }

    public function scopeBank($query)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'is_bank',
            true
        );
    }

    public function scopeCash($query)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'is_cash',
            true
        );
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use App\Models\Deposit;

class AccountingTransaction extends Model
{
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'accounting_transactions';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'transaction_no',
        'transaction_date',
        'transaction_type',
        'source_type',
        'source_id',
        'reference_no',
        'voucher_no',
        'description',
        'status',
        'created_by',
        'approved_by',
        'approved_at',
        'reversed_transaction_id',
        'reversal_reason',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'transaction_date' => 'date',
        'approved_at' => 'datetime',
        'source_id' => 'integer',
        'created_by' => 'integer',
        'approved_by' => 'integer',
        'reversed_transaction_id' => 'integer',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Transaction Lines
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function lines()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            AccountingTransactionLine::class,
            'accounting_transaction_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Source / Deposit
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function deposit()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Deposit::class,
            'source_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Reversal
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function reversedTransaction()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            self::class,
            'reversed_transaction_id'
        );
    }

    public function reversal()
    {
        return $this->hasOne(
            self::class,
            'reversed_transaction_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Users
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function creator()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            User::class,
            'created_by'
        );
    }

    public function approver()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            User::class,
            'approved_by'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeApproved($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', 'approved');
    }

    public function scopePending($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', 'pending');
    }

    public function scopeDraft($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', 'draft');
    }

    public function scopeRejected($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', 'rejected');
    }

    public function scopeReversed($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', 'reversed');
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Debit / Credit Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getTotalDebitAttribute()
    {
        return $this->lines->sum(function ($line) {
            return (float) $line->debit;
        });
    }

    public function getTotalCreditAttribute()
    {
        return $this->lines->sum(function ($line) {
            return (float) $line->credit;
        });
    }

    public function getIsBalancedAttribute()
    {
        return bccomp(
            number_format($this->total_debit, 2, '.', ''),
            number_format($this->total_credit, 2, '.', ''),
            2
        ) === 0;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\AccountSecurity.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * ACCOUNT SECURITY
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Central security state for Admin / Member accounts.
 *
 * Supported account types:
 * - admin
 * - member
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * - Failed login counter
 * - Temporary account lock
 * - Password change/reset timestamps
 * - Forced password change state
 *
 * NOTE:
 * This model stores SECURITY STATE.
 * Authentication itself remains handled by the respective Laravel guards.
 */
class AccountSecurity extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'account_security';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'failed_login_attempts',
        'last_failed_login_at',
        'locked_until',

        'password_changed_at',
        'password_reset_at',
        'force_password_change',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'failed_login_attempts' => 'integer',

            'last_failed_login_at' => 'datetime',
            'locked_until'         => 'datetime',
            'password_changed_at'  => 'datetime',
            'password_reset_at'   => 'datetime',

            'force_password_change' => 'boolean',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Related Admin account.
     */
    public function admin(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'user_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Related Member account.
     */
    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Member::class,
            'user_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Account Resolver
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Resolve the actual account based on user_type.
     *
     * This intentionally is not a polymorphic relationship because
     * AccountSecurity uses separate admin/member guards.
     */
    public function resolveAccount(): Admin|Member|null
    {
        return match ($this->normalizedUserType()) {
            'admin'  => $this->admin,
            'member' => $this->member,
            default  => null,
        };
    }

    /**
     * Backward-compatible accessor.
     *
     * Usage:
     * $security->resolved_user
     */
    public function getResolvedUserAttribute(): Admin|Member|null
    {
        return $this->resolveAccount();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | User Type
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function normalizedUserType(): ?string
    {
        $type = strtolower(trim((string) $this->user_type));

        return match ($type) {
            'admin',
            'app\models\admin',
            'administrator' => 'admin',

            'member',
            'app\models\member' => 'member',

            default => $type !== '' ? $type : null,
        };
    }

    public function isAdmin(): bool
    {
        return $this->normalizedUserType() === 'admin';
    }

    public function isMember(): bool
    {
        return $this->normalizedUserType() === 'member';
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Lock State
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Determine whether account is currently locked.
     */
    public function isLocked(): bool
    {
        if (!$this->locked_until) {
            return false;
        }

        return $this->locked_until->isFuture();
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether a previous lock has expired.
     */
    public function hasExpiredLock(): bool
    {
        return $this->locked_until !== null
            && $this->locked_until->isPast();
    }

    /**
     * Remaining lock time in seconds.
     */
    public function lockedSeconds(): int
    {
        if (!$this->isLocked()) {
            return 0;
        }

        return max(
            0,
            (int) now()->diffInSeconds($this->locked_until)
        );
    }

    /**
     * Remaining lock time in minutes.
     */
    public function lockedMinutes(): int
    {
        if (!$this->isLocked()) {
            return 0;
        }

        return max(
            0,
            (int) now()->diffInMinutes($this->locked_until)
        );
    }

    /**
     * Automatically clear an expired lock.
     */
    public function clearExpiredLock(bool $save = true): bool
    {
        if (!$this->hasExpiredLock()) {
            return false;
        }

        $this->locked_until = null;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Failed Login State
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasFailedAttempts(): bool
    {
        return $this->failedLoginAttempts() > 0;
    }

    public function failedLoginAttempts(): int
    {
        return max(
            0,
            (int) $this->failed_login_attempts
        );
    }

    /**
     * Increment failed login counter.
     */
    public function incrementFailedAttempts(
        ?Carbon $at = null,
        bool $save = true
    ): int {
        $this->failed_login_attempts =
            $this->failedLoginAttempts() + 1;

        $this->last_failed_login_at =
            $at ?: now();

        if ($save) {
            $this->save();
        }

        return $this->failed_login_attempts;
    }

    /**
     * Reset failed login state after successful authentication.
     */
    public function resetFailedAttempts(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->failed_login_attempts = 0;
        $this->last_failed_login_at = null;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Lock Management
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Lock account until a specific time.
     */
    public function lockUntil(
        Carbon $until,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->locked_until = $until;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Lock account for a number of minutes.
     */
    public function lockForMinutes(
        int $minutes,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $minutes = max(1, $minutes);

        return $this->lockUntil(
            now()->addMinutes($minutes),
            $save
        );
    }

    /**
     * Unlock account and optionally reset failed attempts.
     */
    public function unlock(
        bool $resetAttempts = true,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->locked_until = null;

        if ($resetAttempts) {
            $this->failed_login_attempts = 0;
            $this->last_failed_login_at = null;
        }

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Password Security
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function requiresPasswordChange(): bool
    {
        return (bool) $this->force_password_change;
    }

    public function markPasswordChanged(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->password_changed_at = now();
        $this->force_password_change = false;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function markPasswordReset(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->password_reset_at = now();

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function requirePasswordChange(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->force_password_change = true;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Query Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeForAdmin(
        Builder $query,
        int $adminId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->whereIn('user_type', [
                'admin',
                Admin::class,
            ])
            ->where('user_id', $adminId);
    }

    public function scopeForMember(
        Builder $query,
        int $memberId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->whereIn('user_type', [
                'member',
                Member::class,
            ])
            ->where('user_id', $memberId);
    }

    public function scopeLocked(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNotNull('locked_until')
            ->where('locked_until', '>', now());
    }

    public function scopeUnlocked(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(function ($q) {
            $q->whereNull('locked_until')
                ->orWhere('locked_until', '<=', now());
        });
    }

    public function scopePasswordChangeRequired(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'force_password_change',
            true
        );
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\ActivityLog.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * ACTIVITY LOG
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Human-readable application activity.
 *
 * Examples:
 * - Admin logged in
 * - Member submitted deposit
 * - Admin approved deposit
 * - Admin viewed member
 * - Member updated profile
 *
 * ActivityLog is NOT the authoritative database-change audit trail.
 * For old/new values use AuditLog.
 */
class ActivityLog extends BaseModel
{
    use HasFactory;

    protected $table = 'activity_logs';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',

        'action',
        'module',
        'description',

        'ip_address',
        'browser',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'created_at' => 'datetime',
            'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeOfAction(
        Builder $query,
        string $action
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('action', $action);
    }

    public function scopeOfModule(
        Builder $query,
        string $module
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('module', $module);
    }

    public function scopeOfUser(
        Builder $query,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    public function scopeOfUserType(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'user_type',
            $userType
        );
    }

    public function scopeAdmin(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereIn(
            'user_type',
            [
                'admin',
                Admin::class,
            ]
        );
    }

    public function scopeMember(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereIn(
            'user_type',
            [
                'member',
                Member::class,
            ]
        );
    }

    public function scopeGuest(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(function ($q) {
            $q->whereNull('user_id')
                ->orWhere('user_type', 'guest');
        });
    }

    public function scopeByDateRange(
        Builder $query,
        $startDate,
        $endDate
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereBetween(
            'created_at',
            [
                $startDate,
                $endDate,
            ]
        );
    }

    public function scopeRecent(
        Builder $query,
        int $days = 30
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'created_at',
            '>=',
            now()->subDays(max(1, $days))
        );
    }

    public function scopeSearch(
        Builder $query,
        string $search
    ): Builder {
        $search = trim($search);

        if ($search === '') {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
            $q->where(
                'action',
                'like',
                "%{$search}%"
            )
            ->orWhere(
                'module',
                'like',
                "%{$search}%"
            )
            ->orWhere(
                'description',
                'like',
                "%{$search}%"
            );
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Accessors
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function userTypeLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {

                $type = strtolower(
                    trim((string) $this->user_type)
                );

                return match ($type) {

                    'admin',
                    'app\models\admin' =>
                        'Admin',

                    'member',
                    'app\models\member' =>
                        'Member',

                    'guest' =>
                        'Guest',

                    default =>
                        $this->user_type
                            ? ucfirst(
                                str_replace(
                                    '_',
                                    ' ',
                                    $this->user_type
                                )
                            )
                            : 'System',
                };
            }
        );
    }

    protected function timeAgo(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () =>
                $this->created_at
                    ? $this->created_at->diffForHumans()
                    : ''
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Identity Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isGuest(): bool
    {
        return !$this->user_id
            || strtolower(
                (string) $this->user_type
            ) === 'guest';
    }

    public function isAdmin(): bool
    {
        return in_array(
            strtolower(
                (string) $this->user_type
            ),
            [
                'admin',
                'app\\models\\admin',
            ],
            true
        );
    }

    public function isMember(): bool
    {
        return in_array(
            strtolower(
                (string) $this->user_type
            ),
            [
                'member',
                'app\\models\\member',
            ],
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Static Logging API
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function log(
        string $action,
        ?string $description = null,
        ?string $module = null,
        $user = null,
        ?string $ip = null,
        ?string $browser = null
    ): self {

        $user = $user
            ?? auth('admin')->user()
            ?? auth('member')->user();

        $userType = 'guest';
        $userId = null;

        if ($user) {
            $userId = $user->getKey();

            /*
             * Keep the fully-qualified class name because existing
             * morphTo() records may already use it.
             */
            $userType = get_class($user);
        }

        return static::create([
            'user_type' => $userType,
            'user_id' => $userId,

            'action' => Str::limit(
                trim($action),
                100,
                ''
            ),

            'module' => $module
                ? Str::limit(
                    trim($module),
                    100,
                    ''
                )
                : null,

            'description' => $description,

            'ip_address' =>
                $ip ?? request()->ip(),

            'browser' =>
                $browser
                ?? Str::limit(
                    (string) request()->userAgent(),
                    255,
                    ''
                ),
        ]);
    }

    public static function logAdmin(
        string $action,
        ?string $description = null,
        ?string $module = null,
        $admin = null
    ): self {
        return static::log(
            $action,
            $description,
            $module,
            $admin ?? auth('admin')->user()
        );
    }

    public static function logMember(
        string $action,
        ?string $description = null,
        ?string $module = null,
        $member = null
    ): self {
        return static::log(
            $action,
            $description,
            $module,
            $member ?? auth('member')->user()
        );
    }

    public static function logGuest(
        string $action,
        ?string $description = null,
        ?string $module = null
    ): self {
        return static::log(
            $action,
            $description,
            $module,
            null
        );
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Admin.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphMany;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;
use Laravel\Sanctum\HasApiTokens;
use Spatie\Permission\Exceptions\PermissionDoesNotExist;
use Spatie\Permission\Traits\HasRoles;

class Admin extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasFactory, Notifiable, HasRoles, HasApiTokens;

    protected $table = 'admins';

    protected $guard_name = 'admin';

    protected $fillable = [
        'admin_code',
        'name',
        'username',
        'email',
        'mobile',
        'password',
        'status',
        'last_login_at',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'last_login_at' => 'datetime',
        'status'        => 'string',
        'password'      => 'hashed',
    ];

    public const STATUS_ACTIVE = 'active';
    public const STATUS_INACTIVE = 'inactive';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function approvedDeposits(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Deposit::class, 'approved_by');
    }

    public function activityLogs(): MorphMany
    {
        return $this->morphMany(ActivityLog::class, 'user');
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Query Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query->whereRaw('LOWER(status) = ?', [
            self::STATUS_ACTIVE,
        ]);
    }

    public function scopeInactive($query)
    {
        return $query->whereRaw('LOWER(status) = ?', [
            self::STATUS_INACTIVE,
        ]);
    }

    public function scopeSearch($query, string $search)
    {
        $search = trim($search);

        if ($search === '') {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
            $q->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('email', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('username', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('mobile', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('admin_code', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhereHas('roles', function ($roleQuery) use ($search) {
                    $roleQuery
                        ->where('guard_name', 'admin')
                        ->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%");
                });
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Accessors and Labels
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getDisplayNameAttribute(): string
    {
        $name = trim((string) $this->name);
        $code = trim((string) $this->admin_code);

        return $code === '' ? $name : "{$name} ({$code})";
    }

    public function getRoleLabel(): string
    {
        $role = $this->getSpatieRoleName();

        if ($role === null) {
            return 'No Role Assigned';
        }

        return match (strtolower($role)) {
            'super-admin' => 'Super Admin',
            'admin'       => 'Admin',
            'manager'     => 'Manager',
            'accountant'  => 'Accountant',
            default       => ucwords(str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', $role)),
        };
    }

    public function getStatusLabel(): string
    {
        return $this->isActive() ? 'Active' : 'Inactive';
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isActive(): bool
    {
        return strtolower((string) $this->status) === self::STATUS_ACTIVE;
    }

    public function isInactive(): bool
    {
        return ! $this->isActive();
    }

    public function toggleStatus(): void
    {
        $this->status = $this->isActive()
            ? self::STATUS_INACTIVE
            : self::STATUS_ACTIVE;

        $this->save();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Spatie Roles
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasAdminRole(string $role): bool
    {
        return $this->hasRole($role, 'admin');
    }

    public function hasAnyAdminRole(array $roles): bool
    {
        return $this->hasAnyRole($roles, 'admin');
    }

    public function isSuperAdmin(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasAdminRole('super-admin');
    }

    public function hasAdminPermission(string $permission): bool
    {
        try {
            return $this->hasPermissionTo($permission, 'admin');
        } catch (PermissionDoesNotExist) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Return the first assigned Admin-guard role.
     * The application should enforce one primary role per Admin.
     */
    public function getSpatieRoleName(): ?string
    {
        if ($this->relationLoaded('roles')) {
            return $this->roles
                ->where('guard_name', 'admin')
                ->first()
                ?->name;
        }

        return $this->roles()
            ->where('guard_name', 'admin')
            ->value('name');
    }

    public function assignSpatieRole(string $role): void
    {
        $roleModel = Role::query()
            ->where('name', $role)
            ->where('guard_name', 'admin')
            ->firstOrFail();

        $this->syncRoles([$roleModel]);
    }

    public function removeSpatieRoles(): void
    {
        $this->syncRoles([]);
    }

    /**
     * Role options for Admin management forms.
     *
     * @return array<string, string>
     */
    public static function roleOptions(): array
    {
        return Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', 'admin')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->get()
            ->mapWithKeys(function (Role $role): array {
                $label = match (strtolower($role->name)) {
                    'super-admin' => 'Super Admin',
                    'admin'       => 'Admin',
                    'manager'     => 'Manager',
                    'accountant'  => 'Accountant',
                    default       => ucwords(
                        str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', $role->name)
                    ),
                };

                return [$role->name => $label];
            })
            ->all();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Login
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function updateLastLogin(): void
    {
        $this->forceFill([
            'last_login_at' => now(),
        ])->save();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Admin Code Generator
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function generateAdminCode(): string
    {
        $lastCode = self::query()
            ->where('admin_code', 'like', 'ADM-%')
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->value('admin_code');

        $lastNumber = 0;

        if ($lastCode) {
            $numberPart = substr($lastCode, 4);

            if (is_numeric($numberPart)) {
                $lastNumber = (int) $numberPart;
            }
        }

        do {
            $lastNumber++;

            $newCode = 'ADM-' . str_pad(
                (string) $lastNumber,
                3,
                '0',
                STR_PAD_LEFT
            );
        } while (
            self::query()->where('admin_code', $newCode)->exists()
        );

        return $newCode;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Available Statuses
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function statuses(): array
    {
        return [
            self::STATUS_ACTIVE   => 'Active',
            self::STATUS_INACTIVE => 'Inactive',
        ];
    }
}

            ->orderBy('revision_no')
            ->orderBy('step_no');

    public function allSteps(): HasMany
    {

    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class, 'returned_by_member_id');

    {
        return $this->steps()
            ->where('step_no', (int) $this->current_step);

    {
        return in_array($this->status, ['approved', 'rejected'], true);
    }

    {
        return $this->status === 'pending_finance';

    {

    }

    public function getCreatorMemberId(): ?int
    {
        return $this->created_by_member_id !== null
            ? (int) $this->created_by_member_id
            : null;
    }

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\AuditLog.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class AuditLog extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'audit_logs';

    public $timestamps = false;

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'actor_type',
        'actor_id',
        'actor_uuid',
        'actor_name',

        'module',
        'action',

        'table_name',
        'record_id',
        'target_uuid',

        'old_values',
        'new_values',
        'changed_fields',

        'request_id',
        'method',
        'route',
        'url',

        'ip_address',
        'user_agent',

        'context',

        'performed_at',
        'created_at',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'record_id' => 'integer',
            'actor_id' => 'integer',

            'old_values' => 'array',
            'new_values' => 'array',
            'changed_fields' => 'array',
            'context' => 'array',

            'performed_at' => 'datetime',
            'created_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function actor(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'actor',
            'actor_type',
            'actor_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Query Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeOfModule(
        Builder $query,
        string $module
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('module', $module);
    }

    public function scopeOfAction(
        Builder $query,
        string $action
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('action', $action);
    }

    public function scopeOfActor(
        Builder $query,
        string $actorType,
        int $actorId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('actor_type', $actorType)
            ->where('actor_id', $actorId);
    }

    public function scopeForRecord(
        Builder $query,
        string $table,
        int|string $recordId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('table_name', $table)
            ->where('record_id', $recordId);
    }

    public function scopeByRequest(
        Builder $query,
        string $requestId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('request_id', $requestId);
    }

    public function scopeByDateRange(
        Builder $query,
        $startDate,
        $endDate
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereBetween(
            'performed_at',
            [$startDate, $endDate]
        );
    }

    public function scopeRecent(
        Builder $query,
        int $days = 30
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'performed_at',
            '>=',
            now()->subDays(max(1, $days))
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasChanges(): bool
    {
        return !empty($this->changed_fields);
    }

    public function changedFieldCount(): int
    {
        return is_array($this->changed_fields)
            ? count($this->changed_fields)
            : 0;
    }

    public function isCreate(): bool
    {
        return strtolower((string) $this->action) === 'create';
    }

    public function isUpdate(): bool
    {
        return strtolower((string) $this->action) === 'update';
    }

    public function isDelete(): bool
    {
        return strtolower((string) $this->action) === 'delete';
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Static Audit API
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function record(
        string $action,
        string $module,
        ?string $tableName = null,
        int|string|null $recordId = null,
        ?array $oldValues = null,
        ?array $newValues = null,
        $actor = null,
        ?string $targetUuid = null,
        ?array $context = null
    ): self {
        // Respect an explicitly supplied actor.
        // Otherwise resolve the authenticated Admin or Member.
        $actor = $actor
            ?? Auth::guard('admin')->user()
            ?? Auth::guard('member')->user();

        $actorType = null;
        $actorId = null;
        $actorUuid = null;
        $actorName = null;

        if ($actor) {
            $actorType = get_class($actor);
            $actorId = $actor->getKey();

            $actorUuid = $actor->record_uuid
                ?? $actor->uuid
                ?? null;

            $actorName = $actor->name
                ?? $actor->full_name
                ?? $actor->username
                ?? $actor->email
                ?? null;
        }

        $oldValues = static::sanitizeValues($oldValues);
        $newValues = static::sanitizeValues($newValues);
        $context = static::sanitizeValues($context);

        $changedFields = static::calculateChangedFields(
            $oldValues,
            $newValues
        );

        $request = request();
        $now = now();

        return static::create([
            'record_uuid' => (string) Str::uuid(),

            'actor_type' => $actorType,
            'actor_id' => $actorId,
            'actor_uuid' => $actorUuid,
            'actor_name' => $actorName,

            'module' => $module,
            'action' => $action,

            'table_name' => $tableName,
            'record_id' => $recordId,
            'target_uuid' => $targetUuid,

            'old_values' => $oldValues,
            'new_values' => $newValues,
            'changed_fields' => $changedFields,

            'request_id' => static::requestId(),

            'method' => $request?->method(),
            'route' => $request?->route()?->getName(),
            'url' => $request?->fullUrl(),

            'ip_address' => $request?->ip(),
            'user_agent' => $request?->userAgent(),

            'context' => $context,

            'performed_at' => $now,
            'created_at' => $now,
        ]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Sanitization
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function sanitizeValues(
        ?array $values
    ): ?array {
        if ($values === null) {
            return null;
        }

        $sensitiveFields = [
            'password',
            'password_confirmation',
            'current_password',
            'new_password',
            'new_password_confirmation',

            'token',
            'access_token',
            'refresh_token',
            'remember_token',
            'api_token',

            'secret',
            'client_secret',
            'two_factor_secret',
            'two_factor_recovery_codes',

            'otp',
            'otp_code',
            'verification_code',
        ];

        $sanitize = function (
            array $items
        ) use (&$sanitize, $sensitiveFields): array {
            foreach ($items as $key => $value) {
                if (
                    is_string($key)
                    && in_array(
                        strtolower($key),
                        $sensitiveFields,
                        true
                    )
                ) {
                    $items[$key] = '[REDACTED]';
                    continue;
                }

                if (is_array($value)) {
                    $items[$key] = $sanitize($value);
                }
            }

            return $items;
        };

        return $sanitize($values);
    }

    protected static function calculateChangedFields(
        ?array $oldValues,
        ?array $newValues
    ): array {
        $oldValues ??= [];
        $newValues ??= [];

        $fields = array_unique(array_merge(
            array_keys($oldValues),
            array_keys($newValues)
        ));

        $changed = [];

        foreach ($fields as $field) {
            $oldExists = array_key_exists($field, $oldValues);
            $newExists = array_key_exists($field, $newValues);

            if ($oldExists !== $newExists) {
                $changed[] = $field;
                continue;
            }

            if (($oldValues[$field] ?? null) != ($newValues[$field] ?? null)) {
                $changed[] = $field;
            }
        }

        return array_values($changed);
    }

    protected static function requestId(): ?string
    {
        $request = request();

        return $request?->header('X-Request-ID')
            ?? $request?->header('X-Correlation-ID');
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;

class Backup extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;

    protected $table = 'backups';
    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    /**
     * The attributes that are mass assignable.
     */
    protected $fillable = [
        'backup_name',
        'backup_file',
        'backup_size',
        'backup_date',
        'created_by',
    ];

    /**
     * The attributes that should be cast.
     */
    protected $casts = [
        'backup_date' => 'datetime',
        'backup_size' => 'integer',
        'created_at'  => 'datetime',
        'updated_at'  => 'datetime',
    ];

    // ============================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================

    /**
     * Get the admin who created this backup.
     */
    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'created_by');
    }

    // ============================================
    // SCOPES
    // ============================================

    /**
     * Scope to order by backup date (newest first).
     */
    public function scopeRecent($query)
    {
        return $query->orderBy('backup_date', 'desc');
    }

    /**
     * Scope to search by backup name.
     */
    public function scopeSearch($query, string $search)
    {
        return $query->where('backup_name', 'like', "%{$search}%");
    }

    // ============================================
    // ACCESSORS & MUTATORS (Laravel 9+ Standard Syntax)
    // ============================================

    /**
     * Get the formatted file size (e.g., "2.5 MB").
     */
    protected function formattedSize(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                $bytes = $this->backup_size ?? 0;
                if ($bytes >= 1073741824) {
                    return number_format($bytes / 1073741824, 2) . ' GB';
                }
                if ($bytes >= 1048576) {
                    return number_format($bytes / 1048576, 2) . ' MB';
                }
                if ($bytes >= 1024) {
                    return number_format($bytes / 1024, 2) . ' KB';
                }
                return $bytes . ' bytes';
            }
        );
    }

    /**
     * Get the full storage URL for the backup file.
     */
    protected function downloadUrl(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => ($this->backup_file && $this->fileExists()) ? route('admin.backup.download', $this->id) : null
        );
    }

    /**
     * Get the backup date formatted for display.
     */
    protected function formattedDate(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => $this->backup_date ? $this->backup_date->format('d M Y, h:i A') : 'N/A'
        );
    }

    // ============================================
    // HELPERS
    // ============================================

    /**
     * Check if the backup file exists on disk.
     */
    public function fileExists(): bool
    {
        return $this->backup_file && Storage::disk('local')->exists($this->backup_file);
    }

    /**
     * Delete the backup file from disk.
     */
    public function deleteFile(): bool
    {
        if ($this->fileExists()) {
            return Storage::disk('local')->delete($this->backup_file);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Override delete to also remove the file.
     */
    public function delete(): bool
    {
        $this->deleteFile();
        return (bool) parent::delete();
    }

    /**
     * Create a new backup record from a file path and size.
     */
    public static function createFromFile(string $filePath, string $name = null, int $createdBy = null): self
    {
        $name = $name ?? basename($filePath);
        
        // âœ… Fix 1: à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦² à¦¸à¦¾à¦‡à¦œ à¦šà§‡à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¾à¦° à¦†à¦—à§‡ à¦«à¦¾à¦‡à¦²à¦Ÿà¦¿à¦° à¦…à¦¸à§à¦¤à¦¿à¦¤à§à¦¬ à¦¯à¦¾à¦šà¦¾à¦‡ à¦•à¦°à§‡ à¦à¦•à§à¦¸à§‡à¦ªà¦¶à¦¨ à¦¬à§à¦²à¦• à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡
        $size = 0;
        if (Storage::disk('local')->exists($filePath)) {
            $size = Storage::disk('local')->size($filePath);
        }

        return self::create([
            'backup_name' => $name,
            'backup_file' => $filePath,
            'backup_size' => $size,
            'backup_date' => now(),
            'created_by'  => $createdBy ?? auth('admin')->id(), // âœ… à¦ªà§à¦°à¦œà§‡à¦•à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦° à¦®à¦¾à¦²à§à¦Ÿà¦¿-à¦—à¦¾à¦°à§à¦¡à§‡à¦° à¦¸à¦¾à¦¥à§‡ à¦•à¦¨à¦¸à¦¿à¦¸à§à¦Ÿà§‡à¦¨à§à¦Ÿ à¦•à¦°à¦¾ à¦¹à§Ÿà§‡à¦›à§‡
        ]);
    }

    // ============================================
    // MODEL BOOT LOGIC
    // ============================================
    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function ($backup) {
            if (empty($backup->backup_date)) {
                $backup->backup_date = now();
            }
        });
    }
}
