<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class BankAccount extends Model
{
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'bank_accounts';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'accounting_account_id',
        'bank_name',
        'branch_name',
        'account_name',
        'account_number',
        'account_type',
        'routing_number',
        'currency',
        'opening_balance',
        'opening_balance_date',
        'is_active',
        'notes',
        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'opening_balance' => 'decimal:2',
        'opening_balance_date' => 'date',
        'is_active' => 'boolean',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Accounting Account
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function accountingAccount()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            AccountingAccount::class,
            'accounting_account_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'is_active',
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Display Helper
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getDisplayNameAttribute()
    {
        return trim(
            $this->bank_name .
            ' - ' .
            $this->account_name .
            ' - A/C ' .
            $this->account_number
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Current Balance
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Current Bank Balance:
    |
    | Bank Opening Balance
    | + Approved Ledger Debit
    | - Approved Ledger Credit
    |
    | The balance is calculated dynamically from the accounting ledger.
    | No separate balance column is required.
    |
    | IMPORTANT:
    | BankAccount opening_balance is treated as the bank's opening balance.
    | Do not add AccountingAccount opening_balance again for this bank.
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function currentBalance(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | OPENING BALANCE
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                $openingBalance = (float) (
                    $this->opening_balance ?? 0
                );


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | NO ACCOUNTING LINK
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                |
                | If this bank account is not linked with an accounting
                | account, only the opening balance is available.
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (!$this->accounting_account_id) {
                    return $openingBalance;
                }


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | APPROVED LEDGER MOVEMENT
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                $movement = AccountingTransactionLine::query()
                    ->where(
                        'accounting_account_id',
                        $this->accounting_account_id
                    )
                ->whereHas(
                    'transaction',
                    function ($query) {
                        $query->whereIn('status', [
                            'approved',
                            'reversed',
                        ]);
                    }
                    )
                    ->selectRaw(
                        'COALESCE(SUM(debit), 0) as total_debit,
                         COALESCE(SUM(credit), 0) as total_credit'
                    )
                    ->first();


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | TOTAL DEBIT
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                $totalDebit = (float) (
                    $movement->total_debit ?? 0
                );


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | TOTAL CREDIT
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                $totalCredit = (float) (
                    $movement->total_credit ?? 0
                );


                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                | CURRENT BALANCE
                |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                return $openingBalance
                    + $totalDebit
                    - $totalCredit;
            }
        );
    }
}

namespace App\Models;

use App\Traits\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * BASE MODEL
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Shared Eloquent foundation for the Taqwa Properties ERP.
 *
 * Keep this class intentionally small.
 * Business logic belongs in individual models/services.
 */
abstract class BaseModel extends Model
{
    use HasRecordUuid;

    /**
     * Common public/reference identifier.
     *
     * Numeric `id` remains the internal database primary key.
     * `record_uuid` is used as the public-safe reference.
     */
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models\Concerns;

use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * Adds a public UUID without changing the internal numeric primary key.
 *
 * Database convention:
 * - id          => internal numeric primary key
 * - record_uuid => public/external identifier
 */
trait HasRecordUuid
{
    protected static function bootHasRecordUuid(): void
    {
        static::creating(function ($model): void {
            if (empty($model->record_uuid)) {
                $model->record_uuid = (string) Str::uuid();
            }
        });
    }

    public function getRouteKeyName(): string
    {
        return 'record_uuid';
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class Customer extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'customers';

    protected $fillable = [
        'customer_code',
        'name',
        'mobile',
        'alternate_mobile',
        'nid',
        'address',
        'remarks',
        'status',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'status' => 'boolean',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | RELATIONSHIPS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Customer's sales
     */
    public function sales(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            Sale::class,
            'customer_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | BOOT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (Customer $customer) {

            if (empty($customer->customer_code)) {

                $customer->customer_code =
                    self::generateCustomerCode();
            }
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CUSTOMER CODE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function generateCustomerCode(): string
    {
        $year = date('Y');

        $last = self::withTrashed()
            ->where(
                'customer_code',
                'like',
                "CUS-{$year}-%"
            )
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        $lastNumber = 0;

        if ($last) {

            $lastNumber = (int) substr(
                $last->customer_code,
                -5
            );
        }

        $nextNumber = str_pad(
            $lastNumber + 1,
            5,
            '0',
            STR_PAD_LEFT
        );

        return "CUS-{$year}-{$nextNumber}";
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SCOPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            true
        );
    }

    public function scopeSearch(
        $query,
        string $term
    ) {
        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($term) {

            $q->where(
                'customer_code',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            )

            ->orWhere(
                'name',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            )

            ->orWhere(
                'mobile',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            )

            ->orWhere(
                'nid',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            );
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACCESSORS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return $this->status
            ? 'Active'
            : 'Inactive';
    }

    public function getTotalSalesAttribute(): float
    {
        return (float) $this->sales()
            ->sum('sale_price');
    }
}

// ============================================================
// GENERATE DEPOSIT NUMBER
// ============================================================

public static function generateDepositNo(): string
{
    $year = now()->format('Y');
    $month = now()->format('m');

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Prefix
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Example:
    | DEP-202609-
    |
    */

    $prefix = "DEP-{$year}{$month}-";

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Find latest VALID serial for current month
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Only standard Deposit No. format is considered:
    |
    | DEP-YYYYMM-00001
    | DEP-YYYYMM-00002
    |
    | Old/random formats such as:
    |
    | DEP-20260921-9E13A4
    |
    | are ignored.
    |
    */

    $latest = self::withTrashed()
        ->where(
            'deposit_no',
            'LIKE',
            $prefix . '%'
        )
        ->whereRaw(
            'deposit_no REGEXP ?',
            ['^DEP-[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{5}$']
        )
        ->orderByRaw(
            'CAST(SUBSTRING(deposit_no, 12, 5) AS UNSIGNED) DESC'
        )
        ->first();

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Determine next serial
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    $lastNumber = 0;

    if ($latest && $latest->deposit_no) {
        $lastNumber = (int) substr(
            (string) $latest->deposit_no,
            -5
        );
    }

    $nextNumber = $lastNumber + 1;

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Return standard Deposit No.
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Example:
    | DEP-202609-00049
    |
    */

    return $prefix . str_pad(
        (string) $nextNumber,
        5,
        '0',
        STR_PAD_LEFT
    );
}
// ============================================================
// GENERATE MR ID
// ============================================================

public function generateMrId(): string
{
    $year =
        $this->year
        ?? date('Y');

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Month
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    if (!empty($this->month)) {

        try {

            $monthNum = Carbon::parse(
                $this->month
            )->format('m');

        } catch (\Throwable $e) {

            $monthNum = date('m');
        }

    } else {

        $monthNum = date('m');
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Prefix
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Example:
    | MR-202608-
    | MR-202609-
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    $prefix =
        'MR-' .
        $year .
        $monthNum .
        '-';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Get latest serial from CURRENT YEAR only
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | August:
    | MR-202608-00001
    | MR-202608-00002
    |
    | September:
    | MR-202609-00003
    |
    | New Year:
    | MR-202701-00001
    |
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    $latest = self::withTrashed()
        ->where(
            'mr_id',
            'LIKE',
            'MR-' . $year . '%'
        )
        ->orderByRaw(
            'CAST(
                SUBSTRING_INDEX(
                    mr_id,
                    "-",
                    -1
                )
                AS UNSIGNED
            ) DESC'
        )
        ->first();

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Generate next serial
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    if (
        $latest &&
        $latest->mr_id
    ) {

        $lastNum = intval(
            substr(
                $latest->mr_id,
                -5
            )
        );

        $nextNum = str_pad(
            $lastNum + 1,
            5,
            '0',
            STR_PAD_LEFT
        );

    } else {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | New Year / First MR
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $nextNum = '00001';
    }

    return $prefix . $nextNum;
}

    // ============================================================
    // STATS
    // ============================================================

    public static function getStats(): array
    {
        return [

            'total' =>
                self::count(),

            'pending' =>
                self::pending()->count(),

            'approved' =>
                self::approved()->count(),

            'rejected' =>
                self::rejected()->count(),

            'total_amount' =>
                (float) (
                    self::approved()
                        ->sum('amount')
                    ?? 0
                ),

            'pending_amount' =>
                (float) (
                    self::pending()
                        ->sum('amount')
                    ?? 0
                ),
        ];
    }

// ============================================================
// APPROVE
// ============================================================

/**
 * Approve Deposit
 *
 * Financial rule:
 *
 * Total Received = amount
 * Member Fund    = amount - fine_amount - others_amount
 *
 * Example:
 *
 * amount        = 10,000
 * fine          = 500
 * others        = 500
 *
 * Member Fund   = 9,000
 */
public function approve($adminId = null): bool
{
    if ($this->status !== self::STATUS_PENDING) {
        return false;
    }

    return DB::transaction(function () use ($adminId) {

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | LOCK DEPOSIT
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $deposit = static::query()
            ->where('id', $this->id)
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->first();

        if (!$deposit) {
            return false;
        }

        if ($deposit->status !== self::STATUS_PENDING) {
            return false;
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | LOCK MEMBER
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $member = Member::query()
            ->where('id', $deposit->member_id)
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->first();

        if (!$member) {
            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Member not found for this deposit.'
            );
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | AMOUNT VALIDATION
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $totalReceived = round(
            (float) $deposit->amount,
            2
        );

        $fineAmount = round(
            (float) ($deposit->fine_amount ?? 0),
            2
        );

        $othersAmount = round(
            (float) ($deposit->others_amount ?? 0),
            2
        );


        if ($totalReceived <= 0) {
            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Deposit amount must be greater than zero.'
            );
        }


        if ($fineAmount < 0) {
            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Fine amount cannot be negative.'
            );
        }


        if ($othersAmount < 0) {
            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Others amount cannot be negative.'
            );
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | MEMBER FUND
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | Member Fund = Total Received - Fine - Others
        |
        */

        $memberFund = round(
            $totalReceived
                - $fineAmount
                - $othersAmount,
            2
        );


        if ($memberFund < 0) {
            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Fine and Others amount cannot be greater than total deposit amount.'
            );
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | APPROVE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $deposit->status =
            self::STATUS_APPROVED;

        $deposit->approved_at =
            now();

        if ($adminId) {
            $deposit->approved_by =
                $adminId;
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | GENERATE MR ID
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        if (empty($deposit->mr_id)) {
            $deposit->mr_id =
                $deposit->generateMrId();
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | SAVE APPROVED DEPOSIT
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $saved = $deposit->save();

        if (!$saved) {
            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Failed to approve deposit.'
            );
        }


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | FINANCIAL ALLOCATION
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | This handles:
        |
        | - Member Fund / Fee allocation
        | - Fine allocation
        | - Others
        | - Advance
        | - FIFO
        | - Financial allocation persistence
        |
        */

        $financialService =
            app(
                \App\Services\MemberFinancialService::class
            );

        $financialService->allocateApprovedDeposit(
            $deposit
        );


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | UPDATE MEMBER FUND
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | IMPORTANT:
        |
        | Do NOT add full deposit amount.
        |
        | Member Fund = amount - fine - others
        |
        */

        $member->total_deposit =
            round(
                (float) $member->total_deposit
                + $memberFund,
                2
            );

        $member->save();


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | CLEAR MEMBER DUE FIFO CACHE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        Cache::forget(
            'member_due_fifo_' . $deposit->member_id
        );


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | POST ACCOUNTING
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | Accounting will post:
        |
        | Dr Cash/Bank       = Total Received
        | Cr Member Fund     = Member Fund
        | Cr Fine Income     = Fine
        | Cr Others Income   = Others
        |
        */

        app(DepositAccountingService::class)
            ->postApprovedDeposit(
                $deposit,
                $adminId
            );


        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | REFRESH CURRENT MODEL
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $this->refresh();


        return true;
    });
}

    // ============================================================
    // REJECT
    // ============================================================

    public function reject(
        $adminId = null,
        string $reason = null
    ): bool {

        if (
            $this->status !==
            self::STATUS_PENDING
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        $this->status =
            self::STATUS_REJECTED;

        $this->rejected_at =
            now();

        if ($adminId) {

            $this->rejected_by =
                $adminId;
        }

        if ($reason !== null) {

            $this->rejection_reason =
                trim($reason);
        }

        return $this->save();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // BOOT
    // ============================================================

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | CREATING
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::creating(
            function ($deposit) {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                | Deposit Number
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    empty(
                        $deposit->deposit_no
                    )
                ) {

                    $deposit->deposit_no =
                        self::generateDepositNo();
                }

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                | Year / Month
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    !empty(
                        $deposit->deposit_date
                    )
                ) {

                    $date = Carbon::parse(
                        $deposit->deposit_date
                    );

                    if (
                        empty(
                            $deposit->year
                        )
                    ) {

                        $deposit->year =
                            $date->format('Y');
                    }

                    if (
                        empty(
                            $deposit->month
                        )
                    ) {

                        $deposit->month =
                            $date->format('F');
                    }
                }

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                | Default Status
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    empty(
                        $deposit->status
                    )
                ) {

                    $deposit->status =
                        self::STATUS_PENDING;
                }

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                | Created By
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    !array_key_exists(
                        'created_by',
                        $deposit->attributes
                    )
                    ||
                    is_null(
                        $deposit->created_by
                    )
                ) {

                    if (
                        auth('admin')->check()
                    ) {

                        $deposit->created_by =
                            auth('admin')->id();

                    } else {

                        $deposit->created_by =
                            null;
                    }
                }
            }
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | UPDATING
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::updating(
            function ($deposit) {

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                | Updated By
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    auth('admin')->check()
                ) {

                    $deposit->updated_by =
                        auth('admin')->id();
                }

                /*
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                | Clear Due FIFO Cache
                |--------------------------------------------------------------
                */

                if (
                    $deposit->isDirty(
                        [
                            'status',
                            'amount',
                            'member_id',
                        ]
                    )
                ) {

                    $oldMemberId =
                        $deposit->getOriginal(
                            'member_id'
                        );

                    $newMemberId =
                        $deposit->member_id;

                    /*
                    |----------------------------------------------------------
                    | Old Member
                    |----------------------------------------------------------
                    */

                    if ($oldMemberId) {

                        Cache::forget(
                            'member_due_fifo_' .
                            $oldMemberId
                        );
                    }

                    /*
                    |----------------------------------------------------------
                    | New Member
                    |----------------------------------------------------------
                    */

                    if (
                        $newMemberId
                        &&
                        $newMemberId !=
                        $oldMemberId
                    ) {

                        Cache::forget(
                            'member_due_fifo_' .
                            $newMemberId
                        );

                    } elseif ($newMemberId) {

                        Cache::forget(
                            'member_due_fifo_' .
                            $newMemberId
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | CREATED
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::created(
            function ($deposit) {

                if (
                    $deposit->member_id
                ) {

                    Cache::forget(
                        'member_due_fifo_' .
                        $deposit->member_id
                    );
                }
            }
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | DELETED
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::deleted(
            function ($deposit) {

                if (
                    $deposit->member_id
                ) {

                    Cache::forget(
                        'member_due_fifo_' .
                        $deposit->member_id
                    );
                }
            }
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | RESTORED
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::restored(
            function ($deposit) {

                if (
                    $deposit->member_id
                ) {

                    Cache::forget(
                        'member_due_fifo_' .
                        $deposit->member_id
                    );
                }
            }
        );
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class Expense extends Model
{
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'expenses';

    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    // ============================================================
    // MASS ASSIGNMENT
    // ============================================================

    protected $fillable = [
        'expense_no',
        'project_id',
        'category',
        'amount',
        'expense_date',

        // Payment
        'payment_method',
        'to_bank_id',

        // Document / Remarks
        'attachment',
        'remarks',

        // Audit
        'created_by',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // CASTS
    // ============================================================

    protected $casts = [
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'expense_date' => 'date',

        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        'deleted_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // EXPENSE CATEGORIES
    // ============================================================

    public const CATEGORY_OFFICE = 'Office';

    public const CATEGORY_PROJECT = 'Project';

    public const CATEGORY_OTHERS = 'Others';

    public const CATEGORIES = [
        self::CATEGORY_OFFICE,
        self::CATEGORY_PROJECT,
        self::CATEGORY_OTHERS,
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // PAYMENT METHODS
    // ============================================================

    public const PAYMENT_CASH = 'cash';

    public const PAYMENT_BANK = 'bank';

    public const PAYMENT_METHODS = [
        self::PAYMENT_CASH => 'Cash',
        self::PAYMENT_BANK => 'Bank',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================================

    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Project::class,
            'project_id'
        );
    }

    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'created_by'
        );
    }

    public function bankAccount(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            BankAccount::class,
            'to_bank_id'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Legacy transaction relationship.
     */
    public function transactions(): MorphMany
    {
        return $this->morphMany(
            Transaction::class,
            'transactionable'
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SCOPES
    // ============================================================

    public function scopeByCategory($query, $category)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'category',
            $category
        );
    }

    public function scopeByDateRange($query, $from, $to)
    {
        return $query->whereBetween(
            'expense_date',
            [$from, $to]
        );
    }

    public function scopeByPaymentMethod($query, $paymentMethod)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'payment_method',
            $paymentMethod
        );
    }

    public function scopeByProject($query, $projectId)
    {
        return $query->where(
            'project_id',
            $projectId
        );
    }

    public function scopeSearch($query, $term)
    {
        $term = trim((string) $term);

        if ($term === '') {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($term) {

            $q->where(
                'expense_no',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            );

            $q->orWhere(
                'remarks',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            );
        });
    }

    // ============================================================
    // EXPENSE NUMBER
    // ============================================================

public static function generateExpenseNo(): string
{
    $latest = self::withTrashed()
        ->whereNotNull('expense_no')
        ->where('expense_no', 'like', 'EXP-%')
        ->orderByDesc('id')
        ->first();

    if (!$latest) {
        return 'EXP-000001';
    }

    $lastNumber = (int) preg_replace(
        '/[^0-9]/',
        '',
        (string) $latest->expense_no
    );

    $nextNumber = $lastNumber + 1;

    return 'EXP-' . str_pad(
        (string) $nextNumber,
        6,
        '0',
        STR_PAD_LEFT
    );
}

    // ============================================================
    // MODEL BOOT
    // ============================================================

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function ($expense) {

            if (empty($expense->expense_no)) {
                $expense->expense_no =
                    self::generateExpenseNo();
            }
        });
    }

    // ============================================================
    // PAYMENT HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    public function isCashPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::PAYMENT_CASH;
    }

    public function isBankPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::PAYMENT_BANK;
    }

    public function requiresBankAccount(): bool
    {
        return $this->isBankPayment();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ACCESSORS
    // ============================================================

    public function getFormattedAmountAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->amount,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getCategoryLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        $labels = [
            self::CATEGORY_OFFICE => '🏢 Office',
            self::CATEGORY_PROJECT => '🏗️ Project',
            self::CATEGORY_OTHERS => '📦 Others',
        ];

        return $labels[$this->category]
            ?? $this->category;
    }

    public function getPaymentMethodLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return self::PAYMENT_METHODS[
            $this->payment_method
        ] ?? ucfirst(
            (string) $this->payment_method
        );
    }

    public function getAttachmentUrlAttribute(): ?string
    {
        if (empty($this->attachment)) {
            return null;
        }

        return asset(
            'storage/' . ltrim(
                $this->attachment,
                '/'
            )
        );
    }

    public function getExpenseDateFormattedAttribute(): ?string
    {
        return $this->expense_date
            ? $this->expense_date->format('d M Y')
            : null;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // STATIC HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    public static function categories(): array
    {
        return self::CATEGORIES;
    }

    public static function paymentMethods(): array
    {
        return self::PAYMENT_METHODS;
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\ImportBatch.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class ImportBatch extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;

    protected $table = 'import_batches';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const STATUS_PENDING    = 'pending';
    public const STATUS_PROCESSING = 'processing';
    public const STATUS_COMPLETED  = 'completed';
    public const STATUS_FAILED     = 'failed';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Import Types
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_EXCEL = 'excel';
    public const TYPE_CSV   = 'csv';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Mass Assignment
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $fillable = [
        'batch_no',
        'module',
        'import_type',

        'file_name',
        'file_path',

        'total_rows',
        'processed_rows',
        'successful_rows',
        'failed_rows',
        'skipped_rows',

        'status',
        'error_data',
        'remarks',

        'started_at',
        'completed_at',

        'created_by',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Casts
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $casts = [
        'total_rows'      => 'integer',
        'processed_rows'  => 'integer',
        'successful_rows' => 'integer',
        'failed_rows'     => 'integer',
        'skipped_rows'    => 'integer',

        'error_data' => 'array',

        'started_at'   => 'datetime',
        'completed_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Creator
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'created_by'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Progress
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getProgressAttribute(): int
    {
        $total = (int) $this->total_rows;

        if ($total <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }

        return min(
            100,
            (int) round(
                ($this->processed_rows / $total) * 100
            )
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Success Rate
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getSuccessRateAttribute(): float
    {
        $total = (int) $this->total_rows;

        if ($total <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }

        return round(
            ($this->successful_rows / $total) * 100,
            2
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Error Count
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getErrorCountAttribute(): int
    {
        if (is_array($this->error_data)) {
            return count($this->error_data);
        }

        return (int) $this->failed_rows;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status Label
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->status) {
            self::STATUS_PENDING =>
                'Pending',

            self::STATUS_PROCESSING =>
                'Processing',

            self::STATUS_COMPLETED =>
                'Completed',

            self::STATUS_FAILED =>
                'Failed',

            default =>
                ucfirst(
                    str_replace(
                        '_',
                        ' ',
                        (string) $this->status
                    )
                ),
        };
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Finished
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getIsFinishedAttribute(): bool
    {
        return in_array(
            $this->status,
            [
                self::STATUS_COMPLETED,
                self::STATUS_FAILED,
            ],
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeMembers(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'module',
            'members'
        );
    }

    public function scopePending(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            self::STATUS_PENDING
        );
    }

    public function scopeProcessing(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            self::STATUS_PROCESSING
        );
    }

    public function scopeCompleted(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            self::STATUS_COMPLETED
        );
    }

    public function scopeFailed(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            self::STATUS_FAILED
        );
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;

class Investment extends Model
{
    use HasRecordUuid, HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'investments';

    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    protected $fillable = [
        // Identification
        'investment_no',

        // Project & Assignment
        'project_id',
        'member_id',           // Assigned Member
        'responsible_person',

        // Investment Details
        'investment_category',
        'description',
        'amount',
        'investment_date',

        // Payment Information
        'reference_no',
        'paid_to',
        'payment_method',
        'to_bank_id',

        // Approval
        'is_approved',
        'approved_at',
        'approved_by',

        // Additional Information
        'remarks',
        'document_file',

        // Audit
        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',

        'investment_date' => 'date',

        'is_approved' => 'boolean',
        'approved_at' => 'datetime',

        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        'deleted_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // CONSTANTS
    // ============================================================

    public const CATEGORY_LAND_PURCHASE = 'land_purchase';
    public const CATEGORY_REGISTRATION = 'registration';
    public const CATEGORY_DEED = 'deed';
    public const CATEGORY_MUTATION = 'mutation';
    public const CATEGORY_TAX = 'tax';
    public const CATEGORY_SURVEY = 'survey';
    public const CATEGORY_DEVELOPMENT = 'development';
    public const CATEGORY_LEGAL = 'legal';
    public const CATEGORY_DOCUMENTATION = 'documentation';
    public const CATEGORY_TRANSPORT = 'transport';
    public const CATEGORY_UTILITY = 'utility';
    public const CATEGORY_OTHER = 'other';

    public const PAYMENT_CASH = 'cash';
    public const PAYMENT_BANK = 'bank';

    // ============================================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================================

    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Project::class);
    }

    /**
     * Assigned Member
     *
     * This is NOT the investor.
     */
    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class);
    }

    /**
     * Responsible Person
     */
    public function responsiblePerson(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class, 'responsible_person');
    }

    public function bankAccount(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(BankAccount::class, 'to_bank_id');
    }

    public function approvedBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'approved_by');
    }

    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'created_by');
    }

    public function updater(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'updated_by');
    }

    /**
     * Legacy generic transaction relation.
     *
     * Accounting integration will be handled separately.
     */
    public function transactions(): MorphMany
    {
        return $this->morphMany(Transaction::class, 'transactionable');
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SCOPES
    // ============================================================

    public function scopeByProject($query, $projectId)
    {
        return $query->where('project_id', $projectId);
    }

    public function scopeByMember($query, $memberId)
    {
        return $query->where('member_id', $memberId);
    }

    public function scopeByCategory($query, string $category)
    {
        return $query->where('investment_category', $category);
    }

    public function scopeByPaymentMethod($query, string $method)
    {
        return $query->where('payment_method', $method);
    }

    public function scopeApproved($query)
    {
        return $query->where('is_approved', true);
    }

    public function scopeSearch($query, string $search)
    {
        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
            $q->where('investment_no', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('reference_no', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('paid_to', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('description', 'like', "%{$search}%");
        });
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ACCESSORS
    // ============================================================

    protected function amountFormatted(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => '৳ ' . number_format(
                (float) $this->amount,
                2
            )
        );
    }

    protected function categoryLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => self::categoryLabelFor(
                $this->investment_category
            )
        );
    }

    protected function paymentMethodLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => match ($this->payment_method) {
                self::PAYMENT_CASH => 'Cash',
                self::PAYMENT_BANK => 'Bank',
                default => ucfirst((string) $this->payment_method),
            }
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // CATEGORY HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    public static function categories(): array
    {
        return [
            self::CATEGORY_LAND_PURCHASE => 'Land Purchase',
            self::CATEGORY_REGISTRATION => 'Registration',
            self::CATEGORY_DEED => 'Deed',
            self::CATEGORY_MUTATION => 'Mutation',
            self::CATEGORY_TAX => 'Tax',
            self::CATEGORY_SURVEY => 'Survey',
            self::CATEGORY_DEVELOPMENT => 'Development',
            self::CATEGORY_LEGAL => 'Legal',
            self::CATEGORY_DOCUMENTATION => 'Documentation',
            self::CATEGORY_TRANSPORT => 'Transport',
            self::CATEGORY_UTILITY => 'Utility',
            self::CATEGORY_OTHER => 'Other',
        ];
    }

    public static function categoryLabelFor(?string $category): string
    {
        return self::categories()[$category] ?? ucfirst(
            str_replace('_', ' ', (string) $category)
        );
    }

    public static function paymentMethods(): array
    {
        return [
            self::PAYMENT_CASH => 'Cash',
            self::PAYMENT_BANK => 'Bank',
        ];
    }

    // ============================================================
    // BUSINESS HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    public static function generateInvestmentNo(): string
    {
        $year = date('Y');

        $last = static::where(
            'investment_no',
            'like',
            "INV-{$year}-%"
        )
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        if (!$last) {
            return "INV-{$year}-00001";
        }

        $parts = explode('-', $last->investment_no);

        $lastNumber = isset($parts[2])
            ? (int) $parts[2]
            : 0;

        return sprintf(
            'INV-%s-%05d',
            $year,
            $lastNumber + 1
        );
    }

    public function approve(int $adminId): void
    {
        $this->update([
            'is_approved' => true,
            'approved_at' => now(),
            'approved_by' => $adminId,
        ]);
    }

    public function isApproved(): bool
    {
        return (bool) $this->is_approved;
    }

    public function isCashPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::PAYMENT_CASH;
    }

    public function isBankPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::PAYMENT_BANK;
    }

    public function hasDocument(): bool
    {
        return !empty($this->document_file);
    }

    /**
     * Every Investment entry contributes its amount
     * to the Project's total investment.
     */
    public function projectCostContribution(): float
    {
        return (float) $this->amount;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SUMMARY
    // ============================================================

    public function summary(): array
    {
        return [
            'investment_no' => $this->investment_no,
            'project_id' => $this->project_id,
            'member_id' => $this->member_id,
            'category' => $this->categoryLabel,
            'amount' => (float) $this->amount,
            'investment_date' => $this->investment_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'payment_method' => $this->paymentMethodLabel,
            'paid_to' => $this->paid_to,
            'is_approved' => $this->is_approved,
        ];
    }

    // ============================================================
    // MODEL EVENTS
    // ============================================================

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (Investment $investment) {
            if (empty($investment->investment_no)) {
                $investment->investment_no =
                    self::generateInvestmentNo();
            }

            if (empty($investment->investment_date)) {
                $investment->investment_date = now()->toDateString();
            }

            if (empty($investment->payment_method)) {
                $investment->payment_method =
                    self::PAYMENT_CASH;
            }

            if ($investment->is_approved === null) {
                $investment->is_approved = false;
            }
        });
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * LOGIN ATTEMPT
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Authentication-level event.
 *
 * LoginAttempt != LoginHistory
 *
 * LoginAttempt:
 *     Represents one authentication attempt.
 *
 * LoginHistory:
 *     Represents one authenticated session lifecycle.
 */
class LoginAttempt extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'login_attempts';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Mass Assignment
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'guard',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'login_identifier',

        'successful',
        'status',
        'failure_reason',

        'ip_address',
        'user_agent',

        'request_id',

        'device_type',
        'browser',
        'platform',

        'attempted_at',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Hidden
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $hidden = [
        'login_identifier',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Casts
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'successful'   => 'boolean',
            'attempted_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeForGuard(
        Builder $query,
        string $guard
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'guard',
            $guard
        );
    }

    public function scopeSuccessful(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'success'
        );
    }

    public function scopeFailed(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereIn(
            'status',
            [
                'failed',
                'invalid',
                'error',
            ]
        );
    }

    public function scopeLocked(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'locked'
        );
    }

    public function scopeBlocked(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'blocked'
        );
    }

    public function scopeRecent(
        Builder $query,
        int $days = 30
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'attempted_at',
            '>=',
            now()->subDays(
                max(1, $days)
            )
        );
    }

    public function scopeFromIp(
        Builder $query,
        string $ip
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'ip_address',
            $ip
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isSuccessful(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'success';
    }

    public function isFailed(): bool
    {
        return in_array(
            strtolower(
                (string) $this->status
            ),
            [
                'failed',
                'invalid',
                'error',
            ],
            true
        );
    }

    public function isLocked(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'locked';
    }

    public function isBlocked(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'blocked';
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * LOGIN HISTORY
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Tracks the lifecycle of an authenticated session.
 *
 * LoginAttempt:
 *     "Was authentication successful?"
 *
 * LoginHistory:
 *     "What happened to the authenticated session?"
 */
class LoginHistory extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'login_histories';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'login_at',
        'logout_at',

        'ip_address',
        'user_agent',

        'browser',
        'operating_system',
        'device_type',
        'device_name',

        'status',
        'failure_reason',

        'session_id',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'login_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'logout_at' =>
                'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeActive(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNull(
            'logout_at'
        )->where(
            'status',
            'active'
        );
    }

    public function scopeCompleted(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNotNull(
            'logout_at'
        );
    }

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    public function scopeForSession(
        Builder $query,
        string $sessionId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'session_id',
            $sessionId
        );
    }

    public function scopeRecent(
        Builder $query,
        int $days = 30
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'login_at',
            '>=',
            now()->subDays(max(1, $days))
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isActive(): bool
    {
        return $this->logout_at === null
            && strtolower(
                (string) $this->status
            ) === 'active';
    }

    public function isLoggedOut(): bool
    {
        return $this->logout_at !== null;
    }

    public function markLoggedOut(
        ?string $status = 'logged_out',
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {

        $this->logout_at = $this->logout_at
            ?? now();

        $this->status = $status;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function durationInSeconds(): int
    {
        if (!$this->login_at) {
            return 0;
        }

        $end = $this->logout_at ?? now();

        return max(
            0,
            (int) $this->login_at->diffInSeconds($end)
        );
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Carbon\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use App\Traits\HasNotifications;

class Member extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasFactory, Notifiable, SoftDeletes;

        'monthly_fee' => 'decimal:2',
        'fine_rate' => 'decimal:2',

        // Shares
        'shares' => 'integer',
    ];

    /*

    public const TYPE_REFERENCE = 'REFERENCE';

    public const STATUS_PENDING  = 'pending';
    public const STATUS_ACTIVE   = 'active';
    public const STATUS_INACTIVE = 'inactive';
    public const STATUS_CLOSED   = 'closed';
    public const STATUS_RESIGNED = 'resigned';

    /*

    | MEMBERSHIP TYPE CONSTANTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const MEMBERSHIP_GENERAL   = 'general';
    public const MEMBERSHIP_PREMIUM   = 'premium';
    public const MEMBERSHIP_DIRECTOR  = 'director';
    public const MEMBERSHIP_INVESTOR  = 'investor';

    /*

    public const GENDER_FEMALE = 'female';
    public const GENDER_OTHER  = 'other';

    */

     */
    public function approvedBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'approved_by');
    }

    /**

     */
    public function nominees(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(MemberNominee::class);
    }

    /**

     */
    public function deposits(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Deposit::class);
    }

    /**
