     */
    public function approvedDeposits(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->deposits()
            ->where('status', Deposit::STATUS_APPROVED);
    }

    /**
     * Pending deposits only.
     */
    public function pendingDeposits(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->deposits()
            ->where('status', Deposit::STATUS_PENDING);
    }

    /**
     * Member investments.
     */
    public function investments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Investment::class);
    }

    /**
     * Approved investments only.
     */
    public function approvedInvestments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->investments()
            ->where('is_approved', true);
    }

    /**
     * Member shares.
     */
    public function shares(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Share::class);
    }

    /**
     * Active shares only.
     */
    public function activeShares(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->shares()
            ->where('status', Share::STATUS_ACTIVE);
    }

    /**
     * Member profits.
     */
    public function profits(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Profit::class);
    }

    /**
     * Profit distributions.
     */
    public function profitDistributions(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(ProfitDistribution::class);
    }

    /**
     * Member resignations.
     */
    public function resignations(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Resignation::class);
    }

/**
 * All member notifications.
 *
 * Notifications table does NOT have member_id.
 *
 * New structure:
 * owner_type = member
 * owner_id   = members.id
 */
public function notifications(): HasMany
{
    return $this->hasMany(
        Notification::class,
        'owner_id',
        'id'
    )->where(
        'owner_type',
        Notification::USER_MEMBER
    );
}


/**
 * Unread member notifications.
 *
 * A notification is considered unread when:
 *
 * is_read = false
 * OR
 * read_at IS NULL
 */
public function unreadNotifications(): HasMany
{
    return $this->notifications()
        ->where(function ($query) {

            $query
                ->where('is_read', false)
                ->orWhereNull('read_at');

        });
}

    /**
     * Sales where member is the buyer.
     */
    public function sales(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Sale::class, 'buyer_id');
    }

    /**
     * Member transactions.
     */
    public function transactions(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Transaction::class);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | QUERY SCOPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Active and approved members.
     */
    public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query
            ->where('status', self::STATUS_ACTIVE)
            ->where('approved', true);
    }

    /**
     * Pending members.
     */
    public function scopePending($query)
    {
        return $query
            ->where('status', self::STATUS_PENDING)
            ->where('approved', false);
    }

    /**
     * Members having due amount.
     */
    public function scopeHasDue($query)
    {
        return $query->where('due_amount', '>', 0);
    }

    /**
     * Search members.
     */
    public function scopeSearch($query, string $search)
    {
        $search = trim($search);

        if ($search === '') {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {

            $q->where('member_code', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('mobile', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('email', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('nid', 'like', "%{$search}%");

        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACCESSORS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Member name with Member ID.
     *
     * Example:
     * Shamim (TFM-101)
     */
    protected function displayName(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => trim(
                ($this->name ?? '') .
                (
                    $this->member_code
                        ? " ({$this->member_code})"
                        : ''
                )
            )
        );
    }

    /**
     * Human-readable member status.
     */
    protected function statusLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {

                $labels = [
                    self::STATUS_PENDING  => 'â³ Pending',
                    self::STATUS_ACTIVE   => 'âœ… Active',
                    self::STATUS_INACTIVE => 'â¸ï¸ Inactive',
                    self::STATUS_CLOSED   => 'ðŸ”’ Closed',
                    self::STATUS_RESIGNED => 'ðŸš« Resigned',
                ];

                return $labels[$this->status]
                    ?? ucfirst((string) $this->status);
            }
        );
    }

    /**
     * Member age.
     */
    protected function age(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => $this->date_of_birth?->age
        );
    }

    /**
     * Total approved investment.
     */
    protected function totalInvestment(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => (float) $this->approvedInvestments()->sum('amount')
        );
    }

    /**
     * Total active share quantity.
     */
    protected function totalShareQuantity(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => (int) $this->activeShares()->sum('quantity')
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MEMBER CODE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Supported:
    |
    | TFM-101
    | TRM-102
    |
    | Number sequence is GLOBAL between TFM and TRM.
    |
    */

    /**
     * Generate the next Member ID.
     */
    public static function generateMemberCode(string $type): string
    {
        $type = strtoupper(trim($type));

        $prefix = match ($type) {
            self::TYPE_FOUNDER   => 'TFM',
            self::TYPE_REFERENCE => 'TRM',
            default => throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                "Invalid member type: {$type}"
            ),
        };

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Find highest existing number
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $members = static::withTrashed()
            ->where(function ($query) {
                $query->where('member_code', 'like', 'TFM-%')
                    ->orWhere('member_code', 'like', 'TRM-%');
            })
            ->get(['member_code']);

        $maxNumber = 0;

        foreach ($members as $member) {

            if (
                preg_match(
                    '/^(TFM|TRM)-(\d+)$/i',
                    trim((string) $member->member_code),
                    $matches
                )
            ) {
                $number = (int) $matches[2];

                if ($number > $maxNumber) {
                    $maxNumber = $number;
                }
            }
        }

        $nextNumber = $maxNumber + 1;

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Prevent duplicate ID
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        do {

            $code = $prefix . '-' . str_pad(
                $nextNumber,
                3,
                '0',
                STR_PAD_LEFT
            );

            $exists = static::withTrashed()
                ->where('member_code', $code)
                ->exists();

            if ($exists) {
                $nextNumber++;
            }

        } while ($exists);

        return $code;
    }

    /**
     * Validate Member ID.
     */
    public static function isValidMemberCode(?string $code): bool
    {
        if (!$code) {
            return false;
        }

        return preg_match(
            '/^(TFM|TRM)-\d+$/i',
            trim($code)
        ) === 1;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PASSWORD HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Generate default member password.
     */
    public static function generateDefaultPassword(
        ?string $memberCode = null
    ): string {

        if (!$memberCode) {
            return 'TPS@' . now()->format('YmdHis');
        }

        return 'TPS@' . strtoupper(trim($memberCode));
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether password is already hashed.
     */
    protected static function isHashedPassword(
        ?string $password
    ): bool {

        if (!$password) {
            return false;
        }

        return str_starts_with($password, '$2y$')
            || str_starts_with($password, '$2a$')
            || str_starts_with($password, '$2b$');
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MODEL BOOT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | CREATING
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::creating(function (Member $member) {

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | MEMBER CODE
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if (!empty($member->member_code)) {

                $member->member_code = strtoupper(
                    trim($member->member_code)
                );

                // Validate manually supplied Member ID
                if (!self::isValidMemberCode($member->member_code)) {

                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                        "Invalid Member ID: {$member->member_code}. " .
                        "Use TFM-XXX or TRM-XXX."
                    );
                }

                // Prevent duplicate Member ID
                $exists = static::withTrashed()
                    ->where('member_code', $member->member_code)
                    ->exists();

                if ($exists) {

                    throw new \RuntimeException(
                        "Member ID already exists: {$member->member_code}"
                    );
                }

            } else {

                // Generate Member ID automatically
                $member->member_code = static::generateMemberCode(
                    $member->member_type ?: self::TYPE_REFERENCE
                );
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | PASSWORD
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if (empty($member->password)) {

                $member->password = Hash::make(
                    static::generateDefaultPassword(
                        $member->member_code
                    )
                );

            } elseif (
                !self::isHashedPassword($member->password)
            ) {

                $member->password = Hash::make(
                    $member->password
                );
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | DEFAULT STATUS
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if (is_null($member->status)) {
                $member->status = self::STATUS_PENDING;
            }

            if (is_null($member->approved)) {
                $member->approved = false;
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | FINANCIAL DEFAULTS
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            $financialDefaults = [
                'shares'              => 0,
                'advance_balance'    => 0,
                'total_deposit'      => 0,
                'total_profit'       => 0,
                'total_profit_earned'=> 0,
                'total_withdraw'     => 0,
                'due_amount'         => 0,
                'monthly_fee'        => 0,
                'fine_rate'          => 0,
            ];

            foreach ($financialDefaults as $field => $value) {

                if (is_null($member->{$field})) {
                    $member->{$field} = $value;
                }
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | FIRST LOGIN SETUP
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if (is_null($member->profile_updated_once)) {
                $member->profile_updated_once = false;
            }
        });

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | UPDATING
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::updating(function (Member $member) {

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | MEMBER CODE
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if ($member->isDirty('member_code')) {

                if (empty($member->member_code)) {

                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                        'Member ID cannot be empty.'
                    );
                }

                $member->member_code = strtoupper(
                    trim($member->member_code)
                );

                if (!self::isValidMemberCode($member->member_code)) {

                    throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                        "Invalid Member ID: {$member->member_code}. " .
                        "Use TFM-XXX or TRM-XXX."
                    );
                }

                $exists = static::withTrashed()
                    ->where('member_code', $member->member_code)
                    ->whereKeyNot($member->id)
                    ->exists();

                if ($exists) {

                    throw new \RuntimeException(
                        "Member ID already exists: {$member->member_code}"
                    );
                }
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | PASSWORD
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Prevent double hashing.
            |
            */

            if (
                $member->isDirty('password') &&
                !empty($member->password) &&
                !self::isHashedPassword($member->password)
            ) {

                $member->password = Hash::make(
                    $member->password
                );
            }
        });

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | DELETING
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        static::deleting(function (Member $member) {

            if ($member->isForceDeleting()) {
                $member->nominees()->forceDelete();
            }
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MEMBER APPROVAL
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Approve member.
     */
    public function approve(?int $adminId = null): void
    {
        $this->update([
            'approved'    => true,
            'approved_at' => now(),
            'approved_by' => $adminId ?? auth('admin')->id(),
            'status'      => self::STATUS_ACTIVE,
        ]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FIRST LOGIN / PROFILE SETUP
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Determine whether member must complete first-login setup.
     */
    public function requiresFirstLoginSetup(): bool
    {
        return !$this->profile_updated_once;
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether member can update profile.
     */
    public function canUpdateProfile(): bool
    {
        return !$this->profile_updated_once;
    }

    /**
     * Mark first-login profile setup as completed.
     */
    public function markProfileUpdated(): void
    {
        $this->forceFill([
            'profile_updated_once' => true,
        ])->save();
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether first-login setup is completed.
     */
    public function hasCompletedFirstLoginSetup(): bool
    {
        return (bool) $this->profile_updated_once;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FINANCIAL BALANCE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Calculate member balance.
     *
     * Balance =
     * Total Deposit
     * - Total Withdraw
     * + Total Profit Earned
     */
    public function getBalance(): float
    {
        return (float) (
            ($this->total_deposit ?? 0)
            - ($this->total_withdraw ?? 0)
            + ($this->total_profit_earned ?? 0)
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PRIMARY NOMINEE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Get primary nominee.
     */
    public function getPrimaryNominee(): ?MemberNominee
    {
        return $this->nominees()
            ->orderByDesc('percentage')
            ->first();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | OPENING DATE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Get system opening date.
     */
    protected function getOpeningDate(): Carbon
    {
        $openingDate = Setting::getOpeningDate();

        return Carbon::parse($openingDate)
            ->startOfMonth();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DUE CALCULATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Calculate total due.
     */
    public function calculateDue(): float
    {
        $opening = $this->getOpeningDate();

        $current = now()->startOfMonth();

        if ($opening->greaterThan($current)) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        $months = $opening->diffInMonths($current) + 1;

        $monthlyFee = (float) ($this->monthly_fee ?? 0);

        $totalFees = $months * $monthlyFee;

        $paid = (float) $this->approvedDeposits()->sum('amount');

        return max(
            0,
            $totalFees - $paid
        );
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether member has pending due.
     */
    public function hasPendingDue(): bool
    {
        return $this->calculateDue() > 0;
    }

    /**
     * Get current month due.
     */
    public function getCurrentMonthDue(): float
    {
        $opening = $this->getOpeningDate();

        $current = now()->startOfMonth();

        if ($opening->greaterThan($current)) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return (float) ($this->monthly_fee ?? 0);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FINE CALCULATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Calculate fine against due.
     */
    public function calculateFine(?float $due = null): float
    {
        $due ??= $this->calculateDue();

        if ($due <= 0) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        $fineRate = (float) ($this->fine_rate ?? 0);

        if ($fineRate <= 0) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        $opening = $this->getOpeningDate();

        $current = now()->startOfMonth();

        if ($opening->greaterThan($current)) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        $months = $opening->diffInMonths($current) + 1;

        return max(
            0,
            ($due * $fineRate / 100) * $months
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TOTAL DUE WITH FINE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Get due + fine + total payable.
     */
    public function getTotalDueWithFine(): array
    {
        $due = $this->calculateDue();

        $fine = $this->calculateFine($due);

        return [
            'due' => round($due, 2),
            'fine' => round($fine, 2),
            'total' => round($due + $fine, 2),
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

class MemberReminderSetting extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'monthly_payment_reminder_settings';

    protected $fillable = [
        'enabled',
        'frequency',
        'reminder_dates',
        'message',
        'message_options',
        'timezone',
        'duplicate_reset_at',
    ];

public const DEFAULT_MESSAGE =
    'প্রিয় {member_name}, আপনার {month} মাসের monthly payment বকেয়া রয়েছে। অনুগ্রহ করে নির্ধারিত সময়ের মধ্যে payment জমা দিন। যদি ইতোমধ্যে payment করে থাকেন, তাহলে এই reminder messageটি উপেক্ষা করুন।';
    protected $attributes = [
        'enabled' => true,
        'frequency' => 1,
        'reminder_dates' => '[1]',
        'message' => self::DEFAULT_MESSAGE,
        'message_options' => '[]',
        'timezone' => 'Asia/Dhaka',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'enabled' => 'boolean',
            'frequency' => 'integer',
            'reminder_dates' => 'array',
            'message_options' => 'array',
            'duplicate_reset_at' => 'datetime',
            'created_at' => 'datetime',
            'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    public static function current(): self
    {
        $settings = static::query()->orderBy('id')->first();

        if (!$settings) {
            $settings = static::query()->create([
                'enabled' => true,
                'frequency' => 1,
                'reminder_dates' => [1],
                'message' => self::DEFAULT_MESSAGE,
                'message_options' => [self::DEFAULT_MESSAGE],
                'timezone' => 'Asia/Dhaka',
            ]);
        }

        return $settings;
    }

    public function reminderDates(): array
    {
        $dates = is_array($this->reminder_dates)
            ? $this->reminder_dates
            : [1];

        return collect($dates)
            ->map(fn ($date) => (int) $date)
            ->filter(fn ($date) => $date >= 1 && $date <= 31)
            ->unique()
            ->sort()
            ->values()
            ->all();
    }

    public function isReminderDate(int|Carbon $date): bool
    {
        $day = $date instanceof Carbon ? $date->day : (int) $date;
        return in_array((int) $day, $this->reminderDates(), true);
    }

    public function messageOptions(): array
    {
        $options = is_array($this->message_options)
            ? $this->message_options
            : [];

        $options = collect($options)
            ->map(fn ($message) => trim((string) $message))
            ->filter()
            ->unique()
            ->values()
            ->take(20)
            ->all();

        $default = trim((string) $this->message);

        if ($default !== '' && !in_array($default, $options, true)) {
            array_unshift($options, $default);
        }

        return array_values(array_unique($options));
    }

    public function getTimezone(): string
    {
        $timezone = trim((string) $this->timezone);
        return $timezone !== '' ? $timezone : 'Asia/Dhaka';
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class MoneyReceipt extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;

    protected $table = 'money_receipts';

    protected $fillable = [
        'receipt_no',
        'payment_id',
        'sale_id',
        'customer_id',
        'receipt_date',
        'amount',
        'payment_method',
        'transaction_no',
        'print_count',
        'last_printed_at',
        'remarks',
        'status',
        'created_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'receipt_date' => 'date',
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'print_count' => 'integer',
        'last_printed_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    public function payment(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            SalePayment::class,
            'payment_id'
        );
    }

    public function sale(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Sale::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    public function customer(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Customer::class,
            'customer_id'
        );
    }

    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        static::creating(function (MoneyReceipt $receipt) {
            if (empty($receipt->receipt_no)) {
                $receipt->receipt_no = self::generateReceiptNo();
            }
        });
    }

    public static function generateReceiptNo(): string
    {
        $year = now()->format('Y');

        $last = self::query()
            ->where('receipt_no', 'like', "MR-{$year}-%")
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        $lastNumber = $last
            ? (int) substr($last->receipt_no, -6)
            : 0;

        return 'MR-' . $year . '-' .
            str_pad($lastNumber + 1, 6, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;

class Notice extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;

    protected $table = 'notices';
    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    protected $fillable = [
        'title',
        'description',
        'publish_date',
        'status',
        'created_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'publish_date' => 'date',
        'created_at'   => 'datetime',
        'updated_at'   => 'datetime',
    ];

    // ============================================
    // CONSTANTS
    // ============================================
    public const STATUS_DRAFT     = 'draft';
    public const STATUS_PUBLISHED = 'published';

    // ============================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================
    
    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'created_by');
    }

    // ============================================
    // SCOPES
    // ============================================

    public function scopeDraft($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_DRAFT);
    }

    public function scopePublished($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_PUBLISHED);
    }

    public function scopeSearch($query, string $search)
    {
        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
            $q->where('title', 'like', "%{$search}%")
              ->orWhere('description', 'like', "%{$search}%");
        });
    }

    // ============================================
    // ACCESSORS & MUTATORS (Updated to Laravel 9+ Standard)
    // ============================================

    protected function statusLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                $labels = [
                    self::STATUS_DRAFT     => 'ðŸ“„ Draft',
                    self::STATUS_PUBLISHED => 'ðŸ“¢ Published',
                ];
                return $labels[$this->status] ?? ucfirst($this->status);
            }
        );
    }

    protected function statusBadge(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                $badges = [
                    self::STATUS_DRAFT     => 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800',
                    self::STATUS_PUBLISHED => 'bg-green-100 text-green-800',
                ];
                return $badges[$this->status] ?? 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800';
            }
        );
    }

    protected function formattedPublishDate(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => $this->publish_date ? $this->publish_date->format('d M Y') : 'Not published'
        );
    }

    // ============================================
    // HELPERS
    // ============================================

    public function isPublished(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_PUBLISHED;
    }

    public function isDraft(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_DRAFT;
    }

    public function publish(): bool
    {
        $this->status = self::STATUS_PUBLISHED;
        if (empty($this->publish_date)) {
            $this->publish_date = now();
        }
        return $this->save();
    }

    public function unpublish(): bool
    {
        $this->status = self::STATUS_DRAFT;
        return $this->save();
    }

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function ($notice) {
            if ($notice->status === self::STATUS_PUBLISHED && empty($notice->publish_date)) {
                $notice->publish_date = now();
            }
        });
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

/**
 * Notification
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Central application notification model for:
 *
 * 1. Admin notifications
 * 2. Member notifications
 * 3. Deposit notifications
 * 4. Notice notifications
 * 5. Reminder notifications
 * 6. System notifications
 *
 * Database table:
 *     notifications
 *
 * IMPORTANT
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * This is a CUSTOM notification table.
 *
 * It is NOT Laravel's default notification structure:
 *
 *     notifiable_type
 *     notifiable_id
 *
 * Canonical ownership:
 *
 *     owner_type
 *     owner_id
 *     owner_uuid
 *
 * Canonical source:
 *
 *     source_type
 *     source_id
 *     source_uuid
 *
 * Numeric `id` remains the database primary key.
 * `record_uuid` is the public/stable UUID.
 *
 * READ STATE
 * ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * `is_read` is the ONLY canonical read/unread state.
 *
 * `read_at` is ONLY the timestamp showing when the notification
 * was marked as read.
 *
 * Therefore:
 *
 *     is_read = false  => UNREAD
 *     is_read = true   => READ
 *
 * `read_at` MUST NOT be used to determine unread/read state.
 * ============================================================================
 */
class Notification extends BaseModel
{
    use HasFactory;
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use SoftDeletes;

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DATABASE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $table = 'notifications';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | OWNER TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const OWNER_ADMIN  = 'admin';
    public const OWNER_MEMBER = 'member';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY ALIASES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const USER_ADMIN  = self::OWNER_ADMIN;
    public const USER_MEMBER = self::OWNER_MEMBER;

    public const VALID_OWNER_TYPES = [
        self::OWNER_ADMIN,
        self::OWNER_MEMBER,
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SOURCE TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const SOURCE_DEPOSIT  = 'deposit';
    public const SOURCE_SALE     = 'sale';
    public const SOURCE_PAYMENT  = 'payment';
    public const SOURCE_PROFILE  = 'profile';
    public const SOURCE_PASSWORD = 'password';
    public const SOURCE_REMINDER = 'reminder';
    public const SOURCE_NOTICE   = 'notice';
    public const SOURCE_OTHER    = 'other';

    public const VALID_SOURCE_TYPES = [
        self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT,
        self::SOURCE_SALE,
        self::SOURCE_PAYMENT,
        self::SOURCE_PROFILE,
        self::SOURCE_PASSWORD,
        self::SOURCE_REMINDER,
        self::SOURCE_NOTICE,
        self::SOURCE_OTHER,
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | GENERAL NOTIFICATION TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_GENERAL   = 'general';
    public const TYPE_SYSTEM    = 'system';
    public const TYPE_NOTICE    = 'notice';
    public const TYPE_ALERT     = 'alert';
    public const TYPE_REMINDER  = 'reminder';
    public const TYPE_PROMOTION = 'promotion';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MEMBER TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_MEMBER_REGISTERED = 'member_registered';
    public const TYPE_MEMBER_APPROVED   = 'member_approved';
    public const TYPE_MEMBER_REJECTED   = 'member_rejected';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DEPOSIT TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_DEPOSIT_SUBMITTED = 'deposit_submitted';
    public const TYPE_DEPOSIT_APPROVED  = 'deposit_approved';
    public const TYPE_DEPOSIT_REJECTED  = 'deposit_rejected';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | NOTICE TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_NOTICE_PUBLISHED = 'notice_published';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | VALID TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const VALID_TYPES = [
        self::TYPE_GENERAL,
        self::TYPE_SYSTEM,
        self::TYPE_NOTICE,
        self::TYPE_ALERT,
        self::TYPE_REMINDER,
        self::TYPE_PROMOTION,

        self::TYPE_MEMBER_REGISTERED,
        self::TYPE_MEMBER_APPROVED,
        self::TYPE_MEMBER_REJECTED,

        self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_SUBMITTED,
        self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_APPROVED,
        self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_REJECTED,

        self::TYPE_NOTICE_PUBLISHED,
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PRIORITIES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const PRIORITY_LOW    = 'low';
    public const PRIORITY_NORMAL = 'normal';
    public const PRIORITY_HIGH   = 'high';
    public const PRIORITY_URGENT = 'urgent';

    public const VALID_PRIORITIES = [
        self::PRIORITY_LOW,
        self::PRIORITY_NORMAL,
        self::PRIORITY_HIGH,
        self::PRIORITY_URGENT,
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CHANNELS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const CHANNEL_APP       = 'app';
    public const CHANNEL_EMAIL     = 'email';
    public const CHANNEL_SMS       = 'sms';
    public const CHANNEL_WHATSAPP  = 'whatsapp';
    public const CHANNEL_BROADCAST = 'broadcast';

    public const VALID_CHANNELS = [
        self::CHANNEL_APP,
        self::CHANNEL_EMAIL,
        self::CHANNEL_SMS,
        self::CHANNEL_WHATSAPP,
        self::CHANNEL_BROADCAST,
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TYPE → SOURCE MAP
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_SOURCE_MAP = [

        self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_SUBMITTED => self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT,
        self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_APPROVED  => self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT,
        self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_REJECTED  => self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT,

        self::TYPE_MEMBER_REGISTERED => self::SOURCE_PROFILE,
        self::TYPE_MEMBER_APPROVED   => self::SOURCE_PROFILE,
        self::TYPE_MEMBER_REJECTED   => self::SOURCE_PROFILE,

        self::TYPE_NOTICE           => self::SOURCE_NOTICE,
        self::TYPE_NOTICE_PUBLISHED => self::SOURCE_NOTICE,

        self::TYPE_REMINDER => self::SOURCE_REMINDER,
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FILLABLE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'owner_type',
        'owner_id',
        'owner_uuid',

        'type',
        'title',
        'message',

        'source_type',
        'source_id',
        'source_uuid',

        'action_url',

        'is_read',
        'read_at',

        'priority',
        'channel',
        'data',
        'expires_at',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CASTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'is_read'    => 'boolean',
            'read_at'    => 'datetime',
            'data'       => 'array',
            'expires_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | RELATIONSHIPS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function admin(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'owner_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Member::class,
            'owner_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    public function deposit(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Deposit::class,
            'source_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | OWNER VALIDATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function isValidOwnerType(
        ?string $ownerType
    ): bool {
        return in_array(
            strtolower(trim((string) $ownerType)),
            self::VALID_OWNER_TYPES,
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SOURCE VALIDATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function isValidSourceType(
        ?string $sourceType
    ): bool {
        if ($sourceType === null) {
            return true;
        }

        return in_array(
            strtolower(trim($sourceType)),
            self::VALID_SOURCE_TYPES,
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TYPE VALIDATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function isValidType(
        ?string $type
    ): bool {
        if ($type === null || trim($type) === '') {
            return false;
        }

        return in_array(
            strtolower(trim($type)),
            self::VALID_TYPES,
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PRIORITY VALIDATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function isValidPriority(
        ?string $priority
    ): bool {
        if ($priority === null) {
            return true;
        }

        return in_array(
            strtolower(trim($priority)),
            self::VALID_PRIORITIES,
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CHANNEL VALIDATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function isValidChannel(
        ?string $channel
    ): bool {
        if ($channel === null) {
            return true;
        }

        return in_array(
            strtolower(trim($channel)),
            self::VALID_CHANNELS,
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DEFAULT SOURCE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function defaultSourceForType(
        ?string $type
    ): string {
        $type = strtolower(trim((string) $type));

        return self::TYPE_SOURCE_MAP[$type]
            ?? self::SOURCE_OTHER;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | NORMALIZE TYPE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function normalizeType(
        ?string $type
    ): string {
        $type = strtolower(trim((string) $type));

        return $type !== ''
            ? $type
            : self::TYPE_GENERAL;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | NORMALIZE OWNER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function normalizeOwnerType(
        ?string $ownerType
    ): string {
        return strtolower(trim((string) $ownerType));
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | NORMALIZE SOURCE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function normalizeSourceType(
        ?string $sourceType,
        ?string $notificationType = null
    ): string {
        if (
            $sourceType !== null
            && trim($sourceType) !== ''
        ) {
            return strtolower(trim($sourceType));
        }

        return self::defaultSourceForType(
            $notificationType
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CREATE FROM ARRAY
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function createFromArray(
        array $attributes
    ): static {
        $type = self::normalizeType(
            $attributes['type'] ?? self::TYPE_GENERAL
        );

        $ownerType = self::normalizeOwnerType(
            $attributes['owner_type'] ?? ''
        );

        if (!self::isValidOwnerType($ownerType)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                'Invalid notification owner type.'
            );
        }

        if (!self::isValidType($type)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                'Invalid notification type.'
            );
        }

        $sourceType = self::normalizeSourceType(
            $attributes['source_type'] ?? null,
            $type
        );

        if (!self::isValidSourceType($sourceType)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                'Invalid notification source type.'
            );
        }

        $priority = strtolower(
            trim(
                (string) (
                    $attributes['priority']
                    ?? self::PRIORITY_NORMAL
                )
            )
        );

        if (!self::isValidPriority($priority)) {
            $priority = self::PRIORITY_NORMAL;
        }

        $channel = strtolower(
            trim(
                (string) (
                    $attributes['channel']
                    ?? self::CHANNEL_APP
                )
            )
        );

        if (!self::isValidChannel($channel)) {
            $channel = self::CHANNEL_APP;
        }

        $attributes['owner_type']  = $ownerType;
        $attributes['type']        = $type;
        $attributes['source_type'] = $sourceType;
        $attributes['priority']    = $priority;
        $attributes['channel']     = $channel;

        $attributes['title'] = trim(
            (string) ($attributes['title'] ?? '')
        );

        $attributes['message'] = trim(
            (string) ($attributes['message'] ?? '')
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | CANONICAL READ STATE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | Every newly created notification is unread unless explicitly
        | requested otherwise.
        |
        */

        $attributes['is_read'] = (bool) (
            $attributes['is_read'] ?? false
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | READ TIMESTAMP SAFETY
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | If notification is unread, read_at must be NULL.
        |
        */

        if ($attributes['is_read'] === false) {
            $attributes['read_at'] = null;
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | IF ALREADY READ BUT NO TIMESTAMP
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | Keep it valid. We do not force a timestamp here because the
        | creation timestamp is not necessarily the actual read time.
        |
        */

        return static::query()->create(
            $attributes
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CREATE FOR USER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY API
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function createForUser(
        string $userType,
        int $userId,
        string $title,
        string $message,
        string $type = self::TYPE_GENERAL,
        ?string $actionUrl = null,
        ?string $icon = null,
        array $data = [],
        ?int $memberId = null,
        ?int $depositId = null
    ): static {
        $ownerType = self::normalizeOwnerType(
            $userType
        );

        if (!self::isValidOwnerType($ownerType)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                'Invalid notification owner type: ' . $userType
            );
        }

        $type = self::normalizeType($type);

        if (!self::isValidType($type)) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException(
                'Invalid notification type: ' . $type
            );
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | SOURCE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $sourceType = self::defaultSourceForType($type);
        $sourceId   = null;
        $sourceUuid = null;

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | DEPOSIT SOURCE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        if ($depositId !== null) {
            $sourceType = self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT;
            $sourceId   = $depositId;

            $deposit = Deposit::query()->find(
                $depositId
            );

            if ($deposit) {
                $sourceUuid =
                    $deposit->record_uuid ?? null;
            }
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | OWNER UUID
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $ownerUuid = null;

        if ($ownerType === self::OWNER_MEMBER) {
            $member = Member::query()->find(
                $userId
            );

            if ($member) {
                $ownerUuid =
                    $member->record_uuid ?? null;
            }
        }

        if ($ownerType === self::OWNER_ADMIN) {
            $admin = Admin::query()->find(
                $userId
            );

            if ($admin) {
                $ownerUuid =
                    $admin->record_uuid ?? null;
            }
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | DATA
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $data = is_array($data)
            ? $data
            : [];

        if ($memberId !== null) {
            $data['member_id'] = $memberId;
        }

        if ($depositId !== null) {
            $data['deposit_id'] = $depositId;
        }

        if ($actionUrl !== null) {
            $data['action_url'] = $actionUrl;
        }

        if ($icon !== null) {
            $data['icon'] = $icon;
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | CREATE
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        return self::createFromArray([
            'owner_type'  => $ownerType,
            'owner_id'    => $userId,
            'owner_uuid'  => $ownerUuid,

            'type'        => $type,
            'title'       => $title,
            'message'     => $message,

            'source_type' => $sourceType,
            'source_id'   => $sourceId,
            'source_uuid' => $sourceUuid,

            'action_url'  => $actionUrl,

            'is_read'     => false,
            'read_at'     => null,

            'priority'    => self::PRIORITY_NORMAL,
            'channel'     => self::CHANNEL_APP,

            'data'        => $data,
        ]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CREATE FOR MEMBER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function createForMember(
        int $memberId,
        string $title,
        string $message,
        string $type = self::TYPE_GENERAL,
        ?string $actionUrl = null,
        ?string $icon = null,
        array $data = [],
        ?int $depositId = null
    ): static {
        return self::createForUser(
            self::OWNER_MEMBER,
            $memberId,
            $title,
            $message,
            $type,
            $actionUrl,
            $icon,
            $data,
            $memberId,
            $depositId
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CREATE FOR ADMIN
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function createForAdmin(
        int $adminId,
        string $title,
        string $message,
        string $type = self::TYPE_GENERAL,
        ?string $actionUrl = null,
        ?string $icon = null,
        array $data = [],
        ?int $memberId = null,
        ?int $depositId = null
    ): static {
        return self::createForUser(
            self::OWNER_ADMIN,
            $adminId,
            $title,
            $message,
            $type,
            $actionUrl,
            $icon,
            $data,
            $memberId,
            $depositId
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | OWNER SCOPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeByOwner(
        Builder $query,
        string $ownerType,
        int $ownerId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'owner_type',
                self::normalizeOwnerType($ownerType)
            )
            ->where(
                'owner_id',
                $ownerId
            );
    }

    public function scopeByUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->byOwner(
            $userType,
            $userId
        );
    }

    public function scopeForAdmin(
        Builder $query,
        int $adminId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'owner_type',
                self::OWNER_ADMIN
            )
            ->where(
                'owner_id',
                $adminId
            );
    }

    public function scopeForMember(
        Builder $query,
        int $memberId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'owner_type',
                self::OWNER_MEMBER
            )
            ->where(
                'owner_id',
                $memberId
            );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | READ STATE SCOPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Canonical unread scope.
     *
     * ONLY is_read controls unread state.
     */
    public function scopeUnread(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'is_read',
            false
        );
    }

    /**
     * Canonical read scope.
     *
     * ONLY is_read controls read state.
     */
    public function scopeRead(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'is_read',
            true
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MARK AS READ
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | This method is required by:
    |
    | - Admin NotificationController
    | - Member NotificationController
    | - Notification UI
    | - Future notification APIs
    |
    */

    public function markAsRead(): bool
    {
        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Already read
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        |
        | Do not unnecessarily change read_at every time.
        |
        */

        if ($this->is_read === true) {
            /*
             * If legacy data has is_read=true but read_at=NULL,
             * repair the timestamp.
             */

            if ($this->read_at === null) {
                return $this->update([
                    'read_at' => now(),
                ]);
            }

            return true;
        }

        return $this->update([
            'is_read' => true,
            'read_at' => now(),
        ]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MARK AS UNREAD
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function markAsUnread(): bool
    {
        return $this->update([
            'is_read' => false,
            'read_at' => null,
        ]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | READ STATE HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isUnread(): bool
    {
        return $this->is_read === false;
    }

    public function isRead(): bool
    {
        return $this->is_read === true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TYPE SCOPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeOfType(
        Builder $query,
        string $type
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'type',
            self::normalizeType($type)
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SOURCE SCOPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeForSource(
        Builder $query,
        string $sourceType,
        ?int $sourceId = null
    ): Builder {
        $query->where(
            'source_type',
            self::normalizeSourceType($sourceType)
        );

        if ($sourceId !== null) {
            $query->where(
                'source_id',
                $sourceId
            );
        }

        return $query;
    }

    public function scopeForDeposit(
        Builder $query,
        int $depositId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'source_type',
                self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT
            )
            ->where(
                'source_id',
                $depositId
            );
    }

    public function scopeForNotice(
        Builder $query,
        int $noticeId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'source_type',
                self::SOURCE_NOTICE
            )
            ->where(
                'source_id',
                $noticeId
            );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACTIVE NOTIFICATIONS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeActive(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            function (Builder $q) {
                $q->whereNull('expires_at')
                    ->orWhere(
                        'expires_at',
                        '>',
                        now()
                    );
            }
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SOURCE CHECKS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function sourceIs(
        string $sourceType
    ): bool {
        return $this->source_type ===
            self::normalizeSourceType(
                $sourceType
            );
    }

    public function sourceIsDeposit(): bool
    {
        return $this->sourceIs(
            self::SOURCE_DEPOSIT
        );
    }

    public function sourceIsNotice(): bool
    {
        return $this->sourceIs(
            self::SOURCE_NOTICE
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TYPE CHECKS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isDepositNotification(): bool
    {
        return $this->sourceIsDeposit();
    }

    public function isNoticeNotification(): bool
    {
        return $this->sourceIsNotice();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | UNREAD COUNT HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function getMemberUnreadCount(
        int $memberId
    ): int {
        return self::query()
            ->forMember($memberId)
            ->unread()
            ->active()
            ->count();
    }

    public static function getAdminUnreadCount(
        int $adminId
    ): int {
        return self::query()
            ->forAdmin($adminId)
            ->unread()
            ->active()
            ->count();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DATA HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function dataValue(
        string $key,
        mixed $default = null
    ): mixed {
        $data = is_array($this->data)
            ? $this->data
            : [];

        return $data[$key] ?? $default;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACTION URL
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getActionUrl(): ?string
    {
        if (
            is_string($this->action_url)
            && trim($this->action_url) !== ''
        ) {
            return $this->action_url;
        }

        $fallback = $this->dataValue(
            'action_url'
        );

        return is_string($fallback)
            && trim($fallback) !== ''
            ? $fallback
            : null;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ICON
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function defaultIcon(
        ?string $type
    ): string {
        return match (
            self::normalizeType($type)
        ) {
            self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_APPROVED =>
                'fas fa-check-circle',

            self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_REJECTED =>
                'fas fa-times-circle',

            self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_SUBMITTED =>
                'fas fa-file-invoice',

            self::TYPE_MEMBER_REGISTERED =>
                'fas fa-user-plus',

            self::TYPE_MEMBER_APPROVED =>
                'fas fa-user-check',

            self::TYPE_MEMBER_REJECTED =>
                'fas fa-user-times',

            self::TYPE_NOTICE,
            self::TYPE_NOTICE_PUBLISHED =>
                'fas fa-bullhorn',

            self::TYPE_REMINDER =>
                'fas fa-clock',

            self::TYPE_ALERT =>
                'fas fa-exclamation-triangle',

            self::TYPE_PROMOTION =>
                'fas fa-gift',

            self::TYPE_SYSTEM =>
                'fas fa-cog',

            default =>
                'fas fa-bell',
        };
    }

    public function getIcon(): string
    {
        return (string) (
            $this->dataValue('icon')
            ?: self::defaultIcon($this->type)
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | COLOR
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getColor(): string
    {
        return match (
            self::normalizeType($this->type)
        ) {
            self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_APPROVED,
            self::TYPE_MEMBER_APPROVED =>
                'success',

            self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_REJECTED,
            self::TYPE_MEMBER_REJECTED =>
                'danger',

            self::TYPE_DEPOSIT_SUBMITTED,
            self::TYPE_REMINDER =>
                'warning',

            self::TYPE_NOTICE,
            self::TYPE_NOTICE_PUBLISHED =>
                'info',

            self::TYPE_ALERT =>
                'danger',

            default =>
                'secondary',
        };
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * PASSWORD HISTORY
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Stores previous password hashes.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - Never store plaintext passwords.
 * - Never decrypt passwords.
 * - Never expose password hashes in API responses.
 */
class PasswordHistory extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'password_histories';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'password',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'password',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    public function scopeLatestFirst(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->latest(
            'created_at'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Check whether a plain password matches this history record.
     */
    public function matches(
        string $plainPassword
    ): bool {
        return password_verify(
            $plainPassword,
            (string) $this->password
        );
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Permission.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission as SpatiePermission;

class Permission extends SpatiePermission
{
    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'guard_name',
        'group_name',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Search
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeSearch(Builder $query, string $search): Builder
    {
        $search = trim($search);

        if ($search === '') {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
            $q->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('group_name', 'like', "%{$search}%");
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Guard
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeByGuard(Builder $query, string $guard): Builder
    {
        return $query->where('guard_name', $guard);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Group
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeByGroup(Builder $query, string $group): Builder
    {
        return $query->where('group_name', $group);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Readable Permission Name
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function readableName(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => ucwords(
                str_replace(['-', '_', '.'], ' ', $this->name)
            )
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Readable Group
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function readableGroup(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => $this->group_name
                ? ucwords(
                    str_replace(
                        ['-', '_'],
                        ' ',
                        $this->group_name
                    )
                )
                : 'General'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Grouped Permissions
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function getGroupedPermissions(
        string $guardName = 'admin'
    ): Collection {
        return self::query()
            ->where('guard_name', $guardName)
            ->orderBy('group_name')
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get()
            ->groupBy(function ($permission) {
                return $permission->group_name ?: 'general';
            });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Guards
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function guards(): array
    {
        return [
            'admin' => 'Admin Portal',
            'member' => 'Member Portal',
        ];
    }
}

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;

/**

 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Specialized security audit trail for:
 * - Role changes
 * - Permission changes
 * - Role assignment/removal
 * - Permission assignment/removal
 *
 * General data changes remain in AuditLog.
 */
class PermissionAuditLog extends BaseModel

        'admin_id',

        'action',

        'old_value',
        'new_value',

        'ip_address',
        'request_id',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'old_value' =>
                'array',

            'new_value' =>
                'array',
        ];
    }

    /*

    | Relationships

    {

        );

            'admin_id',
            $adminId
        );
    }

        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'target_user_type',
                $userType
            )
            ->where(
                'target_user_id',
                $userId
            );
    }

    public function scopeForAction(
        Builder $query,
        string $action
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'action',
            $action
        );
    }

    public function scopeForRole(
        Builder $query,
        int $roleId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'role_id',
            $roleId
        );
    }

    public function scopeForPermission(
        Builder $query,
        int $permissionId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'permission_id',
            $permissionId
        );
    }

    public function scopeRecent(
        Builder $query,
        int $days = 30
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'created_at',
            '>=',
            now()->subDays(max(1, $days))
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasChanges(): bool
    {
        return $this->old_value !== null
            || $this->new_value !== null;
    }

    public function isRoleChange(): bool
    {
        return $this->role_id !== null;
    }

    public function isPermissionChange(): bool
    {
        return $this->permission_id !== null;
    }
}
