<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class Profit extends BaseModel
{
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'profits';

    protected $fillable = [
        'member_id',
        'source_type',
        'source_id',
        'amount',
        'profit_date',
        'period',
        'distribution_id',
        'status',
        'distributed_at',
        'notes',
        'accounting_transaction_id',
        'paid_at',
        'payment_method',
        'bank_account_id',
        'payment_reference',
        'paid_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'member_id' => 'integer',
        'source_id' => 'integer',
        'distribution_id' => 'integer',
        'accounting_transaction_id' => 'integer',
        'bank_account_id' => 'integer',
        'paid_by' => 'integer',
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'profit_date' => 'date',
        'distributed_at' => 'datetime',
        'paid_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status constants
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const STATUS_PENDING = 'pending';

    public const STATUS_DISTRIBUTED = 'distributed';

    public const STATUS_HOLD = 'hold';

    public const STATUS_PAID = 'paid';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class, 'member_id');
    }

    public function distribution(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            ProfitDistribution::class,
            'distribution_id'
        );
    }

    public function accountingTransaction(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            AccountingTransaction::class,
            'accounting_transaction_id'
        );
    }

    public function bankAccount(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            BankAccount::class,
            'bank_account_id'
        );
    }

    public function paidBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'paid_by'
        );
    }

    public function source(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Query scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopePending($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_PENDING);
    }

    public function scopeDistributed($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED);
    }

    public function scopeHold($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_HOLD);
    }

    public function scopePaid($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_PAID);
    }

    public function scopeByMember($query, $memberId)
    {
        return $query->where('member_id', $memberId);
    }

    public function scopeByDistribution($query, $distributionId)
    {
        return $query->where('distribution_id', $distributionId);
    }

    public function scopeByPeriod($query, $period)
    {
        return $query->where('period', $period);
    }

    public function scopeByDateRange($query, $from, $to)
    {
        return $query->whereBetween('profit_date', [$from, $to]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Mark allocation as distributed.
     *
     * This is not proof of payment and must not create an accounting entry.
     */
    public function markAsDistributed(): bool
    {
        if (
            $this->status === self::STATUS_HOLD
            || $this->status === self::STATUS_PAID
            || $this->accounting_transaction_id !== null
                && $this->status === self::STATUS_PAID
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        return $this->update([
            'status' => self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED,
            'distributed_at' => now(),
        ]);
    }

    public function markAsPending(): bool
    {
        if (
            $this->status === self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED
            || $this->status === self::STATUS_PAID
            || $this->accounting_transaction_id !== null
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        return $this->update([
            'status' => self::STATUS_PENDING,
            'distributed_at' => null,
        ]);
    }

    public function markAsHold(): bool
    {
        if (
            $this->status === self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED
            || $this->status === self::STATUS_PAID
            || $this->accounting_transaction_id !== null
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        return $this->update([
            'status' => self::STATUS_HOLD,
            'distributed_at' => null,
        ]);
    }

    public function isPending(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_PENDING;
    }

    public function isDistributed(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED;
    }

    public function isHold(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_HOLD;
    }

    public function isPaid(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_PAID
            && $this->accounting_transaction_id !== null;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Accessors
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->status) {
            self::STATUS_PENDING => 'Pending',
            self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED => 'Distributed',
            self::STATUS_HOLD => 'Hold',
            self::STATUS_PAID => 'Paid',
            default => ucfirst((string) $this->status),
        };
    }

    public function getStatusBadgeAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->status) {
            self::STATUS_PENDING => 'badge-warning',
            self::STATUS_DISTRIBUTED => 'badge-success',
            self::STATUS_HOLD => 'badge-secondary',
            self::STATUS_PAID => 'badge-primary',
            default => 'badge-light',
        };
    }

    public function getFormattedAmountAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format((float) $this->amount, 2);
    }

    public function getProfitAmountAttribute(): float
    {
        return (float) $this->amount;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Source helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isProjectProfit(): bool
    {
        return $this->source_type === Project::class;
    }

    public function getProjectAttribute(): ?Project
    {
        if (!$this->isProjectProfit()) {
            return null;
        }

        $source = $this->source;

        return $source instanceof Project ? $source : null;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Profit validation helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasProfit(): bool
    {
        return (float) $this->amount > 0;
    }

    public function isZero(): bool
    {
        return round((float) $this->amount, 2) === 0.0;
    }

    public function hasDistribution(): bool
    {
        return $this->distribution_id !== null;
    }

    public function hasAccountingPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->accounting_transaction_id !== null
            && $this->status === self::STATUS_PAID;
    }
}

            'payment_status',
            self::PAYMENT_PAID
        );
    }

    public function scopeByProject($query, $projectId)
    {
        return $query->where('project_id', $projectId);
    }

    public function scopeByMember($query, $memberId)
    {
        return $query->where('member_id', $memberId);
    }

    public function scopeByDistribution($query, $distributionId)
    {
        return $query->where('distribution_id', $distributionId);
    }

    public function scopeByDateRange($query, $from, $to)
    {
        return $query->whereBetween(
            'distribution_date',
            [$from, $to]
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PAYMENT STATUS HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Important:
    | These legacy methods only update the status field.
    | They do not create accounting journal entries.
    |
    */

    public function markAsPaid(): bool
    {
        // Actual payment must be posted through accounting service.
        // Prevent bypassing that service through this helper.
        return false;
    }

    public function markAsPending(): bool
    {
        // A paid distribution must be reversed through accounting first.
        if ($this->isPaid()) {
            return false;
        }

        return $this->update([
            'payment_status' => self::PAYMENT_PENDING,
            'paid_at' => null,
        ]);
    }

    public function isPaid(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_status === self::PAYMENT_PAID;
    }

    public function isPending(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_status === self::PAYMENT_PENDING;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROFIT CALCULATION HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function calculateProfitFromPercentage(): float
    {
        return round(
            ((float) $this->total_profit
                * (float) $this->profit_percentage) / 100,
            2
        );
    }

    public function calculateProfitFromShares(
        int|float $memberShares
    ): float {
        $totalShares = (int) $this->total_shares;

        if ($totalShares <= 0 || $memberShares <= 0) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return round(
            ((float) $this->total_profit / $totalShares)
                * (float) $memberShares,
            2
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACCESSORS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getPaymentStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->payment_status) {
            self::PAYMENT_PAID => 'Paid',
            self::PAYMENT_PENDING => 'Pending',
            default => ucfirst((string) $this->payment_status),
        };
    }

    public function getPaymentStatusBadgeAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->payment_status) {
            self::PAYMENT_PAID => 'badge-success',
            self::PAYMENT_PENDING => 'badge-warning',
            default => 'badge-secondary',
        };
    }

    public function getFormattedTotalProfitAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->total_profit,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getFormattedProfitPerShareAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->profit_per_share,
            4
        );
    }

    public function getFormattedProfitAmountAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->profit_amount,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getFormattedProfitPercentageAttribute(): string
    {
        return number_format(
            (float) $this->profit_percentage,
            4
        ) . '%';
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | VALIDATION HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasProject(): bool
    {
        return $this->project_id !== null;
    }

    public function hasMember(): bool
    {
        return $this->member_id !== null;
    }

    public function hasProfit(): bool
    {
        return (float) $this->profit_amount > 0;
    }

    public function hasNoProfit(): bool
    {
        return (float) $this->profit_amount <= 0;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

class ProfitWithdrawal extends Model

        'rejected_at',

        'paid_by' => 'integer',
        'rejected_by' => 'integer',
        'approved_at' => 'datetime',
        'paid_at' => 'datetime',
        'rejected_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    public const STATUS_APPROVED = 'approved';
    public const STATUS_PAID = 'paid';

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class Project extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;
    use SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'projects';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MASS ASSIGNMENT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $fillable = [
        'project_no',
        'project_type',
        'name',
        'location',

        'total_area',
        'remaining_area',
        'area_unit',

        'registration_no',
        'start_date',
        'end_date',
        'status',

        'seller_name',
        'seller_mobile',
        'seller_nid',

        'deed_no',
        'khatian_no',
        'dag_no',
        'mouza',
        'registry_office',

        'description',
        'remarks',

        // Profit approval
        'profit_approval_status',
        'profit_approved_by',
        'profit_approved_at',
        'profit_approval_notes',

        // Approved financial snapshot
        'profit_approved_sales',
        'profit_approved_investment',
        'profit_approved_expenses',
        'profit_approved_net_profit',

        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CASTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $casts = [
        'total_area' => 'decimal:4',
        'remaining_area' => 'decimal:4',

        'start_date' => 'date',
        'end_date' => 'date',

        'profit_approval_status' => 'string',
        'profit_approved_by' => 'integer',
        'profit_approved_at' => 'datetime',

        'profit_approved_sales' => 'decimal:2',
        'profit_approved_investment' => 'decimal:2',
        'profit_approved_expenses' => 'decimal:2',
        'profit_approved_net_profit' => 'decimal:2',

        'created_by' => 'integer',
        'updated_by' => 'integer',

        'deleted_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROJECT STATUS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const STATUS_UPCOMING = 'upcoming';
    public const STATUS_ONGOING = 'ongoing';
    public const STATUS_COMPLETED = 'completed';
    public const STATUS_ON_HOLD = 'on_hold';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROFIT APPROVAL STATUS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const PROFIT_APPROVAL_PENDING = 'pending';
    public const PROFIT_APPROVAL_APPROVED = 'approved';
    public const PROFIT_APPROVAL_REJECTED = 'rejected';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROJECT TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_LAND = 'Land';
    public const TYPE_HOUSE = 'House';
    public const TYPE_COMMERCIAL = 'Commercial';
    public const TYPE_OTHERS = 'Others';

    /*

    | AREA / SALE UNITS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const UNIT_PERCENTAGE = 'percentage';
    public const UNIT_KATHA = 'katha';
    public const UNIT_PLOT = 'plot';
    public const UNIT_BIGHA = 'bigha';
    public const UNIT_ACRE = 'acre';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | RELATIONSHIPS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function investments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Investment::class);
    }

    public function sales(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Sale::class);
    }

    public function plots(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(ProjectPlot::class);
    }

    public function documents(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(ProjectDocument::class);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MODEL BOOT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (Project $project) {
            if (empty($project->project_no)) {
                $project->project_no = self::generateProjectNo();
            }

            if (
                $project->total_area !== null &&
                $project->remaining_area === null
            ) {
                $project->remaining_area = $project->total_area;
            }

            if (empty($project->status)) {
                $project->status = self::STATUS_UPCOMING;
            }

            if (empty($project->profit_approval_status)) {
                $project->profit_approval_status =
                    self::PROFIT_APPROVAL_PENDING;
            }
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROJECT NUMBER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function generateProjectNo(): string
    {
        $year = date('Y');

        $last = self::withTrashed()
            ->where('project_no', 'like', "PRJ-{$year}-%")
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        $lastNum = 0;

        if ($last && $last->project_no) {
            $lastNum = (int) substr($last->project_no, -5);
        }

        $nextNum = str_pad(
            (string) ($lastNum + 1),
            5,
            '0',
            STR_PAD_LEFT
        );

        return "PRJ-{$year}-{$nextNum}";
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SEARCH SCOPE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeSearch($query, $term)
    {
        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($term) {
            $q->where('name', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('project_no', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('location', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('registration_no', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('deed_no', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('dag_no', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('khatian_no', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('mouza', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('seller_name', 'like', "%{$term}%")
                ->orWhere('seller_mobile', 'like', "%{$term}%");
        });
    }

    public function scopeOngoing($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_ONGOING);
    }

    public function scopeCompleted($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_COMPLETED);
    }

    public function scopeUpcoming($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_UPCOMING);
    }

    public function scopeOnHold($query)
    {
        return $query->where('status', self::STATUS_ON_HOLD);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROJECT INVESTMENT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getProjectInvestmentTotalAttribute(): float
    {
        return round(
            (float) $this->investments()->sum('amount'),
            2
        );
    }

    public function getOrganizationInvestmentAttribute(): float
    {
        return $this->project_investment_total;
    }

    public function getTotalCostAttribute(): float
    {
        return $this->project_investment_total;
    }

    /**
     * Backward compatibility for older code.
     *
     * @deprecated Use project_investment_total instead.
     */
    public function getProjectExpensesTotalAttribute(): float
    {
        return $this->project_investment_total;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SALES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getTotalSalesAttribute(): float
    {
        return round(
            (float) $this->sales()->sum('sale_price'),
            2
        );
    }

    public function getTotalSalesCountAttribute(): int
    {
        return (int) $this->sales()->count();
    }

    public function getTotalQuantitySoldAttribute(): float
    {
        return round(
            (float) $this->sales()->sum('quantity'),
            4
        );
    }

    public function getTotalAreaSoldAttribute(): float
    {
        return $this->total_quantity_sold;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROFIT / LOSS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getNetProfitAttribute(): float
    {
        return round(
            $this->total_sales - $this->project_investment_total,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getProfitStatusAttribute(): string
    {
        if ($this->net_profit > 0) {
            return 'profit';
        }

        if ($this->net_profit < 0) {
            return 'loss';
        }

        return 'neutral';
    }

    public function getProfitStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->profit_status) {
            'profit' => '📈 Profit',
            'loss' => '📉 Loss',
            default => '➖ Neutral',
        };
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | APPROVED PROFIT SNAPSHOT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getHasApprovedProfitSnapshotAttribute(): bool
    {
        return $this->profit_approval_status
                === self::PROFIT_APPROVAL_APPROVED
            && $this->profit_approved_sales !== null
            && $this->profit_approved_investment !== null
            && $this->profit_approved_expenses !== null
            && $this->profit_approved_net_profit !== null;
    }

    public function getApprovedProfitSnapshotAttribute(): ?array
    {
        if (!$this->has_approved_profit_snapshot) {
            return null;
        }

        return [
            'sales' => (float) $this->profit_approved_sales,
            'investment' => (float) $this->profit_approved_investment,
            'expenses' => (float) $this->profit_approved_expenses,
            'net_profit' => (float) $this->profit_approved_net_profit,
            'approved_by' => $this->profit_approved_by,
            'approved_at' => $this->profit_approved_at,
            'notes' => $this->profit_approval_notes,
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | AREA / QUANTITY
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getSoldAreaAttribute(): float
    {
        if ($this->total_area === null) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return round(
            max(
                0,
                (float) $this->total_area
                    - (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0)
            ),
            4
        );
    }

    public function getSoldPercentageAttribute(): float
    {
        if (
            $this->total_area === null ||
            (float) $this->total_area <= 0
        ) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return round(
            ($this->sold_area / (float) $this->total_area) * 100,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getRemainingPercentageAttribute(): float
    {
        if (
            $this->total_area === null ||
            (float) $this->total_area <= 0
        ) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return round(
            (
                (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0)
                / (float) $this->total_area
            ) * 100,
            2
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | COST PER UNIT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getCostPerUnitAttribute(): float
    {
        if (
            $this->total_area === null ||
            (float) $this->total_area <= 0
        ) {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return round(
            $this->total_cost / (float) $this->total_area,
            2
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROJECT COMPLETION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isFullySold(): bool
    {
        if (
            $this->total_area === null ||
            (float) $this->total_area <= 0
        ) {
            return false;
        }

        return (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0) <= 0;
    }

    public function markAsCompleted(): bool
    {
        return $this->update([
            'status' => self::STATUS_COMPLETED,
        ]);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | DOCUMENTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function hasDocument(): bool
    {
        return $this->documents()->exists();
    }

    public function getDocumentsCountAttribute(): int
    {
        return (int) $this->documents()->count();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | STATUS LABEL
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return [
            self::STATUS_UPCOMING => '📋 Upcoming',
            self::STATUS_ONGOING => '🔄 Ongoing',
            self::STATUS_COMPLETED => '✅ Completed',
            self::STATUS_ON_HOLD => '⏸️ On Hold',
        ][$this->status] ?? ucfirst((string) $this->status);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PROJECT TYPE LABEL
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getTypeLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return [
            self::TYPE_LAND => '🏞️ Land',
            self::TYPE_HOUSE => '🏠 House',
            self::TYPE_COMMERCIAL => '🏢 Commercial',
            self::TYPE_OTHERS => '📦 Others',
        ][$this->project_type] ?? (string) $this->project_type;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | AREA UNIT LABEL
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getAreaUnitLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return [
            self::UNIT_PERCENTAGE => 'শতাংশ / শতক',
            self::UNIT_KATHA => 'কাঠা',
            self::UNIT_PLOT => 'Plot',
            self::UNIT_BIGHA => 'বিঘা',
            self::UNIT_ACRE => 'একর',
        ][$this->area_unit] ?? ucfirst((string) $this->area_unit);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FORMATTED VALUES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getTotalCostFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format($this->total_cost, 2);
    }

    public function getOrganizationInvestmentFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format($this->organization_investment, 2);
    }

    public function getTotalSalesFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format($this->total_sales, 2);
    }

    public function getNetProfitFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format($this->net_profit, 2);
    }

    public function getCostPerUnitFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format($this->cost_per_unit, 2);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FINANCIAL SUMMARY
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getFinancialSummaryAttribute(): array
    {
        return [
            'organization_investment' => $this->organization_investment,
            'project_investment' => $this->project_investment_total,
            'total_cost' => $this->total_cost,
            'total_sales' => $this->total_sales,
            'total_sales_count' => $this->total_sales_count,
            'net_profit' => $this->net_profit,
            'profit_status' => $this->profit_status,

            'profit_approval_status' => $this->profit_approval_status,
            'has_approved_profit_snapshot' => $this->has_approved_profit_snapshot,
            'approved_profit_snapshot' => $this->approved_profit_snapshot,

            'total_area' => (float) ($this->total_area ?? 0),
            'sold_area' => $this->sold_area,
            'remaining_area' => (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0),
            'sold_percentage' => $this->sold_percentage,
            'remaining_percentage' => $this->remaining_percentage,
            'cost_per_unit' => $this->cost_per_unit,
            'is_fully_sold' => $this->isFullySold(),
            'has_document' => $this->hasDocument(),
            'documents_count' => $this->documents_count,
        ];
    }

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;

class ProjectCost extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid, HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'project_costs';

    protected $fillable = [
        'entry_type',
        'project_id',
        'member_id',
        'responsible_person',
        'investment_no',
        'expense_no',
        'investment_category',
        'expense_category',
        'description',
        'amount',
        'entry_date',
        'reference_no',
        'paid_to',
        'payment_method',
        'to_bank_id',
        'is_approved',
        'approved_at',
        'approved_by',
        'remarks',
        'document_file',
        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'entry_date' => 'date',
        'is_approved' => 'boolean',
        'approved_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    public const TYPE_INVESTMENT = 'investment';
    public const TYPE_EXPENSE = 'expense';

    public const PAYMENT_CASH = 'cash';
    public const PAYMENT_BANK = 'bank';

    public function scopeInvestments(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'entry_type',
            self::TYPE_INVESTMENT
        );
    }

    public function scopeExpenses(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where(
            'entry_type',
            self::TYPE_EXPENSE
        );
    }

    public function scopeApproved(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where('is_approved', true);
    }

    public function scopeByProject(
        Builder $query,
        $projectId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('project_id', $projectId);
    }

    public function scopeByPaymentMethod(
        Builder $query,
        string $method
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where('payment_method', $method);
    }

    public function scopeSearch(
        Builder $query,
        string $search
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(function (Builder $q) use ($search) {
            $q->where('investment_no', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('expense_no', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('reference_no', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('paid_to', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('description', 'like', "%{$search}%");
        });
    }

    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Project::class, 'project_id');
    }

    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class, 'member_id');
    }

    public function responsiblePerson(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class, 'responsible_person');
    }

    public function bankAccount(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(BankAccount::class, 'to_bank_id');
    }

    public function approvedBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'approved_by');
    }

    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'created_by');
    }

    public function updater(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'updated_by');
    }

    protected function amountFormatted(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => '৳ ' . number_format(
                (float) $this->amount,
                2
            )
        );
    }

    protected function entryDateFormatted(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => $this->entry_date?->format('d M Y') ?? '-'
        );
    }

    public function isInvestment(): bool
    {
        return $this->entry_type === self::TYPE_INVESTMENT;
    }

    public function isExpense(): bool
    {
        return $this->entry_type === self::TYPE_EXPENSE;
    }

    public function isCashPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::PAYMENT_CASH;
    }

    public function isBankPayment(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::PAYMENT_BANK;
    }

    public function hasDocument(): bool
    {
        return filled($this->document_file);
    }

    public function approve(int $adminId): void
    {
        if (!$this->isInvestment()) {
            throw new \LogicException(
                'Only investment entries support this approval workflow.'
            );
        }

        $this->update([
            'is_approved' => true,
            'approved_at' => now(),
            'approved_by' => $adminId,
        ]);
    }

    public function getCategoryLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        if ($this->isInvestment()) {
            return Investment::categoryLabelFor(
                $this->investment_category
            );
        }

        return ProjectExpense::CATEGORY_LABELS[
            $this->expense_category
        ] ?? ucfirst(str_replace(
            '_',
            ' ',
            (string) $this->expense_category
        ));
    }

    public function getProjectCostContributionAttribute(): float
    {
        return round((float) $this->amount, 2);
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class ProjectDocument extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;

    protected $table = 'project_documents';

    protected $fillable = [
        'project_id',
        'document_name',
        'document_type',
        'file_path',
        'file_extension',
        'file_size',
        'remarks',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'file_size' => 'integer',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | RELATIONSHIP
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Project::class,
            'project_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FILE SIZE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getFileSizeFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        if (!$this->file_size) {
            return '-';
        }

        $size = $this->file_size;

        if ($size < 1024) {
            return $size . ' B';
        }

        if ($size < 1024 * 1024) {
            return round(
                $size / 1024,
                2
            ) . ' KB';
        }

        if ($size < 1024 * 1024 * 1024) {
            return round(
                $size / (1024 * 1024),
                2
            ) . ' MB';
        }

        return round(
            $size / (1024 * 1024 * 1024),
            2
        ) . ' GB';
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | FILE EXTENSION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getExtensionLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return strtoupper(
            (string) $this->file_extension
        );
    }
}

        $query,
        $term
    ) {

            $q->where(
                'description',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            )
            ->orWhere(
                'reference_no',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            )
            ->orWhere(
                'paid_to',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            )
            ->orWhere(
                'remarks',
                'like',
                "%{$term}%"
            );

        });

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class ProjectPlot extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'project_plots';

    protected $fillable = [

        // ============================================================
        // PROJECT
        // ============================================================

        'project_id',

        // ============================================================
        // PLOT INFORMATION
        // ============================================================

        'plot_no',
        'plot_name',

        // ============================================================
        // AREA
        // ============================================================

        'area',
        'area_unit',
        'remaining_area',

        // ============================================================
        // SALE RATE
        // ============================================================

        'unit_rate',
        'total_price',

        // ============================================================
        // STATUS
        // ============================================================

        'status',

        // ============================================================
        // DESCRIPTION
        // ============================================================

        'description',
        'remarks',
    ];

    protected $casts = [

        'area'           => 'decimal:4',
        'remaining_area' => 'decimal:4',

        'unit_rate'      => 'decimal:2',
        'total_price'    => 'decimal:2',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // STATUS
    // ============================================================

    public const STATUS_AVAILABLE = 'available';
    public const STATUS_PARTIAL   = 'partial';
    public const STATUS_SOLD      = 'sold';

    // ============================================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================================

    /**
     * Parent Project
     */
    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Project::class);
    }

    /**
     * Sales against this Plot
     */
    public function sales(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            Sale::class,
            'plot_id'
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // MODEL BOOT
    // ============================================================

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (ProjectPlot $plot) {

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | Initial Remaining Area
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if ($plot->remaining_area === null) {
                $plot->remaining_area = $plot->area;
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | Calculate Total Price
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            |
            | Plot Area Ã— Unit Rate
            |
            */

            if (
                $plot->total_price === null &&
                $plot->area !== null &&
                $plot->unit_rate !== null
            ) {
                $plot->total_price =
                    (float) $plot->area *
                    (float) $plot->unit_rate;
            }

            /*
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            | Default Status
            |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
            */

            if (empty($plot->status)) {
                $plot->status = self::STATUS_AVAILABLE;
            }
        });
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SOLD AREA
    // ============================================================

    /**
     * Total sold area of this plot.
     */
    public function getSoldAreaAttribute(): float
    {
        return round(
            max(
                0,
                (float) ($this->area ?? 0)
                -
                (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0)
            ),
            4
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SOLD PERCENTAGE
    // ============================================================

    public function getSoldPercentageAttribute(): float
    {
        if (
            $this->area === null ||
            (float) $this->area <= 0
        ) {
            return 0;
        }

        return round(
            (
                $this->sold_area /
                (float) $this->area
            ) * 100,
            2
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // REMAINING PERCENTAGE
    // ============================================================

    public function getRemainingPercentageAttribute(): float
    {
        if (
            $this->area === null ||
            (float) $this->area <= 0
        ) {
            return 0;
        }

        return round(
            (
                (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0) /
                (float) $this->area
            ) * 100,
            2
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // TOTAL SALES
    // ============================================================

    /**
     * Total sales revenue generated from this Plot.
     */
    public function getTotalSalesAttribute(): float
    {
        return round(
            (float) $this->sales()->sum('sale_price'),
            2
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SALES COUNT
    // ============================================================

    public function getSalesCountAttribute(): int
    {
        return (int) $this->sales()->count();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // AVAILABLE
    // ============================================================

    public function isAvailable(): bool
    {
        return
            $this->status === self::STATUS_AVAILABLE &&
            (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0) > 0;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // PARTIALLY SOLD
    // ============================================================

    public function isPartiallySold(): bool
    {
        return
            (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0) > 0 &&
            (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0)
                < (float) ($this->area ?? 0);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // FULLY SOLD
    // ============================================================

    public function isFullySold(): bool
    {
        return
            (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0) <= 0;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // REFRESH STATUS
    // ============================================================

    public function refreshStatus(): void
    {
        $area = (float) ($this->area ?? 0);
        $remaining = (float) ($this->remaining_area ?? 0);

        if ($remaining <= 0) {

            $this->status = self::STATUS_SOLD;

        } elseif ($remaining < $area) {

            $this->status = self::STATUS_PARTIAL;

        } else {

            $this->status = self::STATUS_AVAILABLE;
        }

        $this->saveQuietly();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // FORMATTED VALUES
    // ============================================================

    public function getUnitRateFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return 'à§³ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->unit_rate,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getTotalPriceFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return 'à§³ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->total_price,
            2
        );
    }

    public function getTotalSalesFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return 'à§³ ' . number_format(
            $this->total_sales,
            2
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // STATUS LABEL
    // ============================================================

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->status) {

            self::STATUS_AVAILABLE =>
                'ðŸŸ¢ Available',

            self::STATUS_PARTIAL =>
                'ðŸŸ¡ Partially Sold',

            self::STATUS_SOLD =>
                'ðŸ”´ Sold',

            default =>
                ucfirst((string) $this->status),
        };
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class ReceivedBank extends Model
{
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'received_banks';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'bank_name',
        'is_active',
        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'is_active' => 'boolean',
    ];

    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        static::creating(function (ReceivedBank $bank) {
            if (empty($bank->record_uuid)) {
                $bank->record_uuid = (string) \Illuminate\Support\Str::uuid();
            }

            if (auth()->check() && empty($bank->created_by)) {
                $bank->created_by = auth()->id();
            }
        });

        static::updating(function (ReceivedBank $bank) {
            if (auth()->check()) {
                $bank->updated_by = auth()->id();
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Scope active received banks.
     */
    public function scopeActive($query)
    {
        return $query
            ->where('is_active', true)
            ->orderBy('bank_name');
    }

    /**
     * Created by user.
     */
    public function creator()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'created_by');
    }

    /**
     * Updated by user.
     */
    public function updater()
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'updated_by');
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Resignation.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;

class Resignation extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'resignations';

    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    protected $fillable = [
        'resignation_no',
        'member_id',
        'share_quantity',
        'refund_amount',
        'request_date',
        'status',
        'note',
        'reason',
        'approved_by',
        'approved_at',
        'rejected_at',
        'rejection_reason',
        'remarks',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'refund_amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'request_date' => 'date',
        'approved_at' => 'datetime',
        'rejected_at' => 'datetime',
        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        'deleted_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // CONSTANTS
    // ============================================================

    public const STATUS_PENDING = 'pending';
    public const STATUS_APPROVED = 'approved';
    public const STATUS_REJECTED = 'rejected';

    // ============================================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================================

    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class);
    }

    public function approver(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'approved_by'
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // BUSINESS LOGIC
    // ============================================================

    public function approve($adminId = null): bool
    {
        return $this->update([
            'status' => self::STATUS_APPROVED,
            'approved_at' => now(),
            'approved_by' => $adminId ?? $this->approved_by,
        ]);
    }

    public function reject(
        $adminId = null,
        string $reason = null
    ): bool {
        return $this->update([
            'status' => self::STATUS_REJECTED,
            'rejected_at' => now(),
            'rejection_reason' => $reason,
            'approved_by' => $adminId ?? $this->approved_by,
        ]);
    }

    public static function generateResignationNo(): string
    {
        $latest = self::withTrashed()
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        $nextSequence = $latest
            ? $latest->id + 1
            : 1;

        return 'RESIGN-' . str_pad(
            $nextSequence,
            6,
            '0',
            STR_PAD_LEFT
        );
    }

    public function calculateRefund(): float
    {
        if (!$this->member) {
            return 0;
        }

        $member = $this->member;

        $totalNetWorth =
            ($member->total_deposit ?? 0)
            + ($member->total_profit_earned ?? 0)
            - ($member->total_withdraw ?? 0);

        $perShare = $member->shares > 0
            ? $totalNetWorth / $member->shares
            : 0;

        $refund = $perShare * $this->share_quantity;

        return max(0, $refund);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // MODEL BOOT
    // ============================================================

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (Resignation $resignation) {
            if (empty($resignation->resignation_no)) {
                $resignation->resignation_no =
                    self::generateResignationNo();
            }

            if (empty($resignation->status)) {
                $resignation->status =
                    self::STATUS_PENDING;
            }

            if (empty($resignation->share_quantity)) {
                $resignation->share_quantity = 1;
            }
        });
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ACCESSORS
    // ============================================================

    public function getStatusBadgeAttribute(): string
    {
        return [
            self::STATUS_PENDING => 'badge-warning',
            self::STATUS_APPROVED => 'badge-success',
            self::STATUS_REJECTED => 'badge-danger',
        ][$this->status] ?? 'badge-secondary';
    }

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return [
            self::STATUS_PENDING => 'â³ Pending',
            self::STATUS_APPROVED => 'âœ… Approved',
            self::STATUS_REJECTED => 'âŒ Rejected',
        ][$this->status] ?? ucfirst((string) $this->status);
    }

    public function getFormattedRefundAttribute(): string
    {
        return 'à§³ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->refund_amount,
            2
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // BOOLEAN HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    public function isPending(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_PENDING;
    }

    public function isApproved(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_APPROVED;
    }

    public function isRejected(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_REJECTED;
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Role.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role as SpatieRole;

class Role extends SpatieRole
{
    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'guard_name',
        'description',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Search Scope
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeSearch(Builder $query, string $search): Builder
    {
        $search = trim($search);

        if ($search === '') {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where(function ($q) use ($search) {
            $q->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                ->orWhere('description', 'like', "%{$search}%");
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Guard Scope
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeByGuard(Builder $query, string $guard): Builder
    {
        return $query->where('guard_name', $guard);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Readable Role Name
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function readableName(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => ucwords(
                str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', $this->name)
            )
        );
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Super Admin
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isSuperAdmin(): bool
    {
        return strtolower($this->name) === 'super-admin'
            && $this->guard_name === 'admin';
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Guards
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function guards(): array
    {
        return [
            'admin' => 'Admin Portal',
            'member' => 'Member Portal',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Readable Name Helper
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function readableRoleName(?string $name): string
    {
        if (!$name) {
            return '-';
        }

        return ucwords(
            str_replace(['-', '_'], ' ', $name)
        );
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Sale.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class Sale extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;
    use SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'sales';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'sale_no',

        'project_id',
        'plot_id',
        'customer_id',

        'sale_type',
        'sale_unit',

        'quantity',
        'unit_rate',
        'sale_price',

        'booking_amount',
        'paid_amount',
        'due_amount',

        'has_installment',

        'sale_date',
        'status',

        'purchase_price',
        'total_expense',
        'profit_amount',

        'remarks',

        'created_by',
        'updated_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'quantity' => 'decimal:4',
        'unit_rate' => 'decimal:2',
        'sale_price' => 'decimal:2',

        'booking_amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'paid_amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'due_amount' => 'decimal:2',

        'purchase_price' => 'decimal:2',
        'total_expense' => 'decimal:2',
        'profit_amount' => 'decimal:2',

        'has_installment' => 'boolean',

        'sale_date' => 'date',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SALE TYPES
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const TYPE_FULL = 'full';
    public const TYPE_PARTIAL = 'partial';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SALE UNITS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const UNIT_PERCENTAGE = 'percentage';
    public const UNIT_KATHA = 'katha';
    public const UNIT_PLOT = 'plot';
    public const UNIT_BIGHA = 'bigha';
    public const UNIT_ACRE = 'acre';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | STATUS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const STATUS_ACTIVE = 'active';
    public const STATUS_COMPLETED = 'completed';
    public const STATUS_CANCELLED = 'cancelled';
    public const STATUS_REFUNDED = 'refunded';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | RELATIONSHIPS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Project::class,
            'project_id'
        );
    }

    public function plot(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            ProjectPlot::class,
            'plot_id'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Customer = Buyer
     */
    public function customer(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Customer::class,
            'customer_id'
        );
    }

    public function items(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            SaleItem::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    public function installments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            SaleInstallment::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    public function payments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            SalePayment::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PAYMENT ADJUSTMENTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | sale_payment_adjustments table does NOT contain sale_id.
    |
    | Therefore there is intentionally no direct Sale ->
    | paymentAdjustments() relationship.
    |
    | Use:
    |
    | $sale->payments()->with('adjustments')->get();
    |
    */

    public function moneyReceipts(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            MoneyReceipt::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    public function cancellations(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            SaleCancellation::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | BOOT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (Sale $sale) {

            if (empty($sale->sale_no)) {
                $sale->sale_no = self::generateSaleNo();
            }

            $sale->calculateAmounts();
        });

        static::updating(function (Sale $sale) {

            $sale->calculateAmounts();
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | SALE NUMBER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function generateSaleNo(): string
    {
        $year = now()->format('Y');

        $last = self::withTrashed()
            ->where(
                'sale_no',
                'like',
                "SALE-{$year}-%"
            )
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        $lastNumber = $last
            ? (int) substr($last->sale_no, -5)
            : 0;

        return sprintf(
            'SALE-%s-%05d',
            $year,
            $lastNumber + 1
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CALCULATE AMOUNTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function calculateAmounts(): void
    {
        $this->sale_price = round(
            (float) $this->quantity *
            (float) $this->unit_rate,
            2
        );

        $this->booking_amount = max(
            0,
            (float) $this->booking_amount
        );

        $this->paid_amount = max(
            0,
            (float) $this->paid_amount
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Paid amount cannot exceed sale price
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $this->paid_amount = min(
            (float) $this->paid_amount,
            (float) $this->sale_price
        );

        $this->due_amount = max(
            0,
            (float) $this->sale_price -
            (float) $this->paid_amount
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | REFRESH PAYMENT STATUS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Payment status is calculated from SalePaymentAdjustment.
    |
    | Counted:
    | - installment
    | - sale_due
    |
    | NOT counted:
    | - advance
    |
    */

    public function refreshPaymentStatus(): void
    {
        if (!$this->exists) {
            return;
        }

        $this->loadMissing([
            'payments.adjustments',
        ]);

        $appliedAmount = 0;

        foreach ($this->payments as $payment) {

            if (
                $payment->status !==
                SalePayment::STATUS_APPROVED
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            foreach ($payment->adjustments as $adjustment) {

                if (
                    in_array(
                        $adjustment->adjustment_type,
                        [
                            SalePaymentAdjustment::TYPE_INSTALLMENT,
                            SalePaymentAdjustment::TYPE_SALE_DUE,
                        ],
                        true
                    )
                ) {
                    $appliedAmount +=
                        (float) $adjustment->amount;
                }
            }
        }

        $salePrice = (float) $this->sale_price;

        $paidAmount = min(
            max(0, $appliedAmount),
            max(0, $salePrice)
        );

        $dueAmount = max(
            0,
            $salePrice - $paidAmount
        );

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Preserve cancelled/refunded status
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        $status = $this->status;

        if (
            !in_array(
                $status,
                [
                    self::STATUS_CANCELLED,
                    self::STATUS_REFUNDED,
                ],
                true
            )
        ) {
            $status = $dueAmount <= 0
                ? self::STATUS_COMPLETED
                : self::STATUS_ACTIVE;
        }

        /*
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        | Save only when changed
        |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
        */

        if (
            (float) $this->paid_amount !== $paidAmount ||
            (float) $this->due_amount !== $dueAmount ||
            $this->status !== $status
        ) {

            $this->paid_amount = $paidAmount;
            $this->due_amount = $dueAmount;
            $this->status = $status;

            $this->saveQuietly();
        }
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACCESSORS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getRemainingDueAttribute(): float
    {
        return max(
            0,
            (float) $this->sale_price -
            (float) $this->paid_amount
        );
    }

    public function getPaymentPercentageAttribute(): float
    {
        if ((float) $this->sale_price <= 0) {
            return 0;
        }

        return round(
            (
                (float) $this->paid_amount /
                (float) $this->sale_price
            ) * 100,
            2
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | PAYMENT HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getTotalApprovedPaymentAttribute(): float
    {
        return (float) $this->payments()
            ->where(
                'status',
                SalePayment::STATUS_APPROVED
            )
            ->sum('amount');
    }

    public function getTotalAdvancePaymentAttribute(): float
    {
        return (float) SalePaymentAdjustment::query()
            ->whereHas('payment', function ($query) {

                $query->where(
                    'sale_id',
                    $this->id
                )->where(
                    'status',
                    SalePayment::STATUS_APPROVED
                );
            })
            ->where(
                'adjustment_type',
                SalePaymentAdjustment::TYPE_ADVANCE
            )
            ->sum('amount');
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | STATUS HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isPaid(): bool
    {
        return (float) $this->due_amount <= 0;
    }

    public function isCancelled(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_CANCELLED;
    }

    public function isRefunded(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_REFUNDED;
    }

    public function isCompleted(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_COMPLETED;
    }

    public function isActive(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_ACTIVE;
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\SaleInstallment.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;

class SaleInstallment extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;
    use SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'sale_installments';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'sale_id',
        'installment_no',
        'due_date',
        'amount',
        'paid_amount',
        'due_amount',
        'paid_date',
        'status',
        'remarks',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'due_date' => 'date',
        'paid_date' => 'date',
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'paid_amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'due_amount' => 'decimal:2',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const STATUS_PENDING = 'pending';
    public const STATUS_PARTIAL = 'partial';
    public const STATUS_PAID = 'paid';
    public const STATUS_OVERDUE = 'overdue';
    public const STATUS_CANCELLED = 'cancelled';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function sale(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Sale::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Model Boot
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function ($installment) {
            $installment->paid_amount = max(
                0,
                (float) ($installment->paid_amount ?? 0)
            );

            $installment->amount = max(
                0,
                (float) ($installment->amount ?? 0)
            );

            $installment->due_amount = max(
                0,
                (float) $installment->amount -
                (float) $installment->paid_amount
            );

            if (empty($installment->status)) {
                $installment->status =
                    $installment->due_amount <= 0
                        ? self::STATUS_PAID
                        : self::STATUS_PENDING;
            }

            if (
                $installment->status === self::STATUS_PAID &&
                empty($installment->paid_date)
            ) {
                $installment->paid_date = now()->toDateString();
            }
        });

        static::updating(function ($installment) {
            $installment->paid_amount = max(
                0,
                (float) ($installment->paid_amount ?? 0)
            );

            $installment->amount = max(
                0,
                (float) ($installment->amount ?? 0)
            );

            $installment->due_amount = max(
                0,
                (float) $installment->amount -
                (float) $installment->paid_amount
            );

            if ($installment->due_amount <= 0) {
                $installment->status = self::STATUS_PAID;

                if (empty($installment->paid_date)) {
                    $installment->paid_date = now()->toDateString();
                }
            } elseif ($installment->paid_amount > 0) {
                $installment->status = self::STATUS_PARTIAL;
            } else {
                $installment->status = self::STATUS_PENDING;
                $installment->paid_date = null;
            }
        });

        static::deleted(function ($installment) {
            $installment->sale?->refreshPaymentStatus();
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Accessors
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function getRemainingAttribute(): float
    {
        return max(
            0,
            (float) $this->amount -
            (float) $this->paid_amount
        );
    }

    public function isPaid(): bool
    {
        return $this->due_amount <= 0;
    }

    public function isPartial(): bool
    {
        return $this->paid_amount > 0 &&
            $this->due_amount > 0;
    }

    public function isPending(): bool
    {
        return $this->paid_amount <= 0 &&
            $this->due_amount > 0;
    }

    public function isOverdue(): bool
    {
        return !$this->isPaid()
            && $this->due_date
            && $this->due_date->isPast();
    }

    public function getStatusLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->status) {
            self::STATUS_PENDING => 'Pending',
            self::STATUS_PARTIAL => 'Partial',
            self::STATUS_PAID => 'Paid',
            self::STATUS_OVERDUE => 'Overdue',
            self::STATUS_CANCELLED => 'Cancelled',
            default => ucfirst(
                str_replace(
                    '_',
                    ' ',
                    (string) $this->status
                )
            ),
        };
    }
}
