--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\SalePayment.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class SalePayment extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory;
    use SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'sale_payments';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',
        'sale_id',
        'customer_id',
        'payment_no',
        'payment_date',
        'amount',
        'payment_method',
        'to_bank_id',
        'bank_name',
        'account_no',
        'cheque_no',
        'cheque_date',
        'transaction_no',
        'status',
        'remarks',
        'created_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'payment_date' => 'date',
        'cheque_date' => 'date',
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'to_bank_id' => 'integer',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Payment Methods
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const METHOD_CASH = 'cash';
    public const METHOD_BANK = 'bank';
    public const METHOD_CHEQUE = 'cheque';
    public const METHOD_MOBILE_BANKING = 'mobile_banking';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Payment Status
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const STATUS_PENDING = 'pending';
    public const STATUS_APPROVED = 'approved';
    public const STATUS_CANCELLED = 'cancelled';
    public const STATUS_REVERSED = 'reversed';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function sale(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Sale::class,
            'sale_id'
        );
    }

    public function customer(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Customer::class,
            'customer_id'
        );
    }

    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'created_by'
        );
    }

    public function bankAccount(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            BankAccount::class,
            'to_bank_id'
        );
    }

    public function adjustments(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            SalePaymentAdjustment::class,
            'payment_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Boot
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (SalePayment $payment) {

            if (empty($payment->payment_no)) {
                $payment->payment_no = self::generatePaymentNo();
            }

            if (empty($payment->payment_date)) {
                $payment->payment_date = now()->toDateString();
            }

            if (empty($payment->status)) {
                $payment->status = self::STATUS_APPROVED;
            }

            if ($payment->payment_method === self::METHOD_CASH) {
                $payment->to_bank_id = null;
            }
        });

        static::created(function (SalePayment $payment) {
            $payment->sale?->refreshPaymentStatus();
        });

        static::updated(function (SalePayment $payment) {

            if (
                $payment->wasChanged([
                    'amount',
                    'payment_date',
                    'payment_method',
                    'to_bank_id',
                    'status',
                ])
            ) {
                $payment->sale?->refreshPaymentStatus();
            }
        });

        static::deleted(function (SalePayment $payment) {
            $payment->sale?->refreshPaymentStatus();
        });
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Payment Number
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public static function generatePaymentNo(): string
    {
        $year = now()->format('Y');

        $lastNo = static::withTrashed()
            ->where('payment_no', 'like', "PAY-{$year}-%")
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->value('payment_no');

        $sequence = $lastNo
            ? ((int) substr($lastNo, -5)) + 1
            : 1;

        return sprintf(
            'PAY-%s-%05d',
            $year,
            $sequence
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isApproved(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_APPROVED;
    }

    public function isCancelled(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_CANCELLED;
    }

    public function isReversed(): bool
    {
        return $this->status === self::STATUS_REVERSED;
    }

    public function isCash(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::METHOD_CASH;
    }

    public function isBank(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::METHOD_BANK;
    }

    public function isCheque(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::METHOD_CHEQUE;
    }

    public function isMobileBanking(): bool
    {
        return $this->payment_method === self::METHOD_MOBILE_BANKING;
    }

    public function getPaymentMethodLabelAttribute(): string
    {
        return match ($this->payment_method) {
            self::METHOD_CASH => 'Cash',
            self::METHOD_BANK => 'Bank',
            self::METHOD_CHEQUE => 'Cheque',
            self::METHOD_MOBILE_BANKING => 'Mobile Banking',
            default => ucfirst(
                str_replace(
                    '_',
                    ' ',
                    (string) $this->payment_method
                )
            ),
        };
    }

    public function getAmountFormattedAttribute(): string
    {
        return '৳ ' . number_format(
            (float) $this->amount,
            2
        );
    }
}

namespace App\Models;

class SalePaymentAdjustment extends BaseModel

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * SECURITY ALERT
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Security monitoring event.
 *
 * Examples:
 * - Too many failed logins
 * - Suspicious login
 * - New device
 * - Session abuse
 * - Repeated verification failure
 * - Account lock
 */
class SecurityAlert extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'security_alerts';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'alert_type',
        'severity',

        'title',
        'description',

        'ip_address',
        'device_id',

        'status',

        'resolved_by',
        'resolved_by_uuid',
        'resolved_at',

        'metadata',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'metadata' =>
                'array',

            'resolved_at' =>
                'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    public function device(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            UserDevice::class,
            'device_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    public function resolver(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'resolved_by',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeOpen(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereIn(
            'status',
            [
                'open',
                'new',
                'unresolved',
            ]
        );
    }

    public function scopeResolved(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'resolved'
        );
    }

    public function scopeCritical(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'severity',
            'critical'
        );
    }

    public function scopeHigh(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'severity',
            'high'
        );
    }

    public function scopeSeverity(
        Builder $query,
        string $severity
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'severity',
            strtolower($severity)
        );
    }

    public function scopeType(
        Builder $query,
        string $type
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'alert_type',
            $type
        );
    }

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    public function scopeRecent(
        Builder $query,
        int $days = 30
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'created_at',
            '>=',
            now()->subDays(max(1, $days))
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isResolved(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'resolved';
    }

    public function isOpen(): bool
    {
        return !$this->isResolved();
    }

    public function isCritical(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->severity
        ) === 'critical';
    }

    public function isHigh(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->severity
        ) === 'high';
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Resolve
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function resolve(
        ?Admin $admin = null,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->status = 'resolved';
        $this->resolved_at =
            $this->resolved_at ?? now();

        if ($admin) {
            $this->resolved_by =
                $admin->getKey();

            $this->resolved_by_uuid =
                $admin->record_uuid
                ?? $admin->uuid
                ?? null;
        }

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function reopen(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->status = 'open';
        $this->resolved_at = null;
        $this->resolved_by = null;
        $this->resolved_by_uuid = null;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }
}

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * SECURITY VERIFICATION
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Verification record for sensitive actions.
 *
 * Examples:
 * - Approve deposit
 * - Change role
 * - Revoke session
 * - Disable 2FA
 * - Change security settings
 */
class SecurityVerification extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'security_verifications';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'action',
        'module',

        'subject_type',
        'subject_id',
        'subject_uuid',

        'verification_method',

        'verified_at',

        'ip_address',
        'device_id',

        'status',
    ];

    {

            'verified_at' =>
                'datetime',
        ];

        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'subject',
            'subject_type',
            'subject_id'
        );
    }

    public function device(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            UserDevice::class,
            'device_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeVerified(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->whereNotNull('verified_at')
            ->where(
                'status',
                'verified'
            );
    }

    public function scopeFailed(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'failed'
        );
    }

    public function scopePending(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'pending'
        );
    }

    public function scopeForAction(
        Builder $query,
        string $action
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'action',
            $action
        );
    }

    public function scopeForModule(
        Builder $query,
        string $module
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'module',
            $module
        );
    }

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isVerified(): bool
    {
        return $this->verified_at !== null
            && strtolower(
                (string) $this->status
            ) === 'verified';
    }

    public function isFailed(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'failed';
    }

    public function isPending(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'pending';
    }

    public function markVerified(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->status = 'verified';
        $this->verified_at =
            $this->verified_at ?? now();

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function markFailed(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->status = 'failed';

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;

class Setting extends Model
{
    use HasFactory;

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | TABLE CONFIGURATION
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $table = 'settings';

    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    public $incrementing = true;

    protected $keyType = 'int';


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MASS ASSIGNMENT
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $fillable = [
        'company_name',
        'company_phone',
        'company_email',
        'company_address',
        'logo',
        'favicon',
        'whatsapp_number',
        'sms_sender_id',
        'currency',
        'timezone',
        'opening_month',
        'opening_year',
        'opening_date',
    ];


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CASTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $casts = [
        'created_at'  => 'datetime',
        'updated_at'  => 'datetime',
        'opening_date' => 'date',
        'opening_year' => 'integer',
    ];


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CONSTANTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public const CURRENCY_BDT = 'BDT';

    public const CURRENCY_USD = 'USD';

    public const CURRENCY_INR = 'INR';


    public const TIMEZONE_DHAKA = 'Asia/Dhaka';

    public const TIMEZONE_DUBAI = 'Asia/Dubai';

    public const TIMEZONE_LONDON = 'Europe/London';

    public const TIMEZONE_NEW_YORK = 'America/New_York';


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CACHE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected const CACHE_KEY = 'app_settings';

    protected const CACHE_SECONDS = 60;


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | GET SETTINGS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Return the application's single settings record.
     *
     * If no record exists, a default record is created.
     */
    public static function getSettings(): self
    {
        return Cache::remember(
            self::CACHE_KEY,
            self::CACHE_SECONDS,
            function () {
                return self::query()->first()
                    ?? self::createDefault();
            }
        );
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CREATE DEFAULT SETTINGS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Create default application settings.
     */
    public static function createDefault(): self
    {
        return self::query()->create([
            'company_name'    => 'Taqwa Properties BD',
            'company_phone'   => '+880 1712-263593',
            'company_email'   => 'info@taqwaproperties.com',
            'company_address' => 'Dhaka, Bangladesh',
            'currency'        => self::CURRENCY_BDT,
            'timezone'        => self::TIMEZONE_DHAKA,
            'opening_month'   => 'January',
            'opening_year'    => 2026,
            'opening_date'    => '2026-01-01',
        ]);
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | ACCESSORS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Full logo URL.
     */
    protected function logoUrl(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                if (!empty($this->logo)) {
                    return asset('storage/' . ltrim(
                        $this->logo,
                        '/'
                    ));
                }

                return asset('images/default-logo.png');
            }
        );
    }


    /**
     * Full favicon URL.
     */
    protected function faviconUrl(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                if (!empty($this->favicon)) {
                    return asset('storage/' . ltrim(
                        $this->favicon,
                        '/'
                    ));
                }

                return asset('favicon.ico');
            }
        );
    }


    /**
     * Currency symbol.
     */
    protected function currencySymbol(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {

                return match ($this->currency) {

                    self::CURRENCY_USD =>
                        '$',

                    self::CURRENCY_INR =>
                        '₹',

                    self::CURRENCY_BDT =>
                        '৳',

                    default =>
                        '৳',
                };
            }
        );
    }


    /**
     * Formatted company address.
     */
    protected function formattedAddress(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () =>
                nl2br(
                    e(
                        $this->company_address ?? ''
                    )
                )
        );
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | OPENING DATE HELPERS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Get opening date.
     */
    public static function getOpeningDate(): string
    {
        $settings = self::getSettings();

        if ($settings->opening_date) {
            return $settings->opening_date
                ->format('Y-m-d');
        }

        return '2026-01-01';
    }


    /**
     * Get opening month.
     */
    public static function getOpeningMonth(): string
    {
        return self::get(
            'opening_month',
            'January'
        );
    }


    /**
     * Get opening year.
     */
    public static function getOpeningYear(): string
    {
        return (string) self::get(
            'opening_year',
            2026
        );
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | GENERIC GETTER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Get a setting value.
     */
    public static function get(
        string $key,
        mixed $default = null
    ): mixed {

        $settings = self::getSettings();

        $value = $settings->getAttribute($key);

        return $value ?? $default;
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | GENERIC SETTER
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Update application settings.
     */
    public static function set(
        array $data
    ): bool {

        $settings = self::query()->first();

        if (!$settings) {
            $settings = self::createDefault();
        }

        $updated = $settings->update($data);

        self::clearCache();

        return $updated;
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | CLEAR CACHE
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Clear cached settings.
     */
    public static function clearCache(): void
    {
        Cache::forget(self::CACHE_KEY);
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | MODEL EVENTS
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        static::saved(
            fn () => self::clearCache()
        );

        static::deleted(
            fn () => self::clearCache()
        );
    }


    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | EMPTY CHECK
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Determine whether settings table is empty.
     */
    public static function isEmpty(): bool
    {
        return self::query()->count() === 0;
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\Share.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphMany;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class Share extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid, HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'shares';

    protected $fillable = [
        'share_no',
        'member_id',
        'project_id',
        'quantity',
        'amount_per_share',
        'value',
        'total_amount',
        'purchase_date',
        'status',
        'remarks',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'quantity' => 'integer',
        'amount_per_share' => 'decimal:2',
        'value' => 'decimal:2',
        'total_amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'purchase_date' => 'date',
        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        'deleted_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    public const STATUS_ACTIVE = 'active';
    public const STATUS_TRANSFERRED = 'transferred';
    public const STATUS_REFUNDED = 'refunded';

    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class);
    }

    public function project(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Project::class);
    }

    public function transactions(): MorphMany
    {
        return $this->morphMany(Transaction::class, 'transactionable');
    }

    public function resignations(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Resignation::class);
    }

    public function calculateTotalAmount(): float
    {
        return (float) $this->quantity
            * (float) $this->amount_per_share;
    }

    public static function generateShareNo(): string
    {
        $latest = self::withTrashed()
            ->orderByDesc('id')
            ->first();

        $nextSequence = $latest ? $latest->id + 1 : 1;

        return 'SHR-' . str_pad(
            (string) $nextSequence,
            6,
            '0',
            STR_PAD_LEFT
        );
    }

    protected static function boot(): void
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function (Share $share) {
            if (empty($share->share_no)) {
                $share->share_no = self::generateShareNo();
            }

            if (empty($share->status)) {
                $share->status = self::STATUS_ACTIVE;
            }

            $share->total_amount = $share->calculateTotalAmount();
        });

        static::updating(function (Share $share) {
            if ($share->isDirty(['quantity', 'amount_per_share'])) {
                $share->total_amount = $share->calculateTotalAmount();
            }
        });
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\HasRecordUuid;
use App\Models\BaseModel;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;

class Transaction extends BaseModel
{
    use HasRecordUuid;
    use HasFactory, SoftDeletes;

    protected $table = 'transactions';
    protected $primaryKey = 'id';

    protected $fillable = [
        'transaction_no',
        'transactionable_type',
        'transactionable_id',
        'member_id',
        'type',
        'amount',
        'payment_method',
        'transaction_date',
        'reference_no',
        'remarks',
        'created_by',
    ];

    protected $casts = [
        'amount' => 'decimal:2',
        'transaction_date' => 'date',
        'created_at' => 'datetime',
        'updated_at' => 'datetime',
        'deleted_at' => 'datetime',
    ];

    // ============================================================
    // CONSTANTS
    // ============================================================
    public const TYPE_CREDIT = 'credit';
    public const TYPE_DEBIT = 'debit';

    public const METHOD_CASH = 'cash';
    public const METHOD_BANK = 'bank';
    public const METHOD_MOBILE = 'mobile';

    // ============================================================
    // RELATIONSHIPS
    // ============================================================
    public function transactionable(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo();
    }

    public function member(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Member::class);
    }

    public function creator(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Admin::class, 'created_by');
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SCOPES
    // ============================================================
    public function scopeCredits($query)
    {
        return $query->where('type', self::TYPE_CREDIT);
    }

    public function scopeDebits($query)
    {
        return $query->where('type', self::TYPE_DEBIT);
    }

    public function scopeByDateRange($query, $from, $to)
    {
        return $query->whereBetween('transaction_date', [$from, $to]);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // BUSINESS LOGIC
    // ============================================================
    public static function generateTransactionNo(): string
    {
        $latest = self::withTrashed()->orderBy('id', 'desc')->first();
        $nextSequence = $latest ? ($latest->id + 1) : 1;
        return 'TRX-' . date('Ymd') . '-' . str_pad($nextSequence, 6, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
    }

    protected static function boot()
    {
        parent::boot();

        static::creating(function ($transaction) {
            if (empty($transaction->transaction_no)) {
                $transaction->transaction_no = self::generateTransactionNo();
            }
            if (empty($transaction->transaction_date)) {
                $transaction->transaction_date = now();
            }
            if (empty($transaction->created_by)) {
                $transaction->created_by = auth('admin')->id();
            }
        });
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ACCESSORS
    // ============================================================
    protected function formattedAmount(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: fn () => 'à§³ ' . number_format($this->amount, 2)
        );
    }

    protected function typeBadge(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                $badges = [
                    self::TYPE_CREDIT => 'badge-success',
                    self::TYPE_DEBIT  => 'badge-danger',
                ];
                return $badges[$this->type] ?? 'badge-secondary';
            }
        );
    }

    protected function typeLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                $labels = [
                    self::TYPE_CREDIT => 'âœ… Credit',
                    self::TYPE_DEBIT  => 'âŒ Debit',
                ];
                return $labels[$this->type] ?? ucfirst($this->type);
            }
        );
    }

    protected function paymentMethodLabel(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::make(
            get: function () {
                $labels = [
                    self::METHOD_CASH   => 'ðŸ’µ Cash',
                    self::METHOD_BANK   => 'ðŸ¦ Bank',
                    self::METHOD_MOBILE => 'ðŸ“± Mobile',
                ];
                return $labels[$this->payment_method] ?? ucfirst($this->payment_method);
            }
        );
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * TWO FACTOR AUTHENTICATION
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Stores the 2FA/TOTP configuration for an Admin or Member.
 *
 * Security rules:
 * - Secret must never be exposed through normal serialization.
 * - Secret should be encrypted at application level.
 * - Recovery codes are stored separately.
 * - Disabled 2FA keeps its historical timestamps.
 */
class TwoFactorAuthentication extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'two_factor_authentications';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'method',
        'secret',

        'enabled',
        'enabled_at',
        'last_verified_at',
        'disabled_at',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'secret',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            /*
             * IMPORTANT:
             * Use this only after confirming existing DB secrets are already
             * Laravel-encrypted values.
             */
            'secret' => 'encrypted',

            'enabled' =>
                'boolean',

            'enabled_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'last_verified_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'disabled_at' =>
                'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeEnabled(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('enabled', true)
            ->whereNull('disabled_at');
    }

    public function scopeDisabled(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(function ($q) {
            $q->where('enabled', false)
              ->orWhereNotNull('disabled_at');
        });
    }

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    public function scopeForMethod(
        Builder $query,
        string $method
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'method',
            $method
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isEnabled(): bool
    {
        return (bool) $this->enabled
            && $this->disabled_at === null;
    }

    public function isDisabled(): bool
    {
        return !$this->isEnabled();
    }

    public function hasBeenVerified(): bool
    {
        return $this->last_verified_at !== null;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Security Actions
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function enable(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->enabled = true;
        $this->enabled_at = $this->enabled_at ?? now();
        $this->disabled_at = null;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function disable(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->enabled = false;
        $this->disabled_at = now();

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function markVerified(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->last_verified_at = now();

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function forgetSecret(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->secret = null;
        $this->enabled = false;
        $this->disabled_at = now();

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * TWO FACTOR RECOVERY CODE
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * One-time recovery code for 2FA.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - Never expose unused codes through API/listing.
 * - Prefer storing a one-way hash.
 * - Used codes cannot be reused.
 */
class TwoFactorRecoveryCode extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'two_factor_recovery_codes';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'code',
        'used_at',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'code',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'used_at' => 'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeUnused(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNull(
            'used_at'
        );
    }

    public function scopeUsed(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNotNull(
            'used_at'
        );
    }

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isUsed(): bool
    {
        return $this->used_at !== null;
    }

    public function isUnused(): bool
    {
        return !$this->isUsed();
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Security
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function markUsed(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        if ($this->used_at !== null) {
            return true;
        }

        $this->used_at = now();

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * USER DEVICE
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Application-level browser/device registry.
 *
 * device_fingerprint must be an application-generated hash.
 * Never store raw hardware identifiers unnecessarily.
 */
class UserDevice extends BaseModel
{
    protected $table = 'user_devices';

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'device_fingerprint',

        'device_name',
        'device_type',

        'operating_system',
        'browser',

        'first_seen_at',
        'last_seen_at',

        'last_ip',

        'is_trusted',
        'status',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'device_fingerprint',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'first_seen_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'last_seen_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'is_trusted' =>
                'boolean',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    public function sessions()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(
            UserSession::class,
            'device_id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function scopeTrusted(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'is_trusted',
            true
        );
    }

    public function scopeUntrusted(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'is_trusted',
            false
        );
    }

    public function scopeActive(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'status',
            'active'
        );
    }

    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where('user_type', $userType)
            ->where('user_id', $userId);
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    public function isTrusted(): bool
    {
        return (bool) $this->is_trusted;
    }

    public function isActive(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === 'active';
    }

    public function trust(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->is_trusted = true;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function untrust(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->is_trusted = false;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function deactivate(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->status = 'inactive';

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function activate(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->status = 'active';

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    public function touchActivity(
        ?string $ip = null,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->last_seen_at = now();

        if ($ip !== null) {
            $this->last_ip = $ip;
        }

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }
}

--- C:\xampp\htdocs\taqwa\app\Models\UserSession.php ---
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\MorphTo;

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * USER SESSION
 * ============================================================================
 *
 * Application-level session monitoring.
 *
 * Different from Laravel's framework `sessions` table.
 *
 * Tracks:
 * - Login
 * - Activity
 * - Logout
 * - Revocation
 * - Device
 * - IP
 */
class UserSession extends BaseModel
{
    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Table
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $table = 'user_sessions';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Status Constants
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    |
    | Keep all application-level session statuses centralized.
    | These values should match the database status values.
    |
    */

    public const STATUS_ACTIVE = 'active';

    public const STATUS_LOGGED_OUT = 'logged_out';

    public const STATUS_EXPIRED = 'expired';

    public const STATUS_REVOKED = 'revoked';

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Mass Assignment
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $fillable = [
        'record_uuid',

        'user_type',
        'user_id',
        'user_uuid',

        'session_id',

        'device_id',

        'ip_address',
        'user_agent',

        'login_at',
        'last_activity_at',
        'logout_at',

        'status',

        'revoked_by',
        'revoked_by_uuid',
        'revoked_at',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Hidden Attributes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected $hidden = [
        'session_id',
    ];

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Casts
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'login_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'last_activity_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'logout_at' =>
                'datetime',

            'revoked_at' =>
                'datetime',
        ];
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Relationships
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Polymorphic application user.
     */
    public function user(): MorphTo
    {
        return $this->morphTo(
            'user',
            'user_type',
            'user_id'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Admin who revoked the session.
     */
    public function revokedBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            Admin::class,
            'revoked_by',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Device associated with this session.
     */
    public function device(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(
            UserDevice::class,
            'device_id',
            'id'
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Scopes
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Active sessions only.
     */
    public function scopeActive(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'status',
                self::STATUS_ACTIVE
            )
            ->whereNull(
                'logout_at'
            )
            ->whereNull(
                'revoked_at'
            );
    }

    /**
     * Logged-out sessions.
     */
    public function scopeLoggedOut(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNotNull(
            'logout_at'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Revoked sessions.
     */
    public function scopeRevoked(
        Builder $query
    ): Builder {
        return $query->whereNotNull(
            'revoked_at'
        );
    }

    /**
     * Sessions belonging to a specific user.
     */
    public function scopeForUser(
        Builder $query,
        string $userType,
        int $userId
    ): Builder {
        return $query
            ->where(
                'user_type',
                $userType
            )
            ->where(
                'user_id',
                $userId
            );
    }

    /**
     * Sessions belonging to a specific device.
     */
    public function scopeForDevice(
        Builder $query,
        int $deviceId
    ): Builder {
        return $query->where(
            'device_id',
            $deviceId
        );
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | State Helpers
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Determine whether this session is currently active.
     */
    public function isActive(): bool
    {
        return strtolower(
            (string) $this->status
        ) === self::STATUS_ACTIVE
            && $this->logout_at === null
            && $this->revoked_at === null;
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether this session has been logged out.
     */
    public function isLoggedOut(): bool
    {
        return $this->logout_at !== null;
    }

    /**
     * Determine whether this session has been revoked.
     */
    public function isRevoked(): bool
    {
        return $this->revoked_at !== null;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Activity
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Update the latest activity timestamp.
     */
    public function markActivity(
        ?string $ip = null,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->last_activity_at = now();

        if ($ip !== null) {
            $this->ip_address = $ip;
        }

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Logout
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Mark this session as logged out.
     */
    public function logout(
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->logout_at =
            $this->logout_at ?? now();

        $this->status =
            self::STATUS_LOGGED_OUT;

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Revoke
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Revoke this session.
     */
    public function revoke(
        ?Admin $admin = null,
        bool $save = true
    ): bool {
        $this->revoked_at =
            $this->revoked_at ?? now();

        $this->status =
            self::STATUS_REVOKED;

        if ($admin) {
            $this->revoked_by =
                $admin->getKey();

            $this->revoked_by_uuid =
                $admin->record_uuid
                ?? $admin->uuid
                ?? null;
        }

        if ($save) {
            return $this->save();
        }

        return true;
    }

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    | Duration
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    */

    /**
     * Calculate session duration in seconds.
     */
    public function durationInSeconds(): int
    {
        if (!$this->login_at) {
            return 0;
        }

        $end =
            $this->logout_at
            ?? $this->revoked_at
            ?? now();

        return max(
            0,
            (int) $this->login_at->diffInSeconds($end)
        );
    }

    /**
     * Calculate idle time in seconds.
     */
    public function idleSeconds(): int
    {
        if (!$this->last_activity_at) {
            return 0;
        }

        return max(
            0,
            (int) $this->last_activity_at->diffInSeconds(now())
        );
    }
}
